# Roblox lessons log (grows with every project)

Every Roblox agent and session that builds or fixes Roblox code **reads this before starting** and **adds to it after finishing**.
One line per lesson, newest at top, grouped by topic. Format: `- YYYY-MM-DD [project] lesson — fix/pattern (file or template)`.
When a lesson repeats 2–3 times or is clearly general, promote it into `SKILL.md` (or a template) and delete the lines here.
Promoted 2026-10-28: ~90 lessons from 2026-10-27 → `roblox-dev/SKILL.md` §2 (nonce remotes, jump cheats, busy locks, auto loops) / §3 (regroup, resolvers, pool test) / §5 (Atomic streaming) / §7 (joints, pivots, tests, captures, grey textures, sync, team workflow); UI → `ui.md`; beams/embers/effect skins → `roblox-vfx` §6; imports/shading maps/procedural buildings/installers → `roblox-3d-pipeline`; island outlines/walkable tops/checks → `floating-islands.md`; dusk skies → `maps.md`; replays/camera fit → `game-feel.md`; anchored-rig drift → `pet-movement.md`; prompts → `chatgpt-prompts.md`; template `RemoteGuard` v2. ~150 older lines (10-23..10-25) were stale re-adds of already-promoted lessons and were dropped. Earlier promotions: see git history of this file.

## Workflow (what made building faster)
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Vertical sky climbs: generate + validate offline (climb_gen.py): h = JP^2/(2g) per jump, chained air jumps at each apex, edge gap <= 0.62 x reach at the required level, BFS at level-1 from the deck AND tall building tops must stall at the intro steps, plan-separation >= r1+r2+2.5 within 30 studs of height (head clearance + no stacking), nothing under the next island's future footprint. Pick checkpoints by "next target height reached" (an abs(y-h)<5 test skipped 300 and left a 108-stud gap).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Open Cloud GLB upload + InsertService works (approved instantly, 29 named MeshParts, vertex colors kept) but Studio turns the glTF model 180 deg about Y (front ends up at +Z): rebuild from the import with `CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi, 0) * part.CFrame` and assert each piece lands on its Blender layout centre. Pieces that must spin (gears) export axis-aligned at the origin; set CFrame.fromMatrix from a layout json + attribute Axis.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] MCP screen_capture timed out all session again; a DPI-aware PowerShell window grab (SetProcessDPIAware + GetWindowRect + CopyFromScreen) of the maximized Studio works. With the device emulator zoomed only the top-left part of the viewport is on screen: aim the camera with WorldToViewportPoint so the subject lands in the visible band, then crop.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Base bodies with cut-outs: model the cavity with an exact boolean of a boxy superellipsoid (p~4) centred INSIDE the corner (flat floor, steep walls); a bowl centred outside the corner only shaves a shallow scoop. Sockets that land over the cavity get mount plates with ShowWhenSlot so the bare body keeps the full bite (PetBuilder must support ShowWhenSlot).
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] PowerShell gotchas for Luau files: variables are case-insensitive ($s overwrites $S); `Set-Content -Encoding utf8` adds a BOM that Luau loadstring rejects; PS 5.1 Get-Content reads BOM-less UTF-8 as ANSI → use [IO.File] with UTF8Encoding($false). Parallel agents: one http.server port each.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] `require` of a client controller from MCP execute_luau gives a fresh module copy with empty state — to inspect client data, listen to the sync remote yourself.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] Syncing scripts into Studio: serve the scripts folder with `python -m http.server --bind 127.0.0.1`, GetAsync with HttpEnabled on only during the fetch, normalize `

` → `
`, `loadstring` to compile-check before setting `.Source`. Patch scripts that find/replace anchors break on CRLF vs LF — prefer syncing the whole file.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] An unsaved Studio place can close mid-job (lost hub built only via execute_luau). Save new places to a file/Roblox before building; agents check `list_roblox_studios` before long jobs and keep code on disk + one install script so work survives a Studio drop.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] Customuse CR1 image-to-3D = 100 credits per run, geometry only (no texture). Free texture: project the source picture onto the mesh from the matching view. Put 2–3 small parts spaced apart in ONE picture to get several meshes per run, then split by loose parts in Blender.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] Base64 JPEG uploads to Customuse got corrupted (long white runs); a WebP exported from Blender (view transform Standard) uploaded byte-exact — md5-check uploads. Image-to-3D keeps the picture's camera angle: rotate + tilt parts to the mount before placing.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] MCP execute_luau runs in its own VM: `_G` set by game scripts is invisible to it. Drive dev tools through a Studio-only DevCommand remote from the Client datamodel; call RemoteFunctions with InvokeServer.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] A whole Model (the hub island) got moved ~11 studs in Studio while building (likely a stray editor drag/select) and covered the plaza; screenshots showed an empty floor. Lock finished map parts (`Locked=true`) and sanity-check a few anchor positions before trusting a capture.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] `screen_capture` draws BillboardGuis a few studs off to the side of their adornee at wide FOV; judge label placement from a close shot, not the spawn capture.
## Workflow (what made building faster)
- 2026-10-24 MCP execute_luau can't read or set Workspace streaming properties (StreamingTargetRadius/MinRadius/IntegrityMode -> "not a valid member", though ReflectionService lists them): set them by hand in the Properties panel and say so in the report.
- 2026-10-24 Colored windows on recolored store meshes in one step: a Texture (white windows on transparent, procedural PNG) on Front/Left/Right of the MeshPart, Color3 = accent, Transparency ~0.25, StudsPerTile ~ height/3. Big/bright window cells read as stripes up close: keep cells small with ~35% unlit.
- 2026-10-24 SurfaceGui on the Top face of a tilted console renders text sideways; use a thin plate rotated so its Front face looks at the player. Picture signs (cut-out PNG): ImageLabel in a SurfaceGui (LightInfluence 0) on a transparent part + a dark backing plate = unlit neon look without extra Neon parts.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] Many agents sharing one Studio: Play was busy 20+ min and Edit calls failed. Use a lock file in the project (`_studio_play.lock`: "<role> <unix-time>"): check it before Play, hold ≤ 3 min, stop Play, delete it. Lead brokers the slot.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] Several agents editing one Studio place: never full-sync scripts — sync an allow-list of your own files and compare Studio line counts with your pre-edit copies before writing.
- 2026-10-23 Bash heredocs holding Python with triple-quoted strings sometimes fail ("unexpected EOF") in this tool — write the script to a scratch file and run it.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] Big map builds: one `.lua` per building + shared `lib.lua`, served by `python -m http.server`, run in Studio via loadstring (lib prepended). Each chunk clears + rebuilds itself → fixes are one rerun and the work survives a Studio drop.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] City boundaries: visible walls 16–30 studs (taller than nearby buildings reads as a fence) + invisible walls (CanQuery off) to Y 1000 + a server bounds check that returns anyone outside to spawn. Brightness 3: light floor tints read white — use ~(80,100,200); lime glow blows out — use ~(45,180,40); gold glow reads lemon.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] Building parts to the bible's size caps made the pet much tamer than the approved concept (small horns/wings). The approved picture beats the caps: measure part sizes from the concept relative to the body and raise the caps if needed.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] gpt-image with -Ref saved the reference instead of the result: ChatGPT re-renders the attachment as `alt="User attachment"` outside any message-role element. Fixed in `assets/roblox-app/gpt-image.mjs` (skip user attachments, prefer `data-testid*="generated-image"`). Always hash-check queue outputs; a result in < 25 s is suspicious.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] ChatGPT ignores "white background" on dark/glowing pets (adds a dark glow haze) → fix with a -Ref edit asking for white; that edit can return a transparent PNG → flatten onto white (System.Drawing, no PIL here).
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] A -Ref "edit this exact picture" keeps camera angle and proportions; turning to three-quarter or forcing 1:1:1 in an edit is ignored → put view + proportions in the FIRST prompt.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] Hub sightlines: before placing tall side props, compute the spawn->portal ray x at that z (x = portalX * (z - spawnZ)/(portalZ - spawnZ)) and keep props outside it; moved Assembly Lab + leaderboards out of the portal view this way. Neon under Brightness 3: use deeper colors (violet (100,50,235), gold (235,150,20)); lighter violet renders pink and gold renders lemon yellow.
- 2026-10-23 [play-session study] watch-play.ps1 left no `sheets/` and no notes text; built sheets with `roblox-game-study/scripts/make-sheets.ps1`. Like/dislike marks can be misclicks — wait for/accept lyopz's correction before writing dislikes into taste.md.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] One-function UI builders hit Luau's 200-locals limit; wrap each panel section in do ... end and compile-check with loadstring(src) in Edit before writing any Source.
- 2026-10-23 MCP upload_image needs an http URL (serve the folder with python -m http.server); same file returns the same id. PreloadAsync can report Failure for images that render; verify with a test ImageLabel + screenshot.
- 2026-10-23 Device simulator: SetOrientationAsync errors until SetDeviceAsync picked a mobile device. Short landscape phones (~338 px) need a scrolling column (ScrollingFrame, fixed-offset CanvasSize on phones, scale 1 elsewhere).
- 2026-10-23 Glow outline without beta features: a transparent frame a bit larger than the button with only a UIStroke draws outside the edge; brighten the stroke on hover.
- 2026-10-23 With many agents sharing Play: lock file + set up the whole test in one Client execute_luau call (give currency, teleport, fire remotes), then screenshot; a full UI check fits in <3 min.
- 2026-10-23 Store meshes come textured and MeshPart.Color doesn't tint the texture: to restyle, remove TextureID and recolour the whole mesh; window insets/ledges still read as cartoon "clay" buildings at one part each.
- 2026-10-23 Check store pack mesh names before using: PolygonCity_* (or other Synty names) = reupload of a paid pack, doubtful licence -> skip.
- 2026-10-23 Two background python -m http.server jobs on one port fail silently (404); give each agent its own port.
- 2026-10-23 gpt-image as a one-at-a-time queue with an MD5 check against the whole project (stop on duplicate) made 47 pictures, 0 duplicates, 26-54 s each. Same STYLE + FRAME text on every prompt gives a matching set without -Ref.
- 2026-10-23 powershell -File can't take arrays (-Names a,b,c arrives as one string): pass a comma string and split inside.
- 2026-10-23 ChatGPT adds painted rust/grunge to scrap or metal subjects even when told "clean": add "smooth clean painted surfaces (no rust, grunge or scratch textures)". "Show the pair as one object, spread from an empty middle gap" gives symmetric ear/wing pairs.
- 2026-10-23 Device simulator: named presets like "Galaxy A06" can stop being accepted by SetDeviceAsync; use CreateDeviceAsync({Name, Width=800, Height=360, PixelDensity=96}) and set the returned id (works from the Client datamodel in Play).
- 2026-10-23 Never name a child "Name" (frame.Name returns the property); use "Label".
- 2026-10-24 Automatic import: Open Cloud Assets API (`roblox-upload.ps1`) uploads .glb/.fbx as a Model package, then MCP `insert_asset` / InsertService:LoadAsset brings it in; destroy the PackageLink so edits stay local. Untested on the PC yet.
- 2026-10-23 No MCP tool uploads meshes. AssetService:CreateEditableMesh + CreateMeshPartAsync(Content.fromObject(em)) works from execute_luau in Edit for an instant preview (vertex colors, UVs, TextureID) but is NOT saved with the place. Persistent MeshIds need CreateAssetAsync, which only runs in a local plugin; Studio doesn't hot-reload local plugins (restart once) - else File > Import 3D.
- 2026-10-23 To run a big generated Luau script via MCP: serve it on 127.0.0.1 with python -m http.server, HttpGet it (HttpEnabled on only for the fetch), then loadstring(src)() instead of pasting 40 KB.
- 2026-10-23 Write Python patch scripts to files (inline python -c quoting breaks in PowerShell); clamp before x ** fractional (negative base -> complex).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Subtle "shiny" twinkle that works on any mesh: Box emitter per big piece; blocks use ShapeStyle Surface, MeshParts use Volume + ZOffset = half the thinnest dimension (stars sit on the mesh, not the box corners). Size = 0.17 x the piece's middle dimension; 0.13 was invisible at 8 studs. Cap per pet by summing an IVBaseRate attribute and scaling Rate.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Particles don't render in ViewportFrames: fake them with a 2D ImageLabel pool over the frame, positions from projecting random points on camera-facing box faces (cam.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace, vertical FOV, aspect = AbsoluteSize.X/Y). You can't store Lua fields on Instances; keep per-overlay data in a weak-keyed table.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Shiny "hue + 180" breaks white colors: a neutral tint at sat 0.45 turned Titanium White pink. Light neutrals need a pearl tint (sat <= 0.09). Edit-mode execute_luau caches require(): clone the ModuleScript and require the clone to test fresh edits.
- 2026-10-24 A RemoteEvent can become a RemoteFunction without breaking the client if the client calls through one request helper that checks the remote's ClassName.
- 2026-10-24 Tests that teleport the player must stay inside the map's bounds check (a city-bounds script snapped the test player to spawn = false FAIL). Temporary tagged parts made during Play are a clean way to test stations that aren't placed yet.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Recolorable meshes: Studio MULTIPLIES MeshPart.Color with mesh vertex colors (EditableMesh verified; grey 0.6 reads much darker, ~linear). So for palette-recolor bake GREYSCALE shading (0.75-1.0) into vertex colors, one MeshPart per palette region, Color = role color + PaletteRole attribute. Colored vertex colors x palette = muddy.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Parts built on a flat-faced body sink into a "sculpted" body: keep the faces parts sit on (front/back/top) flat at the old plane; only bulge faces nothing mounts on. Head bands/crowns must follow the rounded top's bevel (top_z(x) = 0.6+sqrt(0.36-(|x|-0.6)^2) for a 2.4 cube, r 0.6) or they read as a bar floating past the edges.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Dimmed Neon glow (x0.55) on a same-hue body vanishes (orange eyes on crimson). Pick body palettes that contrast the parts' glow, or the face disappears.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Toasts living inside the HUD frame render UNDER open menus (Menus is a later sibling) -> deny/far toasts were invisible; reparent the toast list to the UI root with a higher ZIndex.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] `Get-Date -UFormat %s` in PowerShell 5.1 returns LOCAL time as epoch (hours off from bash `date +%s`): lock-file timestamps must be read with the same clock that wrote them.
- 2026-10-24 Device simulator from MCP: the service is `StudioDeviceSimulatorService` (GetService("StudioDeviceEmulatorService") returns nil); CreateDeviceAsync + SetDeviceAsync from the Client DM.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Resizing jump-gated sky islands: search the island centres offline (small Python copy of the route generator) with constraints = inside the walls, consecutive rim gaps <= cloud arc length, no cloud under any deck within jump reach; then build. Plan-separated islands (centre distance > R1+R2) removed every stacking/bonk case, and the Studio audit + Play hop test passed first try.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Animated stepping clouds: keep the invisible walk disc anchored and still; bob only a "Visual" child model (0.3 stud) client-side. Standing stays 100% reliable, the motion still reads.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Locked/unlocked world props driven by one attribute: store LockedColor/UnlockedColor (+Transparency, LockOnly, LockedText/UnlockedText) as attributes on each part and ship a tiny client script that applies them on attribute change — gameplay code only flips `Unlocked`.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Phone-landscape HUD: the bottom-left counter stack collides with the DynamicThumbstick (ThumbstickStart ~x29-103, y188-262 at 799x339). Check `PlayerGui.TouchGui` AbsolutePosition vs every left-bottom HUD element; keep counters above ~y180 or right of x~110 in compact mode.
- (reviewer) `GetPartBoundsInRadius` skips CanQuery=false decor; when hunting a "too bright" part, loop the model's descendants by distance instead. One-sided banner plates (Size Z 0.001) show as a full bright sheet from behind — give banners a dark back or a frame-only border.
- (reviewer) Test station prompts from MCP with `prompt:InputHoldBegin()/InputHoldEnd()` on the Client after `character_navigation` to the instance; it fires the real PromptService path (OpenUI arg forwarding included).

- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Phone-landscape counters vs thumbstick: read `PlayerGui.TouchGui` > `ThumbstickStart` AbsolutePosition/Size in the rescale and place the stack right of it ((edge+10 - ScreenGui inset)/UIScale); TouchGui appears late, so re-run the layout on PlayerGui.ChildAdded("TouchGui"). A tight station panel on a 339 px phone fits as ONE horizontal row (UIGridLayout FillDirection Vertical + FillDirectionMaxCells 1, ScrollingDirection X, AutomaticCanvasSize X) with a one-line header.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Two currency icons from one prompt series both got the rebirth-arrow emblem (the prompt asked for it) and read as the same icon at 44 px. Give each currency a different SILHOUETTE (gold-bar stack vs round coin/swirl), not just a different color; one gpt-image + System.Drawing flood-fill cutout (white -> alpha from the edges, crop, 512 px) fixed it in ~1 min.
- 2026-10-24 Client-only visual test for fields the server doesn't send yet: a Debug BindableFunction command in the UI controller that writes the field into the client's own data table and re-renders (MCP's VM can invoke a BindableFunction across VMs; next sync overwrites it). PowerShell: `R` is an alias of Invoke-History - never name a helper function R.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Nested `powershell -File gpt-image.ps1 -Prompt $p` (PS 5.1) fails instantly with no output when the prompt contains double quotes (e.g. sign text "OBEY THE GRID") — native arg passing splits it. Quote sign/label text with single quotes inside prompts.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Neon sign art via ChatGPT: "flat front view, dark navy board + gunmetal frame with bolts, chunky rounded neon tubes, glow tight around the tubes, max 2 neon colors, text spelled exactly as written, no wall/poles/shadow, white bg" gave 10/10 correctly spelled signs first try (~27 s each). Cut out with `cutout-white.ps1 -KeepAspect -Size 1024` (new switch: keeps the sign's aspect instead of a square canvas).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Secret-rarity line that worked for parts/pets: "most insane design but still clean and readable; holo rainbow-shift glow only on small accents; small holo rainbow gem on the mount" — keeps the set's subtle-glow look while reading as top tier. Create output dirs in the queue script before Copy-Item (a failed bash heredoc skipped my mkdir).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Customuse base64 upload of a flat-white WebP came back corrupted (the transport dropped a repeated chunk). Give the canvas a subtle non-flat gradient so the base64 has no long repeats, and md5-check the upload; all later uploads were exact. Customuse CR1 'Generate Model & Texture' (1K tex, faceLimit) = 70 credits; 2 parts side by side in one picture both come out textured and split cleanly by island (1 run = 2 parts).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Customuse GLB in Blender: model front faces +X, width along Y; rotate -90 deg about Z for a front -Y rig space. Merge by distance first (UV seams split verts into hundreds of islands). Pairs built from a 3/4 picture (eyes) come out yawed per side: rotate + slide each side separately to sit flat. Whole-pet CU meshes: find the body with ray casts from inside that keep only hits whose normal points along the ray (skips eyes/gems inside), scale the body to the standard cube (non-uniform, mix 0.5 for egg bodies), then delete faces within ~0.04-0.07 of the rounded box: accessories stay, gold bands that hug the body go with it. Script: the project's models/v3/secrets/fit_secrets.py + secrets_cfg.py.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] A local plugin loads after a Studio restart, but AssetService:CreateAssetAsync can still fail ("CreateAssetAsync and CreateAssetVersionAsync are not available yet"). Test one small asset before queueing everything; plan on File > Import 3D (Merge Meshes OFF) + an installer script as the real route.
- 2026-10-24 [study] Play-session with only F7 likes and no words: marks come in bursts (e.g. 5 likes in 10 s on one screen). Treat a burst as one strong like for that screen (count it as "liked Nx"), open the first full shot of each burst + 1 per distinct screen (~8-12 shots), and read the rest from the contact sheets. A repeat session of an already-studied game: append a dated "Session N" section to the same study instead of a new file, and only add what's new.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] A shared Config folder whose init requires every child: put helper modules (odds, boosts) in their OWN child module that requires siblings directly (script.Parent.Eggs) - adding functions to a data table like Config.Eggs breaks every `for id in pairs(Config.Eggs)`. A service's own 'start an event' function must not be called Start when the boot loader calls svc.Start() with no args (renamed to Begin).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Before an allow-list sync, check other agents didn't touch your files: in Edit, fetch each disk file over the local http server and list Studio lines that are NOT in the disk copy - every hit should be a line you replaced.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] UIStroke on a TextLabel/TextButton defaults to ApplyStrokeMode Contextual (outlines the TEXT): for a chip/button border set Border explicitly, or the text turns into an unreadable blob of the border color.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Multi-hatch screen: place eggs in SCREEN slots (u,v -> camera space at depth D via tan(FOV/2) and aspect) so layouts survive phones; dim the world with a big translucent navy Part ~4 studs behind the stage (a ScreenGui dim would also cover the 3D eggs) + DepthOfField; hide the own avatar with LocalTransparencyModifier each frame while it's up. PetBuilder.Build(species, {}) gives a faceless cube: pass Config.Pets[s].StarterParts.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Client auto-hatch over a server-validated RemoteFunction: fetch the next round once the current one is REVEALED (pets stay up until it lands), treat a silent nil as cooldown (retry 0.25 s), stop on a Notify "error" during the call. Another agent can overwrite the Play lock file and stop your Play mid-test: re-read the lock right before each capture.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Studio reads glTF vertex colours as raw sRGB (no linear->sRGB): colours painted with Blender `color_srgb` come out darker and more saturated (gold E8B54A turns orange). For "real colour" vertex-colour pieces write the sRGB value into the linear `.color` field (bodykit.paint_runs does this).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] `require()` from execute_luau in Edit caches the ModuleScript for the whole Studio session: after another agent edits a module, require a fresh `:Clone()` (same parent) to test the new code.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] A cartoon bite that is deep but still shows only a modest notch on the side: two boolean cutters, a broad jagged one (triangle-wave rim, 8 teeth) for the edge plus a smooth inner pit for depth; one big superellipsoid either stays shallow or opens the whole side face.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Several ModuleScripts with the same name under one parent: `require(parent.X)` / FindFirstChild / WaitForChild all return the FIRST in GetChildren order - compare `table.find(parent:GetChildren(), parent:FindFirstChild("X"))` with each copy's Source to know which one is live.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Mesh-AABB boxes give false clip flags (empty corners of wing/horn boxes overlap). `AssetService:CreateEditableMeshAsync(Content.fromUri(meshPart.MeshId))` works from execute_luau in Edit: read vertices x (Size/MeshSize), add triangle centroids/edge midpoints, split into ~0.6-stud slabs along the longest axis -> tight boxes (PetV2.MetaBake). False flags went from 1 to 0 on the approved Void pet.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Accessories unanchored + WeldConstraint/Motor6D to an anchored body follow `body.CFrame = ...` tilts and `model:PivotTo` in Edit mode too (drift 0.00025). Set the Motor6D piece's CFrame to body*C0*C1:Inverse() before parenting the motor so Edit matches Play.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Pet secondary motion on ANCHORED rigs (everything anchored + PivotTo): joints do nothing, so write accessory groups with workspace:BulkMoveTo(parts, cfs) as body.CFrame * pivotXform * restRel, pivotXform = rest * R * rest:Inverse() (rest = pivot in body space, R = pure rotation). The pivot point is fixed by construction: measured hinge drift 0.00006 stud at 5 deg tilt + 35 deg flap. Unanchored rigs: Motor6D.Transform = R (Play) / C0 = C0rest * R (Edit); Weld with an attachment pivot: C1 = A * R:Inverse() * A:Inverse() * C1rest (A = attachment CFrame in the part). Module: Shared.PetV2.FloatMotion.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Spring lag for short accessories: drive = lever x (-accel) * gain / max(leverLen, 1); gain 1 with leverLen floor 0.5 pinned 0.35-stud ears at the 18 deg clamp on a hard stop (peak smoothed accel ~60 studs/s^2). Gain 0.45 + floor 1.0 gave ears ~12 deg, wings ~15 deg. Add a camera-distance LOD to secondary motion, but turn it off in Edit tests (the Edit camera is usually far away, so everything looks "frozen").
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Windows + `python -m http.server`: Python sets SO_REUSEADDR, so a second server on a port another agent already uses starts WITHOUT error and the OLD server keeps answering (you get its files / 404s). Pick a random high port and curl one known file before syncing to Studio.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Floating pieces that must follow a pet but not be welded (top aura): keep them Anchored, weld them only to their own root (root = Part0), put an `AnimPivot_Ornament_*` Attachment on the root at the socket, and store a body-space CFrame attribute per part. model:PivotTo carries them, FloatMotion's ornament pivot rewrites them each frame (bob), and a stateless `StepFloating` (BulkMoveTo body.CFrame * attr) covers direct body.CFrame tilts.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Adding a new rule generation next to a legacy one (v4 zones): key it on the asset meta (Zone / Version) and keep the legacy tables (Points, Order, SlotMap) byte-identical; put new sockets in their own table. 54 legacy tests stayed green with one honest change (count legacy socket names, not all S_ children).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] bmesh: after moving verts (v.co = project(...)) face normals are STALE; call bm.normal_update() before any "flip if facing inward" test. The V2 body r1 shipped with its -X, back and bottom shell faces inward (invisible in Studio/URP backface culling, hidden in Blender renders that draw backfaces). Check every closed mesh with a per-face normal vs analytic-outward count, or look at a Unity URP shot from the back.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Texture-swap mouth band: unroll by arc length (horizontal at the flat rows, vertical along the bevel) and pick the band height so length:height is a clean 8:1 -> any 1024x128 / 2048x256 image maps with square texels. Studio keeps glTF UVs with v0 = top. SurfaceAppearance AlphaMode=Transparency shows the shell through clear pixels; editing ColorMap on an existing SurfaceAppearance did NOT re-render in Edit (stayed white) - destroy and recreate the SurfaceAppearance (and plan runtime swaps as cloning a prepared SA/band, since SA maps aren't script-writable in live games).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] MCP screen_capture timed out with Studio minimized: restore the window (user32 ShowWindow(h, 3)), park the cursor off the viewport (SetCursorPos) so no hover box draws, then _tools/studio_grab.ps1 -Delay 1500. Hide edit-time HUD with StarterGui.ShowDevelopmentGui = false (restore after).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] One universal almond eye socket can still give every style its own eye shape: the eye rig brings body-colour LID plates (PaletteRole Main, same greyscale height shading as the shell) 0.012 inside the socket that fill everything except the style's slit (upper lid = angry brow line, lower lid = slit floor, margins taper to a point past the slit tips). a slit eye style read "worried googly" without lids and "angry slit" (like the concept) with them, for +124 tris. Check the 2D front shape offline (dump face polys from the .blend -> PIL) before uploading.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] A part that a v4 asset textures must NOT carry HideWhenSlot for that slot: Assembler.Build hides every HideWhenSlot==<slot> part after mounting, so the mouth band with HideWhenSlot="Mouth" would vanish exactly when a grin goes on. Front-only grin onto the 8:1 band: scale it to ~92% of the band height, place it at u 0.31-0.69 (ends land in the corner zones = the corner curl), compose at 2048x256, deliver at 1024x256.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Asset lookup across several folders (Parts, then V2.<Category>s): give the resolver an `assetsRoot` option so tests can prove priority order on a fake Folder tree instead of touching the real asset folders; return "where" too and surface it in the build report (report.Where) for debugging.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Before overwriting a shared module in Studio from disk, assert each Studio Source still has the byte length you saw at the start (cheap drift guard when other agents edit sibling modules in parallel) and compile-check every file with loadstring before writing any.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Eye blink on slit eyes with body-colour lid plates: squash eyeball + iris Y to 0.1 around the eyeball centre (not the lid); the dark socket wall shows through the slit = reads as a closed eye. Sliding the lid plate would move its slit hole with it. Module Shared.PetV2.EyeFX (attributes on the eye asset).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Iris clamp: an iris that is designed off-centre in its eyeball (a slit eye style +0.011 up) leaves the socket during a blink/pop unless the drift offset shrinks with the squash (offset.Y * scaleY) and the pop cap uses (eyeballHalf - |designOffset + drift|) / irisHalf. Test "socket margin" per frame (iris box inside eyeball box in body axes) caught both.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Other agents move preview models + the Edit camera while you test: a long Edit test that restores model:PivotTo(savedPivot) at the end can undo their move, and a screenshot can catch another pet. Re-read the model pivot and re-aim the camera right before each grab.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Wide-reach mouths on the band: a uniform stretch or a "bend" both flatten the grin from the front (the front view only sees u ~0.22-0.78; the source's crescent ends up on the side faces). What works: keep the middle 84% of the source at ~natural aspect on u 0.29-0.71 (the Common layout) and stretch only the last 8% each end onto the side faces with a monotone cubic (Fritsch-Carlson) column map: no seam, fangs/crescent read from the front, the ink curl shows on the sides in 3/4. Tool: models/v4/mouths/make_band_sheet.py --knots. Quadratic warps fold (negative density) once centre density x span ratio > 2: check the end stretch is positive before uploading.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Teeth on a SurfaceAppearance go lilac under a blue/purple stage: tint SurfaceAppearance.Color green-warm (0.94, 1, 0.84) to cancel R+B; a red-warm tint (1, 0.93, 0.82) made them pink. Texture-disc irises: transparent Part + SurfaceGui (LightInfluence 0) behind body-colour lid plates, with the iris PNG's rim alpha-feathered so it melts into a vertex-coloured amber lens (no visible circle edge inside the almond).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Bare-body ChatGPT picture: list every slot to leave out ("no wings, horns, ears, tail, armor or accessories of any kind") + "real recesses with visible depth (never painted on)" gave a clean bare cube first try. Skip -Ref to an accessorised anchor when you want the bare body. ChatGPT overdoes "barely visible" details (flush mount discs came out as gold-ringed studs): write the drift down for the modeler instead of spending a redo, unless a hard rule breaks.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Unrolled mouth texture on #00FF00: the first try drew realistic, too-neat human teeth even with "uneven". A -Ref edit of that same picture ("keep shape, ink and background; only fewer, chunkier teeth, each a different width/height/tilt like hand-cut paper") fixed it and kept the layout. Moving a face wrap round a cube corner with -Ref edits swings between overreach and front-only: set the reach with the band mesh + unrolled texture, use pictures only for the style.

Iris decal for a universal eye rig = separate iris-only picture (-Ref the approved pair + "single round flat iris disc, remove the socket outline and second eye"); body-colour lid plates cut the almond. Short fangs: "only a little longer than its neighbours, no long vampire fangs".
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] studio_grab.ps1 returned all-black JPGs mid-session: the Studio window had been minimized again (window rect 237x39). After every grab check the image mean/size (PIL ImageStat) and re-run user32 ShowWindow(h, 3) before retrying; a black shot is never "dark lighting".
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Re-run a disk test that hard-codes one preview on another one without editing it: serve the folder with python http.server on a random port, HttpService:GetAsync the file in execute_luau, `src:gsub("workspace%.the preview stand%.V4_Common", "...V4_Uncommon")`, then loadstring(src)(args). Texture-disc irises (SurfaceGui disc bigger than the eyeball, lids mask it) report a negative socketMargin and a 0 Y look range by design: judge them by the lid opening + a screenshot, not the eyeball-box test.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Dev stages ship on Publish unless stripped: tag each dev root with CollectionService "DevOnly" and run a ServerScriptService guard (logic in a child ModuleScript "Core" so tests can require a clone) that destroys GetTagged("DevOnly") + GetInstanceAddedSignal when not RunService:IsStudio(). Tags are lost when an agent deletes and rebuilds a stage: the test "real dev roots tagged" catches it, re-tag with Core.TagRoots().
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Hide-vs-destroy for hidden default parts: make it an opt (stripHidden) defaulting to `not RunService:IsStudio()` - Studio previews keep hide/restore for Unmount, live builds save the tris; mark the body (V2Stripped_<slot>) so Unmount after a strip is a safe no-op.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Studio lighting in this place pushes saturation hard: #2A1F5C rendered ~#12047B (navy/black), #4B2CC2 rendered ~#4C1AFC (electric blue). Fit a per-channel line from two test renders and solve for the target (concept front-face mean #322885 -> Studio colour ~#4A3488). Never recolour by attribute (PaletteRole=Main) or by part name across a whole folder like Workspace.the preview stand: other agents' legacy bodies also have "Shell" parts with PaletteRole Main (I had to restore 6 from PetAssets.BaseBodies). Walk only your own models by name.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Angry eye read on a level almond socket: lower each style's top lid plate linearly toward the inner corner (0.045 stud at the inner end, clamped to keep >= 0.012 of the slit open) = a brow cut. Grin crescent from the front: arch the SOURCE (corners up 13% of band height, teeth 85%) before the knots map, and narrow the front span so the front stretch stays ~1.15 (an arched source at the old span came out 1.4x fat).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Driving a Studio-only dev UI from execute_luau in the Client DataModel: put a BindableFunction "Control" in the ScreenGui (Invoke(cmd, a, b)) - bindables cross the plugin/LocalScript contexts where _G/shared don't. execute_luau also resets any CameraType change it made after the call, so a Scriptable lab camera must be re-asserted in RenderStepped, not set once.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Pet lab "dark" lighting preset: ClockTime 19.3 / Brightness 1.1 / lamps x0.35 made the face unreadable (mean pixel ~10). ClockTime 17.8, Brightness 1.5, Ambient 90,84,130, OutdoorAmbient 100,94,140, lamps x0.7 keeps the face readable with glow eyes popping. A "Game" preset = snapshot of the real Lighting is separate (this game's Lighting is daytime, not dark).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] require(moduleClone) from execute_luau fails when the module requires script.Parent.X - parent the clone next to the original before requiring it (then destroy it). heredoc python via the Bash tool sometimes errors "unexpected EOF" on large quoted blocks: write the patch script with Write and run it instead.
- 2026-10-24 New template DevContentGuard: tag dev stages DevOnly; a ServerScriptService script destroys them on live servers only (Studio keeps them). Dev stages otherwise ship on Publish.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Unity `DegenPolish.Import` re-scales to a height and moves the pivot to the bottom centre, so a GLB exported back out of Unity loses "origin = socket point". For socket-based kits use Unity for material/look shots + Budget only and ship the Blender GLB (pieces keep exact body-space positions; installer checks every piece centre vs layout.json, 0.03 tolerance).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Spinning parts on an accessory: FloatMotion-style springs (AnimPivot_*) overwrite any extra rotation every frame and ornament spin only turns about body up. Give spinners Meta.AnimPivots (Assembler makes a Motor6D) and drive Motor6D.Transform = CFrame.fromAxisAngle(axisInBodySpace, t*speed) from a small RunContext=Client script inside the asset (falls back to BulkMoveTo from a static anchor part's stored body-space rest CFrame on anchored rigs; returns early outside Workspace). Name the visual attachments something other than AnimPivot_* so the float system leaves them alone.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Compat false "redesign" flags from round parts: a spinning disc as one square OBB clips its neighbours with its empty corners. Three thin rotated boxes (a cross + a 45-deg square, X = spin axis) approximate the circle and cleared the flag; also take accessory Bounds from the mesh AABB, not guesses. Pale shells (#E8F0FF) render lilac under a blue/purple stage: drop red ~8% (#D5ECFF matched the picture's front-face mean).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Opening a socket contract to new kit sockets without breaking old assets: take the new path ONLY where the old resolver would have refused (gate on a kit-version field + slot whitelist), so everything that resolved before resolves byte-identically. Body-only sockets: scan the body's S_<Name> attachments into an "Extra" table (unscaled body space) at build time so validators/compat know them; name a two-socket pair "A+B" (origin = midpoint) instead of inventing per-pair constants.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Pushing several edited modules to Studio safely in one call: fetch each from a 127.0.0.1 http server, check fetched size == disk size, loadstring() each to catch syntax errors, check Studio Source length == the pre-edit length (drift guard), and only then write all Sources. Tests then run on a clone of the module folder parented beside the original.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Setting a Script's Source from the Server DM during Play does NOT change what clients run (they keep the bytecode from Play start): edit the Source in Edit, then restart Play. For per-asset motion scripts, add self-timing (accumulate os.clock() per RenderStepped, write avg ms to a model attribute once a second) so Play perf can be read without the MicroProfiler.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Speed-dependent spin (props/rotors): integrate the angle (ang += dt * rate(speed)) instead of t * rate, or every speed change snaps the blades. ~140 deg/s idle reads as a playful hover, ~2x while moving; at 30-60 fps that's 2-5 deg/frame, crisp, never a strobe/blur.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] FloatMotion only animates AnimPivot_* Motor6Ds/Attachments, and Attachment pivots need a JointInstance: Assembler WeldConstraints give mode "none" (no motion). For accessory spring lag on Assembler pets, declare Meta.AnimPivots (-> Motor6D V2_<Name>) and drive Transform from the asset's own client script; port 8765/8791-style http.server ports are often taken by other agents' servers on Windows (duplicate binds silently share) - check `netstat -ano | grep :<port>` before serving.
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Pet follow with a Model:ScaleTo squash pop: always ScaleTo(baseScale * k) (read GetScale() at track time) or species scales (boss 1.5x) get reset; ScaleTo only when k moved > 0.003 and once back to exactly 1. Humanoid.Jump = true from MCP execute_luau does not jump; use hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping).

Detect jump/land for OTHER players from HRP vertical velocity (vy > 9 rising edge / was < -10 now > -2); Humanoid states don't replicate reliably.

- (map-builder, 2026-10-25) Studio data -> disk: a 12-line Python POST receiver on 127.0.0.1 + HttpService:PostAsync moves big scans (4k rooftop cells) without pasting output. Luau has no math.hypot (use Vector2.new(dx, dz).Magnitude).
- (map-builder, 2026-10-25) A Highlight parented to Workspace with Adornee nil outlines the WHOLE world yellow; check for stray Highlights when every screenshot shows outlines.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude scripter] Current avatars use the joint upgrade: R15 joints are AnimationConstraints (C0 read-only, mirrors Attachment0.CFrame), not Motor6Ds. Procedural body motion (flips, tucks) must write Attachment0.CFrame on AnimationConstraint rigs and C0 on Motor6D rigs; a Motor6D-only lookup silently does nothing. Pre-multiplying the root joint's offset (in HumanoidRootPart space) spins the body while the HRP, physics and camera stay upright.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude scripter] Server checks on client-owned characters: a 10 Hz "velocity jumped up" launch counter misses relaunches (replicated vy is smoothed), so it caught only 1 in 2 infinite-jump runs. A height cap (feet above the last ground > one launch at the airtime's top speed + every allowed air jump + 8 studs) caught 3 of 3 with 0 false flags on real triple jumps. Fall recovery = last spot stood on (ray down, |vy| < 4 for 0.25 s on an island top), reusing the existing fade remote.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude scripter] MCP execute_luau resets CameraType Scriptable back to Custom after each call ("changed camera type. Resetting"), so posed review shots need screen_capture with camera_position + look_at_position. Hold a pose by writing the joints in one call, then capture in the next. Keep a Play test harness as a disabled LocalScript in ServerStorage: clone it into PlayerGui from the Server DM, then drive it with attributes from the Client DM, so it shares the real controllers' module state.

- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude ui-designer] Another agent's Play session blocks syncing (edit-mode Source writes, HttpService and loadstring are all unavailable in Play): write the files and NOTES first, then poll get_studio_state, and never stop someone else's Play.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude ui-designer] Never put a UIGradient directly on a CanvasGroup: it tints the whole flattened group (rows, buttons, text all looked dim). Put the fill + gradient on a child `Bg` Frame (ZIndex 0) and keep the CanvasGroup background transparent.

Add small "side" lobes to walk round a building that fills its plaza.
- (map-builder, 2026-10-27) Spawn-view crowding: kit power poles placed at a bridge end near spawn or on the spine sit right beside the camera or in front of the landmark. Tie those gaps with pipes + chains instead and keep poles for the satellite bridges. Also, with a dark dusk sky a flat haze-deck part shows its square edge on the horizon, and dark cloud-sea ellipses read as holes; make the deck transparent and the puffs lighter than the sky at about 0.6 transparency.
- (map-builder, 2026-10-27) [build-a-pet] Organic floating islands without circles: a star-shaped outline r(θ) per island = smoothed ray-cast radius of the old lobe union x (1 + seeded harmonics k=2..7 + gaussian points/bays), then max() with protected circles (footprints, route samples every 5 studs r4, poles, trees) and pinned to the old edge radius at each bridge landing (blend over ~17 studs) so bridges/routes/buildings stay valid. Stepped strata = each band's top ring = previous band's bottom ring minus a 1.2-2.6 (+4-9% R) step; walls fluted ±jag on alternate columns; normals split at 38° (facets between columns, smooth bends). 1.4k-4.5k tris per island (build/organic/BuildOrganicIslands.cs.txt).
- (map-builder, 2026-10-27) glTFast (DegenPolish.ExportForRoblox) drops vertex colours (no COLOR_0) but the ASCII .fbx it writes beside the .glb keeps them (LayerElementColor): upload the .fbx through roblox-upload.ps1. FBX imports at a uniform ~15.09x (kit at 28x) with no rotation: Size = MeshSize / ratio, Position = origin + Position / ratio, ratio from one part's MeshSize vs its known stud size. Parts near 2048 studs at that scale get capped, so keep island meshes < ~135 studs or split them.
- (map-builder, 2026-10-27) Walkable organic mesh tops: don't trust PreciseConvexDecomposition; a triangle fan of invisible WedgePair floors (centre -> outline r-0.15, top face on the deck plane) + 1x9 invisible wall segments on r+0.3 (skipped within w+2.5 of bridge pins) passed every walk + edge test first try. Edit execute_luau needs HttpService.HttpEnabled = true only for the call that fetches from the local build server; restore it after.
- (roblox-3d-designer, 2026-10-27) Setting Model.PrimaryPart moves the Model's pivot to that part's centre (WorldPivot is replaced by PrimaryPart.CFrame * PivotOffset). For a floor-centre pivot set `Body.PivotOffset = Body.CFrame:Inverse() * wantedPivot` after choosing the PrimaryPart, then check `model:GetPivot()`; installers that set PrimaryPart last silently break footprints/placement.
- (roblox-3d-designer, 2026-10-27) Procedural Unity buildings (kit + one builder per building) beat generators for hub buildings: separate moving parts, exact palette, 0 credits, 1.4k-5k tris each. Grounding that reads: a rock skirt of 3 outline rings stepping down and out, root 2-4 studs under the floor, rubble + a pipe into the ground. Dark gunmetal walls go near-black under a dusk preset: lift those colour ramps ~1.2-1.3x (judge the Studio shot, not Unity).
- (roblox-3d-designer, 2026-10-27) Studio screen_capture: a minimized Studio returns black frames (ShowWindow 4 first), and while another agent runs Play mode the camera arguments are ignored and Edit-only cleanup is blocked; take all captures in one burst and clean temp Workspace previews before anyone presses Play.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude vfx] Beam facts measured in Play: positive TextureSpeed scrolls the texture from Attachment0 toward Attachment1 (put A0 at the source for falls/flows). A FaceCamera=false Beam spreads its width along the attachments' UpVector: for a flat ground decal-beam (pool, ring) build each attachment with CFrame.fromMatrix(pos, beamDir, horizontalPerp) and re-set the WorldCFrame when you spin it, or it stands up as a vertical sheet.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude vfx] Additive embers (LightEmission 1, size <= 0.4) vanish over a glowing beam/pool and in throttled Studio captures; LightEmission ~0.6 + Brightness 3, size 0.8 -> 0, Drag 0.5 and gravity ~-14 so they arc clear of the stream before falling. Studio Play renders ~22-40 fps when unfocused, so measure the script's own cost (os.clock around the Heartbeat step, exposed as an attribute) instead of trusting frame time.
- 2026-10-27 [build-a-pet, Claude vfx] Map-builder lava sources are 1-stud markers 4-5 studs behind the real pipe/spout mouth; read the stub MeshPart's vertices in Edit (AssetService:CreateEditableMeshAsync + Size/MeshSize scale) to find the mouth and the pool shelf height, then store them as per-source offsets (out, up, side).
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude map-builder] Custom skybox at dusk: Roblox dims and reddens the skybox by sun height, so at ClockTime ~17.9 a painted gradient renders near-black crimson; ClockTime ~16.6 shows it as painted. Atmosphere Haze > 0 paints a flat crimson floor over everything below the horizon; keep Haze 0 / Glare 0 and use Density (~0.2) alone for the distance fade. Paint the lower sky light (dusty violet), it renders much darker.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude map-builder] Walk tests in a game with custom controllers: client Humanoid:Move gets zeroed every frame by the game's controls (MoveDirection 0). Use a server-owned dummy (Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription, SetNetworkOwner(nil), Humanoid:MoveTo per point) for walk/edge tests; jump tests on the client work if Move + the re-jump (set AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y = JumpPower when falling) run inside a Heartbeat connection.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude map-builder] Luau build chunks: tables a function uses must be declared as locals BEFORE the function (a later `local X` leaves the function reading global nil -> "attempt to index nil with 'MainLow'"). Distant backdrop islands = scaled clones of existing island MeshParts (no upload), placed far and low (tops near the horizon) so they never frame the landmark from spawn.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Unity execute_code (codedom) only sees Editor scripts after a full domain reload: after copying a new .cs into Assets, call refresh_unity with scope all + compile request (scope scripts alone left the new class "does not exist"), then read_console for errors before running.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Procedural buildings: plan tris before writing - ArchWindow ~45, bollard ~112, porthole ~60, glyph medallion ~300, bevelled box ~44 vs 12 unbevelled; a 13-window round drum + trophy blew the 5k budget, trimming windows to 10 and one lathe from 14 to 12 sides fixed it without a visible loss.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Stepped round tiers (cake shapes) trap baked AO under every overhanging roof ring and the walls read black; bake them with lower ao/aoDistance (0.2 / 0.045) and check the shot, not just the average number. A colour change in a shared kit (rock hue) means re-running every building that uses it before uploading, so change kit colours first, upload last.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Roblox handedness: a viewer standing at -Z looking at +Z sees +X on their LEFT, so text and digits painted into a Unity texture for a -Z face come out mirrored unless you flip them in X; left/right layouts (podium 2 vs 3) swap the same way. Judge from the Studio shot, not the Unity one. Painted arched windows, straps and gear plates (0 tris) close most of the detail gap to a concept.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Freshly inserted meshes/SurfaceAppearances can render grey or invisible for seconds to minutes in Studio, and an older inserted copy can stay grey for the session while a fresh insert of the same model renders textured. Re-capture before judging, and fix grey copies by re-cloning from the newly inserted model, not by re-uploading.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Studio Edit plugin context: Lighting.Technology is not readable and ContentProvider:PreloadAsync always reports failure, so neither is a texture-load test there. Use EditableImage (AssetService:CreateEditableImageAsync) average colour to prove an image asset's data exists.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude 3d-designer] An uploaded image can be "Approved" and readable through EditableImage yet still render grey (or never fetch) on SurfaceAppearances in Studio. EditableImage proves the data exists, NOT that the renderer gets it. The fix that worked: re-upload the same textures as new assets, re-install, and confirm with a render capture of a fresh clone after ~15 s (V5.1 grey -> V5 r2 textured).
- 2026-10-27 [models, Claude modeler] Setting a Model's PrimaryPart moves its pivot. Set PivotOffset afterwards and check GetPivot() before placing or swapping shells.
- 2026-10-27 [models, Claude modeler] Copper and brass look olive above metalness ~0.3 in Roblox. Use SmoothPlastic (as V7 does) or metalness 0.18-0.25.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude lead] When a build might pause, write the 5-line spec into the asset folder (BRIEF.md) and have the agent keep a running NOTES.md. Then a stopped job can restart cold with no lost planning.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude scripter] Recolouring an item's VFX for a skin: SurfaceAppearance.Color is usually already tinted by the mesh-skin path; tinting it again in the VFX pass double-applies and undid the Shiny invert. Store each original ColorSequence/Color in an attribute before tinting and test that Clear restores every value exactly - that test is what caught it.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude scripter] Regrouping items (new chest/slot families) without a migration: derive the group from the item's config def on read (def override, then category map) and keep old ids, so saved data needs no rewrite and no schema bump; unknown categories fall to a "Legacy" group that still loads.
- 2026-10-27 [general, Claude scripter] When a service pre-rolls cosmetics and then calls the add function with NoRolls + Force flags, pass the roll's provenance record through too, or the stored item loses it (or is marked as forced). Keep one RollFor(kind) path that returns skin, shiny and provenance for every item type, plus one Disclosure(kind) the UI reuses.
- 2026-10-27 [models, Claude modeler] Roblox MeshParts ignore glTF COLOR_0, so vertex-colour AO baked into shallow (0.03) grooves never shows and the seams vanish under in-game lighting. Make seams read with a separate dark inset strip mesh (~0.044 wide, 0.004 above the groove floor, ~100 tris for 3 seams), or bake into a texture.
- 2026-10-27 [models, Claude modeler] A GLB pre-flipped (x,z * -1) for Roblox's 180 deg import loads as MeshParts already in body space with identity rotation, so the installer just places each at CFrame.new(bbox centre from layout.json). Tag the ServerStorage import copy with an AssetId attribute and reload it when the id changes, or a re-run silently reuses the old meshes.
- 2026-10-27 [models, Claude modeler] Neon glow on copper only reads with a hue+value gap: FF7A1A/FFC15A Neon on muted copper C47A4E/B8693F. Saturated orange copper swallows an orange leak.
- 2026-10-27 [models, Claude modeler] SurfaceAppearance.Color only darkens the ColorMap, so any part that Skins or Shiny recolour needs a near-white shading map (AO/edges only) plus a bright Color tint, not a fully painted colour map; skin code strips painted maps and Shiny turns them muddy.
- 2026-10-27 [art, Claude art-director] Ear accessories: say "two upright cupped ear shells, rim, recessed panel" in the ChatGPT prompt or it draws antennas/horns; one asset per picture on pure white.
- 2026-10-27 [art, Claude art-director] Check PIL im.mode before a white-to-alpha cutout: some gpt-image results already come back RGBA, and re-cutting them eats light highlights.
- 2026-10-27 [pipeline, Claude lead] For a set of 10+ concept pictures, queue them through one script (concepts/scrap-set/prompts/run_queue.sh pattern) that skips files already present, so a pause or failure resumes without paying twice.
- 2026-10-27 [models, Claude roblox-modeler] Blender headless image save: set colorspace_settings.name before assigning pixels, otherwise the buffer resets and the PNG is black.
- 2026-10-27 [models, Claude roblox-modeler] One near-white shading map in SurfaceAppearance.ColorMap + SA.Color tint per piece lets one 512 map set serve every colour region (and Shiny/Skins recolour SA.Color only); moving multi-colour parts = one MeshPart per colour grouped in a Model with the blade/ring as PrimaryPart and a named Attachment (X axis = rotation axis).
- 2026-10-27 [models, Claude roblox-modeler] Always preview accessories stacked (ears + halo on one body): slot positions alone hid a full clash; fix with a float offset on the Mount plus a StackLift attribute.
- 2026-10-27 [models, Claude roblox-modeler] Painted patina masks: hard boolean masks show cut lines; use smoothstep edges and corner gaussians.
- 2026-10-27 [scripting, Claude roblox-scripter] Stream a replay as events with task.defer and a LeadIn (1 s) after the "enter" event, so the client places the scene before the first action arrives; count LeadIn in the replay length and in any busy lock.
- 2026-10-27 [scripting, Claude roblox-scripter] Exactly-once item machines from a RemoteFunction: client sends a nonce, server reserves it as "pending" before any yield, caches the result (small LRU), a repeat returns the cache and re-fires the visual event; test same-nonce twice = one charge.
- 2026-10-27 [tooling, Claude roblox-scripter] Edit-mode require caches: to test changed Config, clone Shared, swap the clone in, require, then put the original back. Config item ids must match the installed models' PartId attrs (check both after renames).
- 2026-10-27 [tooling, Claude roblox-scripter] Bash heredocs with apostrophes fail in this harness's tool wrapper sometimes; write patch scripts with the Write tool and run python. MCP execute_luau returns only the first value cleanly: return one formatted string.
- 2026-10-27 [animation, Claude roblox-animator] Scripted motion on anchored parts: at the end of every move restore ALL rig groups (not just dirty ones) and snap parts to rest offsets recorded at the start, only when the error is noise (< 0.01 stud); PivotTo float drift otherwise grows ~1e-4 stud per spin, worse in Play.
- 2026-10-27 [animation, Claude roblox-animator] Freeze a pose for screenshots: drive the motion with a manual clock (Core.Manual + Core.Clock) and StartTime = now - t, step once, capture; a DriftTest that loops every move 20x on a fake 60 fps clock and diffs every part CFrame catches drift in seconds.
- 2026-10-27 [tooling, Claude roblox-animator] MCP screen_capture uses the current camera FOV and a camera facing +Z shows the scene mirrored left/right from the battle cam; reset FOV to 70 after shots.
- 2026-10-27 [vfx, Claude roblox-vfx-artist] Effect runners need their own pausable clock (Heartbeat dt, paused by a Workspace attr) and a watchdog that self-cleans ~3 s after the last stage: a frozen capture or a dropped Manual cleanup otherwise leaves orphans that break leftover tests. Clear the freeze attr before leftover tests, and wait >= 3.5 s between sequential CombatFX.Preview calls (it reports at 3.2 s).
- 2026-10-27 [vfx, Claude roblox-vfx-artist] Beam CurveSize bends along the attachment Axis: both axes yAxis + CurveSize0 = h, CurveSize1 = -h gives an upward arc of height 3f(1-f)h. Edit-mode screen_capture DOES show particles and beams; set the camera in the same execute_luau right before capturing (other agents move it).
- 2026-10-27 [vfx, Claude roblox-vfx-artist] Testing a module that uses script.Parent: keep the require clone parented until the test ends (destroying it makes script.Parent nil inside later calls). Ring frames made of boxes: length = 2r*sin(pi/n)*0.88 and a darker colour than the pet body, or they read as loose sticks.
- 2026-10-27 [scripter, Claude roblox-scripter] Edit-mode pure tests: swap a cloned Shared AND a cloned Services folder in under their REAL parents (ServerScriptService), not ServerStorage, or service requires that walk script paths fail with 'Requested module experienced an error'.
- 2026-10-27 [scripter, Claude roblox-scripter] Station stands: place by raycast onto the island (old anchors floated at z=600) and make them Atomic models under StreamingEnabled; melee contact needs a router-computed ctx.LungeTarget because motion modules clamp reach.
- 2026-10-27 [scripter, Claude roblox-scripter] Installing big scripts via MCP: temporarily set HttpService.HttpEnabled, GetAsync from a localhost python http.server, UpdateSourceAsync, restore the flag; compare length diffs to your edit before writing.
- 2026-10-27 [combat, Claude roblox-reviewer] Atomic arena models under StreamingEnabled are not on the client when the battle starts: server RequestStreamAroundAsync before moving the player, client waits for the ArenaSlot parts, and the view claims its stage BEFORE yielding so a fallback path can't build a second copy.
- 2026-10-27 [studio, Claude roblox-reviewer] Edit command-bar require() keeps stale module results after a Source push (Config.Parts had no new rows); clone the Shared folder in under its real name to test fresh, then restore. Play-mode screen_capture lags >13 s, so shoot in-fight or reveal frames in Edit with freeze previews.
- 2026-10-27 [ui, Claude roblox-ui-designer] execute_luau puts a Scriptable camera back to Custom if the camera changed during the call. To shoot a battle cam, start the fight with task.delay, return at once, then screen_capture.
- 2026-10-27 [ui, Claude roblox-ui-designer] Hiding a HUD for a mode: slide each HUD child off-screen (save its Position) instead of setting Visible. Self-toggling widgets (ClimbMeter flips Visible every 0.25 s) otherwise pop back. Restore the saved Positions with Back easing.
- 2026-10-27 [ui, Claude roblox-ui-designer] Phone check without a device: in the Play client, clone the HUD into an 844x390 frame with 47 px side insets and apply the controller's scale formula, then read AbsoluteSize for text and buttons (fight bar 0.676, SKIP 92x46).
- 2026-10-27 [scripter, Claude roblox-scripter] Camera "closer" requests: fit-clamp the push per viewport (binary search on a projected point set: pads + pet height, with HUD margins) instead of a fixed offset, and re-fit on ViewportSize change; read the live client cam CFrame and reproduce it in Edit for shots, since Play screen_capture can return a stale 3D frame.
- 2026-10-27 [scripter, Claude roblox-scripter] Stacked accessories (halo over ears/crown): measure the visible top of the items under the stacker's footprint in body space and rest on it + a small gap; a fixed lift floats over short items and sinks into tall ones. PetBuilder.Build takes a slot map ({Halo=id}), not a list (a list mounts under slots "1","2" inside the body).
- 2026-10-27 [scripter, Claude roblox-scripter] Rewards granted before a replay must not be announced before it: defer the client toast to the replay End/Leave for that battle id (timeout fallback). A skip/leave remote should shorten, not clear, the busy lock (floor) or skipping becomes a farm-speed exploit. require() from execute_luau gets a separate module cache, so it can't drive a running client controller; fire the remote directly.
- 2026-10-27 [scripter, Claude roblox-scripter] Server-driven auto loops (auto hatch/open): one run per player, a server roll window with a per-player lastRollAt that survives stop/start (else spamming stop/start beats the cadence), blockers polled between windows, one batched result event per roll. When a batched open is refused mid-roll, re-derive the reason (storage/currency) instead of reporting "error".
- 2026-10-27 [scripter, Claude roblox-scripter] Before syncing a shared service file into Studio, diff Studio's Source against git HEAD; only overwrite when equal (another agent may have live-edited it).
- 2026-10-27 [ui, Claude roblox-ui-designer] Item reward cards: show the item ALONE (clone the accessory into its own ViewportFrame, frame the camera on the bounds of parts with Transparency < 0.95, 3/4 front dir (0.42,0.28,-1), FOV 30) - never mount it on a preview pet (players read the pet's stats as the item's). Items whose look is pure VFX (Ground trail, ModelAura) resolve to an invisible anchor: detect "no visible parts" and draw a code emblem instead of an empty window.
- 2026-10-27 [ui, Claude roblox-ui-designer] Keep one results entry (HatchResults.Show) and route by row shape (PartId -> item cards, Species -> egg cards) with a lazy require, so other agents' callers never change. Auto-mode overlays: DisplayOrder under the Auto tab, no B/Esc binds (STOP owns B), close themselves on a timer, dedupe by Uid set.
- 2026-10-27 [ui, Claude roblox-ui-designer] To move a HUD stack another controller keeps re-positioning (MainUI relayout), don't touch Position: add a named UIScale and tween it to 0 around the stack's anchor, then Visible=false; restore with a Back bounce. Studio Edit can switch to Play under you (another agent): multi_edit fails atomically with "Edit datamodel is not available", so recheck get_studio_state before each sync.
- 2026-10-27 [scripter, Claude roblox-scripter] Give a remote a new parameter without breaking old clients: RemoteGuard optional trailing args (`{ optional = "integer" }`: nil passes, wrong type/NaN/extra args refuse). Return refusals as `{ Ok = false, Code }` tables, not nil, so the UI can say why (busy / tokens / storage).
- 2026-10-27 [scripter, Claude roblox-scripter] Retiring old shop ids: keep two resolvers, `Resolve` (the item that actually opens, following alias + redirect chains with a hop cap) and `Base` (the row whose own pool/odds old tests and UI read). Opens move; old odds tests stay green.
- 2026-10-27 [scripter, Claude roblox-scripter] Moving many scripts into Studio: toggle HttpService.HttpEnabled on only for the call (works from MCP in Edit and on the Play server), PostAsync Studio sources to a tiny local Python server to diff them on disk, HttpGet disk files back with a loadstring check. Big inline Python heredocs with quotes break bash; write patch scripts to files.
- 2026-10-27 [ui-designer] Two agents feeding the same card screen (ChestOpen hop-out + AutoRoll feed) = double cards. One owner shows them; the other only publishes layout hints as a LocalPlayer attribute (AutoRollTabReserve) that the card module reads when its caller passes none.
- (scripter 2026-10-27) Chest/crate pools can name parts that are only AssetRegistry placeholders (no model) and the player pulls an invisible item. Keep a test that walks every enabled pool and calls Assembler.FindAsset (fallback: PetAssets:FindFirstChild(id, true)); pull placeholders out of the pool, not out of Config.Parts, so saved items still load.
- 2026-10-27 [vfx-artist] Skins on accessories: honour TintOnly on the MODEL too (walk up to 6 ancestors), not only on the SurfaceAppearance; otherwise baked-AO Scrap SAs get stripped under every overlay. Tint-only SA = keep it and recolour via SA.Color.
- 2026-10-27 [vfx-artist] Effect rigs parented outside the item model (Workspace.ScrapAccessoryFXLive) never see a skin. Give the rig module a Tint(host, map) that stores originals per rig and call it from the overlay hook with a map(color, role) built from the skin palette (ItemVisuals.FxMap); a plain re-Apply calls Tint(host, nil) to restore.
- 2026-10-27 [vfx-artist] Studio source can be newer than git HEAD (another agent synced without committing). Patch Studio by a plain-find anchor + length check, never by pasting the whole local file over it.
- 2026-10-27 [vfx-artist] AuraFX sizes a look only from a Body with Transparency < 1: an invisible stand-in pet for a chest reveal needs 0.999, not 1. Gearprint-style floor FX need a hidden floor plate under the stand-in. In amber-lit bays cap LightEmission at 0.4.
- 2026-10-27 [map-builder] upload_image only takes http(s) URLs: serve the texture folder with `python -m http.server` on a free port (others may hold 8765/8791). MaterialVariant has no Pattern property and only shows when the part's Material equals the variant's BaseMaterial. Tag every part you retexture/move with original-value attributes (WorldPolishOrig*) so it can be reverted.
- 2026-10-27 [modeler] The shared ChatGPT window can hand back another agent's picture (two accessory concepts came back as ground textures): look at every concept before using it, an MD5 duplicate check doesn't catch it. Keep an installer ModuleScript in ServerStorage so each install only pastes its CFG. Studio edit calls fail while another agent is in Play: do Blender work meanwhile.
- 2026-10-27 [lead] A "pause" with 5 agents running: message each to stop at a safe point (finish or revert the change in hand, stop Play, free the play lock, NOTES with DONE / IN PROGRESS + exact next step), wait for every report, then hand off. All 5 stopped clean within ~20 min.
- (2026-10-27, map-builder, world polish) `roblox-upload.ps1` returns Decal ids (type 13); a MaterialVariant or Texture needs the image id (`InsertService:LoadAsset(decalId):FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Decal").Texture`), else "Asset type does not match requested type".
- (2026-10-27, map-builder) A MeshPart's collision hull bevels ~0.7 studs down at the rim: trim placed at the rim raycast height hides under the rendered top; sample the height ~3 studs inside the rim. On a Top-face Texture, V=0 lands at the part's local +Z.
- (2026-10-27, map-builder) In Play tests, script `Humanoid:Move()` is overwritten every frame by the control script: aim the camera by code, hold W with real key input, press Space with the keyboard tool (triple jump = 3 presses ~300 ms apart).
- (2026-10-27, reviewer) Jump-window clearance: split the window's marker parts into the two rims at the biggest height jump, cast rays rim to rim at several heights and side offsets (a box overlap reports the side walls as blockers). Measure gaps/rises lip to lip from placed parts; build-script numbers ran up to 0.9 studs low.
- (2026-10-27, reviewer) Fall-recovery test: stand on the islet >= 1.5 s so the safe point is set, then drop into the gap and log Y every 0.4 s (Build-A-Pet catch fired ~1 s after the fall).
- (map-builder r18 turn, 2026-11-02) place_on_hub's 3-stud back push must be off (anchor attribute MaxShift=0) once the anchors already clear the step behind; otherwise portals sink into the next row.
- (map-builder r18 turn) After a perf merge, parts lose their names and positions as lookup keys. Walk-test stairs by ray-casting along each stair's own direction instead of finding steps by name.
- (map-builder r18 turn) Check a new terrace footprint in a small Python model (rows, row depth, stair width vs dock gaps) before building in Studio. It shows "no room for middle stairs" for free.
- (lead r18 turn) Play shots of hub areas: call RequestStreamAroundAsync and move the character there first; with streaming on, the terrace isn't on the client otherwise.
- (lead r18 turn) An amphitheatre that rises +6 studs per row still hides the back rows from plaza eye height (~5 studs). Use +8-9 per row, and keep gate pillars out of the front row's line of sight.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, lead r19] A client script that only reacts to attribute changes (GetAttributeChangedSignal + "skip if unchanged") never shows the starting state: apply the look once at attach time too (locked portal lips stayed lit until that fix).
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, lead r19] Play-mode shots: setting workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame once gets undone by the default camera scripts. BindToRenderStep at RenderPriority.Last+1 to hold it, and anchor the HumanoidRootPart at the camera spot so streaming loads that area.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, reviewer r19] Check "no two alike" in the gameplay shot at player distance, not in per-item close-ups: neighbours with similar colours blur together at ~40 studs even when their patterns differ.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, reviewer r19] A Blender light bake on bright toon colours needs about 2x the AO and rim you'd expect before it shows in Roblox: always judge it with a before/after at player distance.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, reviewer r19] Client quality checks must treat touch-only devices (TouchEnabled and not KeyboardEnabled) as Low: phones report the graphics setting as Automatic. Use one shared check in every FX script.
- 2026-11-02 (vfx r19): Studio HttpEnabled is off by default, so a client/edit sync that fetches files from a local http.server must toggle it on and back off, or patch Source with ScriptEditorService:UpdateSourceAsync.
- 2026-11-02 (vfx r19): orbiting trail beads: put the emitter on a spinning attachment with LockedToPart=false and Speed=0, so the particles stay where they were born and draw an arc instead of riding the bead.
- 2026-11-02 (vfx r19): with streaming on, pick the "next unlock" from the highest rank ever seen Available plus one (kept in a table across stream-out), not from the lowest locked portal that happens to be streamed in.
- (r20 walk-through portals, 2026-11-02) Test suites that find world instances by tag (PortalInstance) break once the real models are placed: override the lookup to only see the test's temp instances, and restore it after.
- (r20) CollectionService tags also live on ServerStorage backup copies: any start-up "apply to tagged" script must check IsDescendantOf(workspace) first.
- (r20) Walk-through travel: detect the crossing on the client in VortexAnchor space (prevZ < 0 <= Z inside a generous box, step under 4 studs), then let the server re-check rank, range from the anchor and a token bucket; refused = push back out the front, never a dead stop.
- (2026-11-02, art-director) Screen-share audit without captures: when Studio is held in Play by another agent, raycast a 64x36 grid from walking-eye views on the Server DataModel (read-only) and classify hits by name; it ranks what to remake first (Scrap hub: ground+path ~41% of the screen).
- (2026-11-02, art-director) gpt-image.ps1 can return a stale older picture from the same ChatGPT chat (lazy-loaded image appears after the before-snapshot): always open the saved file by eye and compare size with older files in build-a-pet/chatgpt/ (identical bytes = stale) before using it; rerun once fixes it.
- 2026-11-02 (modeler r20 portals): raycasts skip CanQuery=false visual meshes (Lip, InnerBand, Tunnel). To measure a visual sill or opening, set CanQuery=true on those parts for the probe, then restore the saved values.
- 2026-11-02 (modeler r20 portals): MeshPart.TextureID ignores alpha, so transparent texels render as the texture's own RGB (a dark alpha-gradient texture came out pure black) and MeshPart.Color does not show through. For one texture shared by many parts and tinted per part, use SurfaceAppearance with AlphaMode=Overlay and ColorMap set (MeshPart.Color shows where alpha is low), or bake one texture per colour.
- 2026-11-02 (modeler r20 portals): roblox-upload.ps1 holds every new GLB Model until you render it front, back, side and top; re-run with -Reviewed after the check. A GLB with an embedded texture also auto-uploads the image, and the MeshPart arrives with TextureID already set.
- 2026-11-02 (scripter r20): ScreenInsets.DeviceSafeInsets ignores IgnoreGuiInset, so a toast root lands under the 58 px top bar. Use CoreUISafeInsets for HUD toasts, and keep toast text to 30 characters or fewer (Notify's two-line H=74 clips on small viewports).
- 2026-11-02 (scripter r20): a light bake captures the shadows of props that toggle later (e.g. the plaque padlock), and the shadow stays after the prop hides. Hide anything togglable before baking.
- 2026-11-02 (scripter r20): one remote name, one owner. Grep both disk and Studio for NetService.Invoke("<name>" before adding a handler; IslandService and WorldRankService both registered "UsePortal".
- 2026-11-02 (modeler r20 portals): a light bake paints the shadow of every part that can be hidden in game (padlock on the plaque). Bake with that part hidden (bake_light_r18.py --hide Padlock) and copy its own texels back from the old pass cache (--fillfrom), so the plaque reads clean when the part is gone and the part still looks baked when it shows. An installer that copies TextureID from the GLB will bring the old atlas back, so keep re-baked atlas ids in an override map (make_installer.py ATLAS).
- 2026-11-02 (modeler r20 ground): a Blender GLB exported Y-up lands in Studio (InsertService) yawed 180 degrees: Blender +X comes in as -x and +Y as +z. Place it with CFrame.new(O) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi, 0) * part.CFrame. Scripts can't set MeshPart.CollisionFidelity (it stays Default), but on a gently crowned ground mesh Default raycasts within 0.03 studs of the surface, so it's walkable.
- 2026-11-02 (modeler r20 ground): in Blender bpy, re-fetch `obj.data.uv_layers[...].data` after every edit/object mode toggle. A stale reference silently drops foreach_set, and the UV squash into a sub-rect never happens. Also keep thin strips (brass edging, grout beds) off smart-UV: give them painted swatch UVs, or they bake as zigzags or take half the atlas.
- 2026-11-02 (modeler r20 ground): a bright kit-ground patch laid on old olive lawn reads as a lit rectangle from above. Fade the outer 7 studs of albedo about 80% toward the old ground colour (smoothstep) plus an edge grass fringe. Still faintly visible against the textured Grass material, so swap the whole plot at once, not one patch.
- 2026-11-02 (modeler r20 portals): prove a "removed" cap is really gone with a raycast through the hole, not a boundary-edge count. The r20 tunnel had two caps (end fan plus a flat back disc on the axis), and the shots looked open until a ray hit the back plane. Delete the disc centre vertex too, then put a separate dark end dome (one shared 200-tri mesh, tinted per rank with Lerp(black, 0.6)) behind the opening.
- 2026-11-02 (ui-designer, Notify r20): a top-centre toast lane must measure the HUD button grid at runtime (MainUI HUD LeftButtons/RightColumn/Arena/Logo AbsolutePosition+Size, +4 px label overhang, +12 px gap) and centre in the free band beside it, shrinking to the band before ever dropping under the grid (N.MinFit 0.5); both ScreenGuis use CoreUISafeInsets so their Absolute coords share one frame. Studio test without a resizable viewport: anchor MainUI.Root at 0,0 sized 812/s x 317/s and scale its UIScale x0.7 (56 px cells) + box Notify.Root to 812x317, then restore; execute_luau Client require() gets its own module copy (HudV2.Layout there is a no-op).
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, reviewer r20] MCP execute_luau runs in its own context in Play, so it can't fire client-side remotes as the player; drive play tests through the game's DevCommand remote instead.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, reviewer r20] To check a walk-through trigger, probe the opening with GetPartBoundsInBox first, then confirm by actually walking the character through it: a box probe alone misses CanTouch/CanQuery gaps.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, reviewer r20] To hold the camera for a Play-mode shot, bind it with RunService:BindToRenderStep at Camera priority + 1; setting CFrame once gets overwritten by the default camera next frame.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Keep the revealed pet the hero: put the big aura layers BEHIND it, not around it. Push the BillboardGui plane back with StudsOffset Z = -(radius + 0.6) (camera space), set ParticleEmitter ZOffset and Beam ZOffset to -(radius + 0.8..1), and pass aura colours through a "vivid" clamp (saturation >= 0.62, value <= 0.92; neutrals capped at value 0.62). Once the layers sit behind the pet they can be dense again; the r3 pass that only thinned them read tame at 13 studs.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Shared Play sessions: another agent's review script can drive workspace.CurrentCamera (even with CameraType Scriptable). Read the camera position back after a few frames before trusting distance-based captures.
