- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Big additive VFX (BillboardGui flames, wide Beam pillars, large flipbook licks) on a bright white plaza with Bloom turn into a white wall when the camera is within ~6 studs. Fade those layers by camera distance (1 at >= 12 studs, ~0.35 at <= 4), keep Bloom <= 0.35 for them, and tint the front layer with the saturated tier colour, not white.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Cheap anime impact frame with no full-screen white: 2 beats of 0.06 s, ColorCorrection Saturation -1 + Contrast 1.1-1.6 (dark world, then light world) plus a Highlight (FillTransparency 0, AlwaysOnTop) on the revealed model, white then black. Only then start the normal soft flash.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Capture QA: Escape in Play opens the Roblox menu over the shot; close a results panel by clicking its button (user_mouse_input with instance_path). A background PrintWindow capture can return 237x39 frames when Studio is not the top window, so check one frame's size before building a sheet.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Ground raycast under a hovering egg hits whatever is below it (here the pedestal lava dish 1.3 studs under the crown), so the base lands in the dish. Trust the caller's ground CFrame and only snap when the hit is within ~0.6 stud.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Fading your own avatar during a reveal: the default TransparencyController rewrites character LocalTransparencyModifier every frame, so set it again inside your RenderStep each frame and set it back to 0 at the end.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Studio MCP tests: each execute_luau that requires a module gets its own module VM, so a pooled rig gets built once per call (duplicate rigs in PlayerGui). Run a whole batch inside one call, report through a player attribute, and expect other agents to fire hatches into a shared Play session.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] "0 instances per reveal" needs more than pooled emitters: TweenService:Create makes a Tween Instance every call. A 30-line manual tweener (list of {obj, prop, a, b, t0, dur, style} stepped in one BindToRenderStep with TweenService:GetValue) plus hand Emit()s from the step kept Stats.Created flat across plays (117 at warm-up, +0 per Secret reveal, 0.16 ms/frame step).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Land a riser exactly on an impact: a 4 s "reverse hard stop" riser started at TimePosition = len - (charge + slow) hits its stop on the break frame at every Fast Open speed; no pitch change needed. Sound.TimeLength needs PreloadAsync first (0 before).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] (lyopz later removed all reveal text for Build-A-Pet; keep this only for games that use a banner) Tall SECRET banners at the top of a 3rd-person view cover the revealed base: the egg sits mid-screen at normal zoom, so keep a reveal banner <= 25% of screen height (UISizeConstraint ~520x168) and keep the world dimmed (ColorCorrection -0.17..-0.3) through the hold on bright white plazas, or additive auras vanish.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude scripter] Chained timed states (Buildup -> Critical -> Hatch -> Reveal -> Done): carry the overshoot into the next state (stateTime = old - duration), else every state adds up to one fixed step (+0.1 s over 5 states at 30 Hz) and cue/timing tests drift. Log the state change BEFORE the state's entry cues so cue-order tests read naturally.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude scripter] Anchored "breathing" plates with a hard MaxOffset: translate d in [0, Max] along the normal, then clamp the rock angle to (Max - d) / lever (lever = farthest box corner from the hinge); every point then moves <= Max. Proven by an 8-corner sweep (3 qualities x 3 states x 12 s) + a 4000-case fuzz of the pure pose function. A BulkMoveTo controller detects someone else's PivotTo by comparing GetPivot() with the pivot recorded after its own write (works with or without a PrimaryPart) and rebases base = pv * last:Inverse() * base.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude scripter] Free-audio proof without Play: MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(id) -> IsPublicDomain true + Creator (ProSoundEffects / APMOfficial / DistrokidOfficial), and ContentProvider:PreloadAsync gives Success + TimeLength in Edit. Long risers (9 s) must be stopped at the release cue. Model.Destroying fires deferred: task.wait() before asserting an unbind in Edit tests.
- 2026-10-25 [general, Codex] Decorative rails may close an otherwise open shell. Removing the rails requires a closed replacement shell; assert manifold edges, positive volume and full bounds before export.
- 2026-10-25 [general, Codex] Roblox can import a GLB node named Shell as a Model containing a MeshPart named Cube. Inspect the hierarchy and map that specific node before installing; never guess mesh IDs.
- 2026-10-25 [general, Codex] Keep centre-origin/stud source exports distinct from bottom-origin/metre Unity exports. Reusing an installer across those conventions moves sockets and changes scale.
- 2026-10-25 [general, Codex] Cosmetic motion must yield when joints or enabled animation drivers appear after binding, and cleanup must not restore over the new owner's pose. Particle budgets include existing skin/Shiny emitters, even when LOD has disabled them.
- 2026-10-25 [general, Codex] CoreMaterialFX is a caller-driven, bounded material aura template (69 Studio assertions); it owns no update connection. Pass camera distance/visibility/quality to Step, and Destroy on removal. Packed with roblox-templates/make_rbxmx.py.
- 2026-10-25 [general, Codex] Read Luau source with explicit UTF8 when transferring from PowerShell; the default code page can turn punctuation and UI symbols into mojibake even though the source compiles.
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] A successful build can silently select a generic fallback; verify catalog-to-template identity as well as non-nil construction, and check empty MeshIds in fresh Play (Codex, 2026-10-25).
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Adding a per-item cosmetic roll (auras): one optional field (id or nil) + a pure config module with per-source { Chance, RareBoost } and an Odds() helper the UI reuses; roll in the one item-creation service, pack it in the sync codec, carry it through fusion as "rarest of inputs", and roll it BEFORE the auto-delete decision so a lucky roll is never thrown away. Migration only cleans bad types and keeps unknown ids.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Version-pinned test checks (`Version == 5`) go red on every schema bump; assert `>=` for old-migration tests and keep exactly one "current version" check.- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] Slider settings rounded with floor(v/0.01+0.5)*0.01 give 0.35000000000000003 (equality checks fail) — divide by the inverse instead: floor(v*100+0.5)/100. Read rotated cylinder tops via the world AABB (|R.Y|*sx/2 + ...), not Size.Y/2.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Swappable 'look' layers (Base Bodies) on a socketed model: keep the invisible anchor part + its slot Attachments as the contract, hide the old shell (store original Transparency in an attribute), clone the new shell aligned by an `Anchor` part; for animated fragments that carry sockets store rest offsets as CFrame ATTRIBUTES (piece^-1*socket on the shell, socket^-1*pivot on each mounted part) so a client can re-pivot accessories on a server-built model. Test with a stand-in template created during Play.
- 2026-10-24 [pet-sim] Equip-one-of-many items (body per pet): make the pet field (pet.Body = uid) the only truth and DERIVE 'equipped on' in the sync codec; trade/delete/rekey code then needs no extra bookkeeping (strip the field from traded copies). Shared lock files get grabbed the second you stop Play: re-read the lock right before EVERY start_stop_play, not just before captures.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] Trading needs globally unique item ids: per-player ids ("x"..NextId) collide across players → upgrade an item to a GUID the first time it's offered and repoint team/loadout/equip refs.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] Two-player DataStore trade without a central store: prepare record (PendingTrades: gives + exact copies of gets) saved to both → commit both in memory in one non-yielding block → save both; on load, read the partner's record (GetAsync, no lock) and roll forward or undo with the same apply function. Guarantee = no dupe/loss for one server crash; NOT for a failed leave-save + rejoin elsewhere before the partner saves. Test by dropping in-memory profiles unsaved. Gate trading to Studio until a real 2-client + published crash test pass.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] Rich per-item fields (abilities, shiny, color, fusion) tripled the full client sync (215 KB→ sent every second with generators). Pack instances (codec: only fields the client can't derive from config) and round-trip test unpack = original. Next step is delta sync.
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] Migration tests with injected old-format profiles must only touch the in-memory store; with real DataStores active (Studio API access on) test on a copy — an injected profile would overwrite the developer's real save. Store rolled values as 0–1 quality so rebalancing ranges rescales owned items.
- (ui, 2026-10-26) Studio script Source comes back CRLF while patch strings are LF: normalise with gsub("
- (ui, 2026-10-26) Overlays in ViewportFrame cards: call ItemVisuals.ApplyOverlay on the CLONE inside the viewport (never the PetIcon cache template); tagged textures then animate via ItemVisualsController.
- (ui, 2026-10-26) A Volcanic results screen only opens for hatches the HatchBridge requested itself: test end to end with require(VolcanicEggReview.HatchBridge).Request("real","ScrapEgg:4") after DevCommand give tokens, not a raw DevCommand volcanichatch.
- (ui, 2026-10-26) python http.server on a used port answers 404 silently: pass --directory and pick a fresh port.
- (ui, 2026-10-26) "No you-opened notifications": stop it at every layer (server meta Quiet, ClientState early return, SecretBanner skips payload.UserId == LocalPlayer); one-layer fixes leave toasts behind.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude ui-designer] Escape never reaches a ContextActionService binding (the core menu marks it gameProcessed). To close a screen on Esc, also listen on UserInputService.InputBegan for KeyCode.Escape regardless of gameProcessed; keep ButtonB on CAS.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude ui-designer] UI modules required from MCP execute_luau run in the plugin VM: GUIs build fine, but CAS/UIS never see real input there. For input tests, create a temporary LocalScript in PlayerGui (from the Server DM) that requires the module and exposes a BindableEvent; drive it from the Client DM.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude ui-designer] ZIndexBehavior Sibling: a raised hero card (higher ZIndex) paints ALL its descendants (glow, rays 1.9x the card) over neighbouring cards. Put the glowing card BELOW its siblings (ZIndex 4 vs 5) so its halo stays behind them.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude ui-designer] Staggered reveals: schedule from one start time (wait until os.clock()-t0 >= target) instead of chaining task.wait(stagger); chained waits round up each frame and 8 cards drifted 1.14 -> 0.98 s once fixed.
- 2026-10-25 [build-a-pet] Big Studio installers without pasting: serve the folder with `python -m http.server 8765 --bind 127.0.0.1`, then in Edit execute_luau `HttpService:GetAsync("http://127.0.0.1:8765/x.lua", true)`, wrap as `"return function()
- 2026-10-25 [build-a-pet] Ink-style pet faces with no textures: one thin MeshPart per colour (ink, teeth, glow, gem, pupil, glint) laid on the rounded cube via an unfold map (s along the front and round the vertical edges, t up), 0.016-0.032 off the surface plus ~0.036 where body edge rails stand proud (wide grins vanished under the rails first). 100-450 tris each, razor-sharp at any distance, recolourable in Studio (3d-designer, 2026-10-25)
- 2026-10-25 [build-a-pet] Shared Studio: Play sessions from other agents block Edit for long stretches and the first screen_capture after someone else's returns their camera. Queue Edit installs as ready-to-run files on disk, read state from the Server DM meanwhile, capture twice (3d-designer, 2026-10-25)
- 2026-10-25 [build-a-pet] Console "Failed to load sound rbxassetid://1" came from a Sound inside a shelved store model in ServerStorage (AssetLibrary): placeholder-id Sounds load even there. Strip Sounds (not just Scripts) when shelving store decor (vfx-artist, 2026-10-25)
- 2026-10-25 [build-a-pet] Repo disk copies can be NEWER than Studio (a reopened place lost unsynced pushes): before pushing a module, POST the Studio Source to a local server, diff both ways, merge the other agents' hunks, then push with a 'Studio unchanged since my snapshot' check (vfx-artist, 2026-10-25)
- 2026-10-25 [build-a-pet] Periodic shine on any mesh with zero particles: a Sheen Texture per face, StudsPerTileU ~2.2x the piece, hidden (T=1) except a 0.55 s window every ~3 s where OffsetStudsU slides one tile; one seed per item so all pieces flash together (vfx-artist, 2026-10-25)
- 2026-10-25 [build-a-pet] Articulated wings: rigid pieces split by planar cuts open visible seams as soon as a joint bends (the seam is a long line, the hinge only a point). A skinned mesh (Blender armature Root at origin -> 3 bones per side, smoothstep weight blends +-0.28 studs, <= 2 influences) uploaded through Open Cloud kept its Bones and bends with no gaps; drive Bone.Transform = q^-1 * R * q (q = bone rest rotation in body space). Pivot the root bone at mid height, not the top edge, or the downstroke swings the lower root into the body (lead-agent, 2026-10-25)
- 2026-10-25 [build-a-pet] Open Cloud glTF import: `CFrame.Angles(0, pi, 0) * part.CFrame` already restores the original geometry AND orientation; mapping it onto a live asset with a full CFrame (orig * ref^-1) adds a second 180 turn (mesh landed 4.58 studs off). Use a translation only and assert the centre matches (0.0000) (lead-agent, 2026-10-25)
- 2026-10-25 [build-a-pet] Pet models replicate to the client in pieces: a rig built on the first frame saw only 1 of 6 wing pieces. Any client rig builder must re-scan (debounced 0.3 s) on DescendantAdded/Removing, ignoring its own joint names (lead-agent, 2026-10-25)
- 2026-10-25 [build-a-pet] Shared Play lock: another agent overwrote my fresh lock 6 s after I wrote it while I was already in Play. Before start_stop_play re-read the lock and compare the timestamp you wrote (lead-agent, 2026-10-25)
- 2026-10-25 [build-a-pet] Model:ScaleTo costs ~0.19 ms on a 23-part pet (PivotTo 0.017): continuous breathing via ScaleTo took 6 pets from 0.47 to 1.1 ms/frame. Throttle breathing-only ScaleTo to ~8 Hz per pet (staggered, near the camera only), full rate only while a squash/reaction plays: 0.78 ms. Measure with a self-timing attribute (PetFollowMs) and the VFXQuality Low toggle (lead-agent, 2026-10-25)
- 2026-10-25 [build-a-pet] Legacy accessory meshes span BOTH sides in one MeshPart per material (Wings_DragonMembrane_Base at x 0): a rigid mirrored flap is impossible; split by side only when part centres sit off-axis, else "pump" (lift + pitch) in the flap rhythm. Meta ModuleScripts are LuaSourceContainers: test `BaseScript and Enabled` to detect an item's own animation. Name-based spin detection must cut the item's own name first (TwinProps_Metal = whole wing pair, not a propeller) (lead-agent, 2026-10-25)
- 2026-10-25 [build-a-pet] Client-side rig for a welded accessory: disable its body WeldConstraint, re-weld the group to its biggest part, one Motor6D body->root at the hinge (body-space bbox inner edge), drive Transform; joint error stayed 0.00002 stud over 3 s on 6 pets. Pets replicated into Workspace may not be settled on the first frame: re-scan a few times (0.6 s) while no group was found (lead-agent, 2026-10-25)
- 2026-10-23 [pet-sim] gpt-image with -Ref saved the reference instead of the result: ChatGPT re-renders the attachment as `alt="User attachment"` outside any message-role element. Fixed in `.app/roblox-app/gpt-image.mjs` (skip user attachments, prefer `data-testid*="generated-image"`). Always hash-check queue outputs; a result in < 25 s is suspicious.
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Retiring a progression mechanic (fusion tiers) without losing items: migrate the old field into the new one on the SAME item (FusionTier Gold/Circuit/Matrix -> Skin of that id), keep ids, and also run the conversion in the every-load repair so stale copies (trade prepare records) convert too. Rebuild derived books (collection) with an add-only backfill so repeated loads never double count; test idempotency by migrating the result twice.
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Passive income ticking every second must not trigger a full data snapshot each tick: update the currency attribute live (TokenService.Add NoSync) and MarkDirty at most every ~10 s from income alone.
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Gameplay tags on CYLINDER parts break pad logic: Shape=Cylinder is X-up, so scripts that read part.CFrame/Size with Y = up (OnPad footprint, padTop, Attachment(0,3,0) prompts) point sideways. Tag an invisible upright Box (CanCollide off, CanTouch on) at the walk surface instead (lib.luau `L.padTag`, world-v7).
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Several agents share ONE Studio camera: a capture right after setting CFrame often shows another agent's view (or a desktop window over Studio - delete those grabs, they leak private UI). Hold the camera with a 6 s Heartbeat loop in the same execute_luau call, grab with -Delay 1500, and take the Play lock for Edit builds too (others start Play the moment it is free).
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Game scripts can't read `.Source` (needs PluginOrOpenCloud capability): a test module that checks other scripts' source must pcall it and skip in Play (run those checks from Edit). Edit tests of NEW Config children need a fresh `ReplicatedStorage.Shared:Clone()` passed in (Edit require() keeps the old Config table without the new child).
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Pull-then-push sync: POST every Studio Source to a tiny local python server first (fresh truth on disk, md5 vs disk copies), then push with a drift guard = Studio length must equal the pulled length. Caught that disk==Studio for all my files and that another agent was live-editing ItemVisuals (left alone). Python heredocs in the Bash tool die on some quote mixes: write patch scripts to files with a small replace-exactly-once helper.
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Screenshots while lyopz types: grab Roblox Studio with PrintWindow(hwnd, hdc, 2 = PW_RENDERFULLCONTENT) (build-a-pet `_tools/studio_grab_bg.ps1`, -Count/-Every for sequences). It never brings Studio forward or steals focus; the older SetForegroundWindow grab scripts broke into his typing. Contact-sheet the frames with PIL to review a 6 s reveal in one image.
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] (lead-agent) Edit-datamodel work (inserts, Source writes) is impossible while ANY agent has Studio in Play: check get_studio_state right before each Edit call, not just the lock file (agents start Play seconds after a lock frees). Effects tagged for ItemVisualsController's cull also get Rate x0.5 past half the cull distance: test captures from > 40 studs look half as dense as in game.
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Never send the Play-lock check and start_stop_play in the SAME parallel tool batch: the start runs even when the check shows another agent's lock (it did once; stopped it within seconds). Check the lock, read the result, then start Play.
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Shared UI kit: one Components module (Panel/Button/Tab/Card/Pill/PriceTag/Countdown/CurrencyCounter/RarityFrame/Toast/Icon) + a Restyle(group) pass over the old builder panels (navy outline 5 + inner accent rim + navy pill outline) unifies a big legacy UI without rebuilding it; icons resolve v7 logo (UI.IconsV7, generated from asset-ids.json) -> v3 icon -> glyph, so logos can land later.
- (map-builder, 2026-10-25) [build-a-pet] A big rim-lip cylinder can be the real visible floor (Scrap City: RimLip top 150.2 over Deck 150.0): raycast with CanQuery parts before laying floor tiles, or they render hidden under it.
- (map-builder, 2026-10-25) Stairs off a floating deck must start OUTSIDE the deck radius (the deck cylinder is solid below its top); hang the steps outside the rim band with stringers + invisible side walls.
- (map-builder, 2026-10-25) Studio daylight lifts dark rock tones a lot (#5B4A42 read pale pink-brown) and a strong orange PointLight (range 26) turns nearby slate slabs pink: use ~#2C2321-#4A3A34 for basalt and keep decorative heat lights at range <= 14, brightness 1.
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Ultra-rare tiers (1 in 500k .. 1 in 350M): don't put them in the float weight table. Give each tier a FIXED chance in integer units of one denominator that divides evenly (3.5e9 -> 7000/700/70/10 units), draw one Random:NextInteger(0, D-1) per roll (int64-safe in Roblox), rarest block first, then roll the normal pool with integer weights for the remainder. Cap luck per tier (CapOneIn) and test exact block boundaries with forced draws; a 10M-roll Edit check runs in ~4 s with task.wait every 250k.
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Python patch scripts: never cut a section with s.index('return X') - it matched an earlier 'return X.Aliases[...]' and duplicated half the module. Use rindex for the final return, and compile-check (loadstring) every file in Studio before writing any of them.
- 2026-10-25 [pet-sim] Shop items that grant a roll (egg/crate) or an item: check delivery (storage space, item exists, shop-only flag) BEFORE taking Tokens, run the grant in pcall and refund on error, return the reveal result in the remote reply and still fire the normal HatchResult/CrateResult so the existing reveal plays.
- (lyopz, 2026-10-25) MCP `screen_capture` in Play can return only the UI over a black 3D view; a DPI-aware PowerShell window grab of Studio (SetForegroundWindow + CopyFromScreen) works.
- (lyopz, 2026-10-25) Additive (LightEmission 1) particles vanish in bright daylight; saturated colours with LightEmission ~0.3 and Brightness ~2 read clearly.
- (lyopz, 2026-10-25) Multi-material skinned GLB via Open Cloud: bones land under a separate RootPart joined by Motor6Ds; if those get swapped for WeldConstraints the meshes stop deforming. Clone the bone tree under EACH MeshPart and drive every copy per joint.
- (lyopz, 2026-10-25) Decimate before weighting (Blender Decimate COLLAPSE, use_symmetry X keeps L/R mirrored). Wing clearance scans should sample from the body wall outwards; AABB corners inside the body lock the root joint.
- (lyopz, 2026-10-25) MCP Client execute_luau has no HttpService/loadstring: fetch source on the Server, write it into a ModuleScript ("return function(...) <src> end") and require it on the Client.
- (lyopz, 2026-10-25) gpt-image picture queues die on a session restart: keep an `_order.txt` + `INDEX.md` and resume from the first missing name. Neon objects need the "pure white background, no dark backdrop" line up front or they come back on black.
- (lyopz, 2026-10-25) MCP `user_mouse_input` with `instance_path` hits the right button; hand-computed coordinates miss after a re-layout.
- (lyopz, 2026-10-25) Phone root UIScale (~0.6-0.7) shrinks offset buttons below 44 px: grow them to ceil(46/k) (k = AbsoluteSize/Offset). Never put a scale-sized child into an AutomaticSize parent with a UIListLayout (grows forever).
- (lyopz, 2026-10-25) Open Cloud Decal uploads (roblox-upload.ps1) give decal ids that fail in ImageLabels; use Studio `upload_image` for UI images.
- (Codex, 2026-10-25) Uniform PBR PNGs uploaded as Decal still need their underlying image id: in Edit, LoadAsset(decalId), read Decal.Texture, destroy the temporary container, then use that image in SurfaceAppearance. A fresh Play console is the render check; Edit PreloadAsync can fail even for working existing maps.
- (Codex, 2026-10-25) Streaming-safe intrinsic FX: resolve named/role-tagged endpoints at bounded intervals from the existing caller Step, disable immediately when an endpoint leaves the body, and reuse the same two beams/light after recovery. Test Attach-before-parts, removal, replacement, and zero-particle reservations; fresh module tests must explicitly accept a cloned dependency instead of reusing require's old cached module.
- (Codex, 2026-10-25) Prove GLB motion-coordinate conversion against actual imported shaft centres before applying a formula from a recipe. This body's measured import used(-Blender.x, Blender.z,+Blender.y); the old last-axis negation made gears orbit offset pivots. Then sweep real source vertices through the actual runtime driver: a stretched circular rotor can fit at rest yet rotate through its pocket wall. Fix its cross-section, not the clearance test.
- 2026-10-25 [general, Codex] CollectionService tags can reach a client before model descendants: wait for the authored animation targets before binding and use Atomic streaming for compact animated assets; isolated module tests do not catch this replication-order race. Verify a fresh real Play after fixing it (build-a-pet/models/volcanic-reactor-egg/VolcanicEggReview.client.lua).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Codex] Unity Mathf.SmoothStep(from,to,t) interpolates endpoints; it is not GLSL smoothstep(edge0,edge1,x). For a spatial threshold normalize/clamp t=(x-edge0)/(edge1-edge0), then t*t*(3-2*t). Test falloff bounds before using it for deformations or emissive masks; negative falloff can expand an intended recess (build-a-pet/models/volcanic-reactor-egg/prepare-v3.py).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude] Cracked/plated shells: a dark recess under a crack reads as a HOLE even when the inner mesh is closed. Put the glowing fill layer right under the plates (no deeper than plate thickness) and make the crack walls carry the glow (own UVs + gradient + emissive mask). Topology tests are not visual acceptance; judge a Studio screenshot.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude] Procedural mesh with a pushed-in region (bowl/dent): decide triangle winding from the undeformed surface, not from "outward from centre" on the deformed one, or triangles in the dent flip and get culled (see-through holes).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude] Studio minimized -> screen_capture returns a black 3D view with UI only. Restore the window (ShowWindow SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE=4, no focus steal) before capturing.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude] Long Unity C# generators: write them as an editor script (Assets/Degen/Editor/*.cs, static Run()) and invoke via reflection from execute_code, instead of pasting 20 KB method bodies. Read the console after import: CS0136 name clashes don't show in execute_code.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Beam UV/geometry facts tested in Studio: image X runs ACROSS the width, Y along the length (Y increases toward Attachment1); TextureMode Stretch = TextureLength is a repeat COUNT, Wrap = studs per tile; a FaceCamera=false Beam spreads its width along the attachments' Y axis (so for a ribbon lying flat on a surface: X = flow tangent, Y = in-surface side vector, not the normal); Bezier control points are A0 + CurveSize0*A0.Axis and A1 - CurveSize1*A1.Axis. Fit crack polylines with adaptive cubic spans (grow while max deviation < ~0.14 studs) to fit a beam budget.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Studio Edit captures: ParticleEmitters and Trails never show, and Beam TextureSpeed scroll time jumps between captures (can't read flow direction from stills): verify particles and scroll direction in Play. Egg meshes ship CanQuery=false: raycasts against them need a local CanQuery toggle (restore after).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] A state machine may call SetIntensity every frame: VFX modules must ignore tiny changes (< 0.05) and only change Beam.TextureSpeed on coarse steps, because every TextureSpeed write jumps the scroll.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Irregular plated shells without merging cells: a weighted "max-dot" spherical Voronoi (owner = argmax dot(d, w_i*s_i)) keeps great-circle edges and convex cells, so the plain triple/convex-hull corner search still works with q_i = w_i*s_i. Weights 1.07 for 4-5 big seeds (clear ~40 deg around them first) vs ~0.98 give big/mid/small plates. Drop cells with inradius < 0.12 rad and re-solve, or slivers appear. Probe a few opening directions: a cell with a near-zero edge reads as a box window (build-a-pet/models/volcanic-reactor-egg/v5).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Open Cloud GLB from DegenPolish.ExportForRoblox imports in metres, and ScaleTo scales about the imported bounding-box centre, so the bottom lands at -height/2. After ScaleTo(1/0.28), TranslateBy so the bbox bottom centre is at the origin BEFORE placing any attachment by world position. Measured mapping: Roblox = (Unity x, y, -z), in studs.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Moving shell plates: verify instead of guessing. Push every vertex out by MaxOffset along the plate normal, and separately rock it about the hinge, then require each displaced vertex to stay inside the plate's own Voronoi cell (margin > 0). Shrink the rock angle until it passes (1 of 8 plates needed 2.6 instead of 6 deg). Floating fragments: never place them on corners owned by a moving plate.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude reviewer] Beam TextureSpeed direction, tested in Play with a vertical test beam (A0 top, A1 bottom, star texture, Wrap): a POSITIVE TextureSpeed scrolls the texture from Attachment0 toward Attachment1. So for lava, put A0 uphill, A1 downhill and keep the speed positive.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude reviewer] Play-mode review shots without MCP capture lag: a client BindToRenderStep camera that reads a CFrame attribute on LocalPlayer, then grab the Studio window with PrintWindow(hwnd, hdc, 2) in ONE PowerShell process (a loop at 200 ms catches a 2 s hatch). In PowerShell, crop params named $W/$H silently collide with $w/$h locals (names are case-insensitive).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Floating pieces that a controller animates: check clearance against the REAL motion, not only the rest pose. EggController spins fragments about world Y through their centre, so a shard lying tangent on a sloped shoulder sweeps its low edge into the shell. Lean the shard toward +Y, test 24 spin angles, and measure against sampled shell triangles (vertices, centroid, edge midpoints) plus every moving plate at rest and at both limits. A coarse "max shell radius" envelope overstates the gap needed by about 2x.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Crack/flow channels from graph paths: a downhill Dijkstra with a soft rise penalty (6x) still allows small rises, and a 2-edge branch can form a V (up then down). Use a high rise penalty (80) for a hero path. Make a branch's 2nd edge keep the 1st edge's direction with a margin, and cut any branch that a later major path runs through, or the VFX gets doubled beams. Then assert in the build that every exported polyline drops at every step.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude scripter] `CFrame:ToAxisAngle()` near identity returns ~5e-4..3e-3 rad of float noise (acos of the trace): clamp tests that ask "is this part rotating?" must use atan2(|skew|/2, (trace-1)/2) from GetComponents. Also label sweep samples by the state that COMPUTED the pose: the step that auto-transitions already reports the next state.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude scripter] Edit-mode tests can read real mesh vertices: `AssetService:CreateEditableMeshAsync(Content.fromUri(part.MeshId))`, scale each GetPosition by Size/MeshSize. Adjacent moving shell plates: find the best separating axis at rest (fibonacci sphere), then per pose test that axis and its rotated copies; a pair sharing a hinge with opposed axes stays clash-free if the follower copies the leader's slide fraction and the same WORLD rotation.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude ui-designer] Shaped (non-rectangular) Roblox buttons without hand-painting: draw each layer as SVG (body / glow / badge, no text), render with Edge headless `--default-background-color=00000000 --screenshot` at 2x for transparent PNGs, upload with Studio `upload_image` from a local `python -m http.server`, then stack ImageLabels + TextLabels. The same SVGs drive the HTML mockup, so mockup = in-game art. Don't pass `--remote-debugging-port` to a one-shot headless screenshot: Edge never exits (build-a-pet/ui/volcanic-egg-prompt/render.ps1).
- 2026-10-26 [pet-sim, Claude ui-designer] Custom proximity UI per station: create the ProximityPrompt on the CLIENT (Style Custom, AlwaysShow) and drive a ScreenGui from PromptShown/PromptHidden; bind the extra counts (R/T, ButtonY/RB) with ContextActionService only while shown. Disable (locally) any other prompt under that model, or the default grey prompt still appears. Animated "elemental" button art = a second copy of the glow layer tinted near-white with a moving UIGradient transparency band.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Matching companion props (pedestal for an egg): reuse the hero's texture ATLAS files as-is (same UV layout: rock tiles, bevel strips, crack-class wall strips) instead of regenerating, so the hero's uploaded emissive-mask image id works unchanged and colours match pixel for pixel; only the new lava map needed an upload. Plated non-spherical shapes: a periodic 2D Voronoi (Sutherland-Hodgman half-plane clipping, labelled edges) on the unrolled surface (azimuth x arc length) + per-cell local metric gives the same plate look (build-a-pet/models/volcanic-egg-pedestal).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Rotating sub-parts (counter-rotating rim, spinning pool): keep their boundary to the static body a horizontal circle with a vertical gap (>= 0.09 stud), and put a rotationally symmetric lava skirt under the gap, so ANY angle is clash-free and the seam glows instead of showing a hole. Floating chunks: verify under a stated motion contract (orbit together + own spin + bob) and write the contract on the parts; independent orbit speeds would need separate radial bands.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Open Cloud GLBs whose bbox is not centred on the rotation axis: in the installer, centre X/Z on a part that is exactly symmetric about the axis (the round pool) and only the Y on the bbox bottom; then check one off-axis part against parts.json to catch a 180-degree import turn.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Hiding a part another script keeps writing (Transparency every frame): set LocalTransparencyModifier = 1 on the original and animate a client clone as its stand-in (opaque, emissive ramp, Neon swap, shrink); restore LTM = 0 on finish AND in teardown. Tested in Play on SurfaceAppearance MeshParts.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Beam/flare budgets: count Beams + Trails together against one cap and test it by counting Enabled Beams/Trails under the effect root every frame through idle, crack and eruption (h:LiveBeams()). Flares as FaceCamera Beams need ~0.3-0.6 stud width and LightEmission ~0.3 to read in daylight; 0.16 wide additive ones vanish.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Highlight as a reveal rim: outline only (FillTransparency >= 0.88); a stronger rarity fill visibly recolours the pet (navy body read tan). Rim + moved back light + a ZOffset -2 glow particle behind the pet separates a dark body.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude scripter] A NaN CFrame on any part of a StreamingEnabled Atomic model makes the client stream the whole model out and back (Parent nil, tags removed/re-added, controllers rebind). Source here: `jolt.t = math.huge` then `exp(-k*inf) * sin(inf)` = 0 * NaN. Never use inf as a "long ago" timer; guard the final pose (x ~= x) and test that every part stays finite through every state.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude scripter] Luau: a `local` declared BELOW a function that uses it makes that function read/write a GLOBAL of the same name (silent bug: a per-request setting never reached the handler). Declare shared module state at the top; a test can scan getfenv(moduleFn) for written globals (ignore script/_G/shared).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude scripter] A lazily created RemoteEvent (server makes it on first use) loses its first FireClient to whichever client listener connects first (the queue drains to one connection). Create it at startup, or have the client trigger creation and hook it before the real request.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude ui-designer] Big Studio installs without pasting source into execute_luau: serve the generated install.lua with `python -m http.server` and in Edit run `loadstring(HttpService:GetAsync("http://127.0.0.1:<port>/install.lua", true))()` (works in the Edit DM; compile-check each script with loadstring first). Attachments for prompts near spinning/levitating props: parent a static Attachment to workspace.Terrain. Python one-line replaces that append a `-- comment` mid-line silently comment out the rest of a Luau table (a Size vanished -> 0x0 frame).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude art-director] Never fit a finished asset by non-uniform scale: a 0.45 tall scale turned a plated pedestal into a flat disc (bevels and rim plates squashed) and, because the installer had set PrimaryPart.PivotOffset = part.CFrame:Inverse(), the model pivot moved 1.36 studs under the floor, dragging a pivot-anchored UI prompt with it. Redesign at the new size; after any resize check GetPivot() against the authored point (build-a-pet/models/volcanic-egg-pedestal/v2/spec.md).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude art-director] Floating chunks around a low dish-shaped body: check clearance against the body radius at the chunk's HIGHEST bob point (Home y + half height + chunk bob + root bob), not at its centre height; on a flared profile that adds ~0.3 stud of required orbit distance.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude ui-designer] Studio `upload_image` can hand back a DECAL id: an ImageLabel with it logs "Asset type does not match requested type" and shows nothing. Resolve once in Edit with InsertService:LoadAsset(id) -> Decal.Texture (the image id) and store both ids in asset-ids.json (volcanic glow: decal 123039593952390 -> image 76091398327672).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude ui-designer] Smooth "pill" game buttons need no baked art: UICorner(0.5) + obsidian UIGradient body + UIStroke with its own rotating UIGradient (molten rim) + an inset pill with a transparency-gradient lava lip; the only raster is a white 9-slice halo behind the button (SliceScale = H/coreHeightPx, frame = (W+1.5H, 2H)). Lay out in explicit px per k instead of a UIScale so the 9-slice and strokes stay exact. Put halo + button in a transparent Slot (Sibling ZIndex: a child can't render behind its parent).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude ui-designer] Phone layout checks without the device emulator: let fit() read a `DebugViewport` Vector2 attribute on the ScreenGui and log AbsoluteSize per viewport (844x390, 390x844, 1024x768, 2560x1080). Keep HUD-avoidance (Arena button) on the REAL screen size or the panel lands on the button. Another agent's Play session may disable every PlayerGui for its shots: check ScreenGui.Enabled before trusting a blank capture.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] A "god ray" sunburst made of many big additive camera-facing sprites (18-23 rays, 20+ studs each, ~0.5 transparency) tints the WHOLE screen yellow-white in daylight: that was the "bland sunburst". Fewer narrow rays (5-6 per layer, length ~0.8-1.2x egg height, min transparency 0.52+, LightEmission 0.2) behind the subject + a contrasting dark smoke puff (same flame flipbook, dark colour, LightEmission 0) read as an explosion instead of a wash (vfx-artist, 2026-10-26)
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Ground shockwave rings: size them from the pedestal/base radius (diameter ~ base*2+0.8, grow to ~1.1-1.3x), never from the effect height; a 13-stud soft ring at a grazing camera angle smears across the whole floor. Find the base by NAME next to the subject (it may be rebuilt) and put the ring on its top surface (vfx-artist, 2026-10-26)
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] MCP execute_luau runs in its own VM: require() there gives a separate module instance, and the LocalScript a game actually runs may be a StarterPlayerScripts copy of a ReplicatedStorage "template" (patch both). Reach live client handles through a BindableFunction on LocalPlayer. Edit is the only DM with loadstring + HttpService for pushing big sources from a local http.server (vfx-artist, 2026-10-26)
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude 3d-designer] SurfaceAppearance.Color is a multiply that clamps at 1: it can only darken, so it cannot warm a dark atlas (a (1,.74,.6) tint turned a navy-reading egg black). A dark grey-mauve albedo (62,54,56) + sky specular reads navy in Studio; re-bake the ColorMap warmer AND brighter (x1.9/1.22/1.0 -> (118,66,56)) and swap it by recreating the SA (copy maps, emissive, attributes). Companion pieces then load the same image (build-a-pet/models/volcanic-reactor-egg/v6/textures/make_warm_atlas.py).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Clearance checks against a turning body: slice the real triangles (plane/edge intersections per height, radius is convex along a segment); taking each triangle's max radius over its whole height span gave a false FAIL (0.02 vs a real 0.35-0.43). Inward offsets along a normal that tilts down near a top seam rise above the seam (plate walls/lava poked 0.06 into a 0.08 ring gap): clamp offset points to the seam height (build-a-pet/models/volcanic-egg-pedestal/v2).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] Rate-based ParticleEmitters are thinned by the client's graphics level: a Rate 12 test emitter showed 1-2 particles in Studio Play, while :Emit(n) counts are kept. For an effect that must read (hatch ember column), emit by hand from the effect's step (acc += dt*rate; Emit(floor(acc))) and count it in the budget as before. Also check a "continuous" layer's real duration in Play: the column was computed from a short ToReveal and ran 0.8 s (vfx-artist, 2026-10-26)
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude vfx] FaceCamera Beam flares with a sparse tiling streak texture (Wrap) vanish in daylight at ~10 studs even at 0.5 stud width; a soft continuous core texture (Stretch) at Brightness 3 / LightEmission 0.35 plus a 1-particle hot flick at the root reads clearly. A gold additive glow disc (LightEmission 1) on top of a flipbook swirl flattens it into a "yellow target": put the glow behind (ZOffset < 0), orange, LightEmission ~0.35 (vfx-artist, 2026-10-26)
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude animator] One shared rhythm for a stacked display (egg + floating pedestal): drive every bob from the hero's integrated HEART clock (beatTime), not wall time, as cos((beat - swellPeak) * TAU / period) with periods in whole beats (egg 1 beat, pedestal 2, chunks 1 with a 0.6 s delay + 0.15 s ripple); hand the companion a clock function (SetClock(fn, owner), first egg wins, cleared on Destroy). Speed-ups (Buildup/Critical) then stay in sync with no pops; cap bob amplitude with period/idlePeriod (floor 0.5) so a fast heart does not jitter (build-a-pet VolcanicEggReview EggController/PedestalController).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude animator] "How high does it float" disputes: measure per column, not min/max of bounding boxes. Raycast down onto the base (include list) then up from that hit onto the hero (CanQuery toggled locally) over a polar grid; the pedestal's highest point (crown tips at r >= 1.85) is never under the egg, so "0.8 above the top" and "1.2-1.4 above the pool it hovers over" are both true. A model with no PrimaryPart moved by BulkMoveTo keeps its GetPivot(): sample a part's Position for bob timing.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude animator] Studio with several agents: Play gets restarted within seconds of being free, so multi_edit fails with "Edit datamodel is not available in Play mode". Keep each batch of edits self-consistent (fallbacks like `P.ChunkDelay or 0.6`), fire the edits FIRST when Edit frees up (state check costs the window), and run tests in the same window.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude reviewer] "Where is the prompt anchored" disputes: read the LIVE Source and also check where the UI is drawn. The Volcanic Open 1/4/8 panel is a bottom-docked ScreenGui (holder Position UDim2(0.5,0,1,-gap)); the world anchor only sets ProximityPrompt range, so it never sits on the pedestal and covers it at player view. An anchor computed from a SPINNING model's GetPivot().LookVector lands at a random side (3.9 studs off-axis), making the prompt range lopsided: anchor on the axis instead.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude reviewer] Colour-match checks vs a concept: crop plate patches in both and compare median HLS (PIL colorsys), not eyeballing. Example Egg V6 r2: concept plates L 0.10-0.20 hue 6-11; Studio plates L 0.19-0.27 hue 352-3 (lighter and pinker), so "too red/light" was measurable. PrintWindow(hwnd, hdc, 3) grabs need ~1.5 s of PowerShell start-up: arm a hatch with task.delay(3.5) on the client, then start the grab, or a 3x hatch (~2 s) finishes before frame 0.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude ui-designer] Screen-anchoring UI to a 3D object: WorldToViewportPoint is full-viewport px, GuiObject.AbsolutePosition is viewport px minus GuiService:GetGuiInset() (top bar 58 in Studio), and a DeviceSafeInsets ScreenGui's own AbsolutePosition can be negative (0,-58). Offset for a child = (vp - inset) - gui.AbsolutePosition. Hatch-aware hiding: a hatch passes through the egg's ambient states (Buildup/Critical) first, so once a hatch starts only "Idle" may bring the panel back. ProximityPrompts only show when the anchor is in the camera view, so range tests must face the camera at the anchor.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude 3d-designer] Matching a concept's dark warm colour in Studio daylight: measure median patches (concept vs Studio capture, same plate), not by eye. Under the blue sky ambient a dark albedo keeps ~70% of its blue but only ~38% of its red, so "neutral warm" textures render pink/magenta (hue 320-358). To land hue ~0-6 deg at L 0.125-0.155 (concept 6-11, L 0.10-0.20) the rock texture had to be (118,52,24) (V5 grey x 1.9/0.95/0.42). A freshly uploaded decal image can render WHITE in Studio (a -Force re-upload stayed white 15+ min): always re-capture after a swap and keep the last good id to fall back to.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude animator] "Choppy" egg opening with no frame spikes: a fixed 30 Hz BulkMoveTo step at 40-60 fps moves the egg every 2nd/3rd frame (uneven cadence). Step every rendered frame while any handle is busy (h._busy flag read by the shared scheduler), keep 30 Hz only for idle; measured egg moved on 125/128 frames. Pre-build the reveal body (clone + ScaleTo + emitters) at the press, hidden with LocalTransparencyModifier, and cache its BaseParts (no per-frame GetDescendants).
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude animator] Keep long music stings from outliving a short opening: track every one-shot (start time, base volume), fade stings over the last ~65% of Reveal, ring others <= 0.8 s, fade everything over Done and Stop all at Idle; always reset Volume to the authored value before :Play() so a faded Sound is not reused quiet. Verified by sampling IsPlaying+Volume every 0.25 s in Play.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude animator] Timed Play frame strips: screen_capture has ~1-1.5 s latency and returns a blank frame when Studio is not foreground. Use build-a-pet/_tools/studio_grabseq.ps1 (ShowWindow 3 + SetForegroundWindow, PIL grabs at a fixed interval, print wall time per frame) and log state changes in the client with DateTime.now().UnixTimestampMillis; match frames to states by wall clock. Patch scripts in Edit by fetching JSON old/new pairs from a local http.server (HttpEnabled on only for the fetch), compile-check with loadstring, then set Source.
- (vfx, 2026-10-26) Hitch-free hatch FX: build every emitter/beam/trail/Highlight/bed stand-in once at attach (a "hatch pool"), hatches only move + Emit + Enabled. Test it with a DescendantAdded counter that ignores already-known instances (reparenting a pooled light/SurfaceAppearance fires DescendantAdded too). Pooled BaseParts go in a folder under workspace.CurrentCamera, not inside the egg model (controllers that collect the model's parts as riders would grab them).
- (vfx, 2026-10-26) Fit burst lifetimes to the timing window from Config (toReveal = hatch*(1-Eruption)/speed) and clamp the floor to the window too: fixed minimum lifetimes (0.4 s) made a 3x hatch (reveal 0.15 s after eruption) leave particles 0.3 s over the pet. Log LastEmit + LastAlive in the burst ledger and test both against the reveal.
- (vfx, 2026-10-26) Frame-time checks in Studio Play: measure the effect's DELTA over an idle sample in the same session (Heartbeat dt + Stats.HeartbeatTimeMs + RenderCPUFrameTime), and also point the camera at the sky. Here idle was a flat ~29 ms (sky 28.9, egg on Low 29.2), later ~20-24 ms, so an absolute "<= 20 ms" target measured the PC/Studio, not the hatch; the hatch added <= +2-4 ms frame and +0.3 ms Heartbeat. Use MicroProfiler or a phone for absolute numbers.
- (vfx, 2026-10-26) A trail that turns on "after the first position update" reads 0 if a test samples it after one Step: count the PEAK of enabled trails over the whole run and check none are left on at the end (LavaFXTest rev 3).
- (vfx, 2026-10-26) execute_luau that sets CameraType Scriptable gets reset to Custom by the Studio Assistant when the call returns; set the camera inside a task.spawn thread that also runs the capture window, and restore HUD/character LTM in the same thread.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude] Rarity-dependent cosmetic odds (e.g. "Shiny + overlay way rarer on Secrets"): pass the BASE rarity into the one shared roll function and apply a config multiplier before luck, then the cap; the server row carries the exact "1 in N" from the same numbers (body odds with luck x present traits only) so UI never recomputes.
- 2026-10-26 [general, Claude] Testing edited services while another agent holds Play: clone Shared into ServerStorage under a test name, load the repo source into the clones (HttpGet from a localhost server), gsub the service's `WaitForChild("Shared")` to the clone, Init with mock services; nothing live is touched and Play-stop discards it. Check the port first: another agent's http.server on the same port answers 404 silently.

- 2026-10-27 · roblox-ui-designer · A weak-keyed table (`__mode="k"`) keyed by Instances drops entries while the Instance is still alive in the DataModel (no other Lua reference), so ProximityPrompts "owned" that way never drew. Mark ownership with an attribute (`PromptStyled`) or use a strong table cleaned on Destroying.
- 2026-10-27 · roblox-ui-designer · Custom ProximityPrompt plates on one part: stack per UIOffset.X column, sorted by UIOffset.Y, each at max(own offset, previous + plate height + gap); stacking only "same UIOffset" overlaps prompts that are offset a little (View Odds 72 vs See items lifted 68).

- 2026-10-27 · roblox-art-director · Before a long ChatGPT picture queue, run one short picture first: a signed-out ChatGPT only fails after the full 15-minute timeout ("message box not found"), and a queue repeats that for every item.
- 2026-10-27 · roblox-art-director · A Bash heredoc whose body has apostrophes can fail with "unexpected EOF"; write script files with the Write tool instead.
- 2026-10-27 · lead · Remodel an island by moving the island folder's CHILDREN to a ServerStorage archive, never the folder itself: IslandService/MovementService find the island by Workspace.World7.<IslandId>.

To set a gap, put the Root.Mount Y at visible-bottom (relative to Root) + slot offset; to shrink a ring, use `model:ScaleTo(s)` and store the pre-scale MountOffset (= post-scale value / s), because ScaleTo scales attachments too.
- 2026-10-27 · roblox-modeler · A Studio-only fix is undone by the next reinstall. Write the same values into the generator (one idempotent `apply_<fix>.py` that edits item.json / install_cfg.lua and regenerates the installers), and chain it from the older generator so a rerun can't bring back the old colours.
- 2026-10-27 · roblox-modeler · Glowing slits or bars on ears and horns read as a second pair of eyes from the play camera. Make them dark SmoothPlastic (e.g. 23272F) and keep glow only for small beads or tips.
- 2026-10-27 · roblox-art-director (pet consistency) · A missing body model silently falls back to the plain BaseCube, which looks like "a pet". Check every catalogued body's bounding box and part count in a lab row, not just the item list. A screen_capture looking toward +Z flips left and right, so label the shots. Space lab rows further apart than the camera distance. Take shots at FOV 40 with ShowDevelopmentGui off, then restore both.
- 2026-10-27 · roblox-vfx-artist (pet consistency) · To freeze an attack frame in Edit: run CombatFX.Preview with a long Recovery, set the CombatFXFreeze attribute and TimeScale 0, take the screenshot in the NEXT tool call, then clear CombatFXLive. Keep the cloned router parented during Preview; otherwise it silently plays the generic impact. A Back ease overshoots about 10%, so clamp the final size to the cap.
- 2026-10-27 · roblox-vfx-artist (pet consistency) · Hover pets break "raycast to the floor = airborne" checks for ground effects. Seat the effect on the mount Root and fade only when the pet is well above its tracked rest gap. With StreamingEnabled, look up the pet body lazily: it can stream in after the mount.
- 2026-10-27 · roblox-vfx-artist (pet consistency) · Long execute_luau calls can answer "Target is not reachable" while the code is still running. Run them in task.spawn, write results to _G or StringValues, then read them back.
- 2026-10-27 · lead (pet consistency) · Compare the spec palette with the COOKBOOK palette before tuning an element's effects. Scrap disagreed (cyan-led vs bronze-led), and the spec wins: bronze/brass first with a white-hot core, cyan only as a rim. Two fix agents running in the same lab will overwrite each other's lab copies, so keep every fix in the source asset and re-clone it into the lab before shots.
- 2026-10-27 · roblox-reviewer (pet consistency) · A command-bar/MCP `require` of a ModuleScript whose Source was edited this session can return the stale cached version (ScrapAccessoryFX built the old flat-octagon Dynamo). For previews, clone the module beside the original under a new name, require the clone, then destroy it. ScrapAccessoryFX rigs also hide (parent nil) when the camera is more than 90 studs away, so frame the camera before attaching or measuring.

- 2026-10-27 · roblox-modeler (pet consistency w3) · On a tilted halo ring, the tilt adds about 2·R·sin(tilt) to its height (R 0.98 at 8° = +0.27). Cut the tilt before cutting towers when a halo is too tall. TeslaCrown went 1.08 → 0.49 tall and 2,976 → 1,468 tris mostly from tilt 8°→3°, an 18-segment ring, flat coils and dropping the rivets.
- 2026-10-27 · roblox-modeler (pet consistency w3) · Paint panel plates and seams into the colour map from a 3D signed-distance plate function on the bake positions (skirt, side, back, top). That gives exact coverage numbers (39% gunmetal) and a thin seam line (exp(-(d/0.014)^2)) on every edge, with no new geometry. Under a sunset ClockTime, bronze needs a mid tone around 6E3A1F to read bronze instead of orange.
- 2026-10-27 · roblox-modeler (pet consistency w3) · screen_capture with camera_position/look_at can return a frame from the old camera. Set workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame in execute_luau first, then capture with no camera args. Model attributes like `Core` live on the body model, not on the pet clone, so retint lab copies by matching SurfaceAppearance.ColorMap ids instead.

Steam that must never read as fire: white-alpha procedural wisps (numpy+PIL, free via roblox-upload.ps1), cream F2E3CF→copper C98B5A, transparency ~0.5, LightEmission ≤ 0.2, slow TextureSpeed (0.35-0.55).
- 2026-10-27 · roblox-vfx-artist (pet consistency f3) · Long Studio test modules (OverlayTests) time out an execute_luau call ("Target is not reachable"): run them in task.spawn, store the result in _G, read it in the next call. Separately, the ASCII .fbx and roblox.json that ExportForRoblox writes embed absolute paths, so scrub them to "~" before they land in the repo.

To check a tilt sign along a curve (teeth on a smile), use a straight front shot, because 3/4 perspective misleads.

- 2026-10-27 · roblox-modeler (scrap chests fix1) · Chest colour that reads from far: thick Neon seam band (.3 tall, proud of the box), coloured inlays on lid tops, glowing lines along brass lips; narrow teeth (.27 wide at .38 pitch) so the glow behind them shows. - Never run a Low-quality test on the pets lyopz can see: patch a cloned module's quality() on a duplicate pet only, and restore it in the same tool call (a leaked _G flag dropped every lab aura to Low mid-review).
- ParticleEmitter.TimeScale = 0 freezes particles mid-air: a cheap "time stop" moment.
- RGB afterimages: clone the pet Body (strip children + tags), Neon, weld; it cleans up with the host.
- A white PointLight ~2 blows out a white pet: tint it and keep ≤ 1.2 at rest.
- Braided ring strands: two extra beams on the same attachments with CurveSize x1.16 and x0.84. Curved beam ring segments: CurveSize = r * 4/3 * tan(pi/2n); fade the ends next to gaps.
- Emit glows from a centre Attachment, not a big host part (it scatters into bokeh blobs).
- Studio Edit `require` caches modules: test fresh code with module clones, and Edit previews need their own Heartbeat stepper.
- Pushing a big script from disk into Studio Edit: HttpService is off by default; toggle HttpEnabled on inside a pcall, GetAsync from a local `python -m http.server`, set Source, restore HttpEnabled in the same call.

A cloned welded part keeps its WeldConstraint to the ORIGINAL body, so PivotTo snaps it back: destroy joints first, and copy the source CFrame (rotation too) or the Front face points away.

Count Activate bursts in the live-particle cap (ambient rate x life + burst per eye).

- 2026-10-28 · roblox-vfx-artist · Timed FX tasks must pause on the freeze flag for freeze-frame shots. Beam Attachments must be parented before setting WorldPosition. A heredoc Python patch that dies with "unexpected EOF": write the script to a file and run `python -I`.
- 2026-10-28 · roblox-vfx-artist (eyes v2) · Mirror a sprite-sheet cell in an ImageLabel: ImageRectOffset = ((col+1)*cell, row*cell) with ImageRectSize = (-cell, cell). Per-part shiny: ItemVisuals parts()/ownEffect() must skip descendants of `model.Equipped`, or a shiny body recolours its eyes too. In Luau `x = a and f() or b` returns b whenever f() gives false/nil, even when a is true: use `if a then f() else b`.

Snap each spike to an exact sample or the cusp gets cut off.

The fix was moving the wing's leading edge to the front centre (theta 250-262°) and using more, narrower feathers (11 instead of 8), not making the shell bigger.
- 2026-10-28 · Blender writes the full .blend path (with the Windows username) into a PNG tEXt "File" chunk on every render: strip text chunks (or swap the name, same length) before committing renders. Also check roblox-assets.json, prompt logs and run_*.sh for home paths.
- 2026-10-28 · An in-app save of a file (e.g. MASTER-PROMPT) can overwrite edits Claude made since the app loaded it: re-grep after lyopz saves and re-apply.
- 2026-10-28 · App Update/Refresh copies the app from degen-roblox and overwrites local-only app fixes: fix in ~/BuildAPet first, then copy.
- 2026-10-28 · roblox-vfx-artist (auras round 4) · When cutting Lua with a script, cut to the start of the next statement, not the next newline, or multi-line statements leave broken leftovers.
- 2026-10-28 · roblox-vfx-artist (auras round 4) · A PointLight inside a pet body doesn't light its faces: offset it toward the camera. Neon brightens and shifts hues, so give it darker values.
- 2026-10-28 · roblox-vfx-artist (auras round 4) · Roblox recentres imported meshes: export a root marker and store offset + base size as attributes. Meshes using texture transparency ignore part Color: split into flat pieces and colour each. An inverted-hull SmoothPlastic piece gives a clean cel outline.
- 2026-10-28 · roblox-art-director · gpt-image.ps1: call it in-process (`& "$env:DEGEN_APP\gpt-image.ps1" ... -Prompt $p`) with the prompt read from a file and its double quotes removed. Nesting `powershell -NoProfile -File ... -Prompt "<text with quotes>"` splits the prompt and fails with "cannot convert to System.Int32" (it binds to -Minutes).
- 2026-10-28 · roblox-art-director · Cel fire concepts: "three flat colour bands with crisp edges + a 1.3x black-brown shadow flame behind each tongue like an ink outline" gave the right anime fire on the first try. A lone flame asked for "on white" came back RGBA with a soft dark glow instead of a hard partner, so show the dark partner on the full aura picture, not on a lone tongue.

Check high-tier bodies (8-15k) by hand until BudgetFor learns the tier. Skinning a shared base body: author the detail as separate meshes in body space (python + numpy is enough for plates/rivets/bands, 0 credits), test every vertex against the attachments.json keep-outs in the build script, and clone the base model in Studio so sockets stay identical.
- 2026-10-28 · roblox-3d-designer · Placing corner details on a rounded cube by elevation angle: pass the elevation away from the equator for bottom corners (sign once), never multiply the sign twice, or the bottom plates flip up onto the front bevel. A per-vertex keep-out check caught it.
- 2026-10-28 · roblox-modeler (cel fire kit, later archived) · Blender GLB imports land in Studio rotated 180°: fix with PivotOffset, not by rotating the mesh. SurfaceAppearance with emissive ~0.6 reads as glow without Neon's bloom blow-out. bmesh delaunay_2d_cdt from a 2D outline gives clean flat cel flame shapes; put the dark ink partner's offset in the notches. Preview kits on the standard body, never a new cube.
- 2026-10-28 · roblox-vfx-artist (Furnace Heart r5) · screen_capture is dark/black when Studio is minimized (restore it with ShowWindow 9 first). A stale AuraHost after code edits: AuraFX.Remove then Apply. Neon amber goes lemon-yellow under bloom: use F0761A. Hold a short bolt on for screenshots, it is only 2-3 frames live. Git Bash `sed -i` strips CRLF: edit CRLF files with python (newline=''). Studio lock file is `.degen/locks/Roblox_Studio.json`.

- 2026-10-28 · roblox-modeler (Scrap_RivetPlate fix2) · Corner plates on a rounded cube drawn as polar grids from the corner can't reach an arm that runs beside a keep-out (a ray from the corner crosses it): define the shape in 3D (disc + capsule arms, smooth min), take the last-inside point per ray, turn the ray origin toward the face the arm lives on, and clip each ray at the first keep-out hit; blur the radii 1-2-1 (only pulling in) or the plate shows creases.
