- 2026-10-28 · roblox-modeler (wings A) · WingAnimationController clamps a wing root that sits inside the body: keep every moving piece at local z >= 1.176 (behind the back face) and the limits open to +-62 deg; a frozen root scored motion 0 in review. Put glow drivers in a ModuleScript under the item (a Script/LocalScript there makes WAC skip it) and start them from ItemVisuals.PartFX.
- 2026-10-28 · roblox-modeler (wings A) · Studio HttpEnabled is off and other agents keep Studio in Play, so paste installers straight into execute_luau and batch several items in one call; write escaped Lua strings with the Edit tool, since a bash heredoc collapses `\t`.
- (reviewer, 2026-10-28) An FX ModuleScript inside an item does nothing until something calls it. Grep the client controllers for a caller (e.g. `PartFX`, `require(item.X)`) before scoring the effect, and score it as broken if there is no caller.
- (reviewer, 2026-10-28) Unclamping wing roots to +-62 caused new body clipping on multi-segment arms (Crane Arm1). After any change to the root range, re-run the clip sweep on all 3 bodies, with a per-wing clamp attribute ready.
- (reviewer, 2026-10-28) Lab pets bound with WAC.Bind are static, so they go straight to Hover. Use the Edit sweep (Move speed 16) to measure stroke travel, and Play only for FX and spins.
- (modeler wings A, 2026-10-28) WAC's per-type Amp sets both tip travel and tip lag: Mechanical (Amp 10) freezes short 1-segment wings at ~0.18 stud. Pick the WingType from a measured sweep (tip-Y travel + root-vs-seg cross-correlation lag), not by name. `WingDetail` on the item adds tip delay without changing the type.
- (modeler wings A, 2026-10-28) ItemVisualsController only watches workspace.Pets: lab copies under MapAssetsLab never get item hooks. To test a client hook in Play, clone the lab into workspace.Pets.
- (modeler wings A, 2026-10-28) WingAnimationController: WingDetail <= 1 locks seg 2 rigid to seg 1 (a hinged 2-leaf wing looks like one plate); d2 adds segment gain with no tip delay, d3+ adds TipDelay. Pick a wing's WingType from a measured tip-travel sweep (Move 16), not by name.
- (modeler wings A, 2026-10-28) Size-option masters: clone the live master in Edit and scale non-collar parts about each side's seg-1 WingHinge (Size, Position, child Attachment.Position, Beam width, light range x SC) = same result as the generator --big path, without pasting a 14k installer. For a big-vs-normal shot use a 140-stud camera at FOV ~5 so the nearer row isn't inflated by perspective.
- (lead, 2026-10-28) Studio screen_capture can come back with the HUD drawn but the 3D scene blank (dark grey, view cube frozen) when Studio is minimized/behind a window. Check once with ShowDevelopmentGui=false; if still blank, stop and leave the shots for later instead of looping. execute_luau also resets CameraType to Scriptable after it runs, so set Custom back at the end on purpose.
- (lead, 2026-10-28) A `.degen/locks/<tool>.json` older than the 3-minute hold is stale (a paused window left it): take it, don't wait.
- (reviewer, 2026-10-28) A chest's own coloured PointLight shifts the measured bronze hue (violet pulls it red, yellow pulls it gold): fix the light's strength or range before touching the shared palette, and mask brass/gold out before hue sampling.
- (lead, 2026-10-28) Posing models for Studio shots (lift neighbours out of frame, hinge lids open): save world pivots first, and on restore move the whole models back BEFORE putting sub-part pivots back, or the parts land offset. Check by a wide shot, not a formula.
- 2026-10-28 (lead): showcase.ps1 -Shots/-Files/-Concept paths are relative to the GAME folder (build-a-pet/), not the repo root; a wrong path is skipped silently and the Showcase entry has no pictures. Check the showcase folder has 1-*.jpg after saving.
- (scripter, 2026-10-28) A mesh crown's bounding box includes its hollow middle, so a box-overlap clip test counts anything inside the ring as clipping. Test "hidden == overlap" per item, not "always hides": a floating halo can already clear the head at its socket.
- (scripter, 2026-10-28) Repo .lua files can be CRLF: patch scripts normalise to LF and write back with the original ending; in a bash heredoc build CRLF with chr(13)+chr(10), not a literal 
- (scripter, 2026-10-28) Edit-mode shots: Studio's Assistant resets CameraType after each execute_luau; set Scriptable, capture, then set Fixed back; ShowDevelopmentGui off for clean shots and back on after.
- (scripter, 2026-10-28) Studio's hover outline shows in window screenshots: park the cursor in a screen corner before capturing ([System.Windows.Forms.Cursor]::Position = 5,5).
- 2026-10-28 (vfx, auras r7): with StreamingEnabled, cloning a far-away model on the client before it streams in gives an empty clone (auras build at 0,0,0); call `RequestStreamAroundAsync` and wait for its BaseParts first.
- 2026-10-28 (studio): push a big module in Edit mode by turning `HttpService.HttpEnabled` on, `GetAsync` from a local `python -m http.server` (127.0.0.1), `loadstring`-check, set `.Source`, turn Http back off. Studio stores LF, so compare hashes on LF-normalised text.
- 2026-10-28 (vfx): a Secret reads at distance from one big camera-facing 4-point sparkle (beams re-oriented in the camera plane each frame) and a bigger core glow, not from more particles; a solid gold mesh wheel out-reads thin neon rings at 16 studs, so check the ladder in one wide shot.
- 2026-10-28 wings v2 (modeler): Studio bloom blows an orange/lava SurfaceAppearance EmissiveStrength of 2.0 out to white; ~1.15 keeps the colour (cyan survives 2.4). To shoot without stealing focus, drive a client RenderStep camera from workspace attributes and grab with build-a-pet/_tools/studio_grab_bg.ps1; the shot lands about one grab later than you set it, so shoot one subject at a time (or rename after checking). Studio is shared: check get_studio_state before any Edit call, because another agent may have started Play.
- 2026-10-28 wings v2 (modeler): kit fit() scales by the tighter axis, so wings come out width-limited; get height from longer hanging plates. Flat (unbevelled) gears save ~60% of the tris for spinners. WingAnimationController caches SpinRate at Bind, so a "moment" can't speed spinners up; use beams (Belt* TextureSpeed) and bursts instead.
- 2026-10-28 · wings v2 · Install big accessory sets with a temp installer script and send only each item's params per call (avoids huge Studio calls); lab models need the anim bind + WingFX started by hand in play tests.
- 2026-10-28 · headgear v2 · Rounded-rect collar at a constant offset from the body bevel = one seat height, every crown sits flush (collar front y ≥ 1.05 keeps eyes clear). When Studio is stuck in someone's Play: build offline; read-only checks still work on the Server datamodel. loadstring is off in Play-server execute_luau: wrap test code in a temp ModuleScript and require it. A lab install lib + compact part helper cuts installer pastes 25-40%.
- 2026-10-28 · wings v3 (modeler) · Feathers/plates that are their own WingSeg segments lag (TipDelay), fold at detail >= 4 and slide along the parent (squash-stretch moves joints 2+ by Stretch x length): lyopz saw it as "feathers moving weird". Draw every feather into its bone's mesh part (one rigid piece per bone) and keep the chain to the real bones; per item, WingAnimationController now reads attrs `WingStretch` / `WingTipDelay` (0 / 0.03 = glued) without touching the shared preset.
- 2026-10-28 · wings v3 (modeler) · A slim collar (collar_lite, 392 vs 552 tris a side) frees ~320 tris for feather spines, clamps and bolts; a rarity reads as "different" from its neighbour through one material swap on the biggest part (Legendary gold arm vs Epic gunmetal), not more feathers.
- 2026-10-28 · auras r8 (vfx-artist) · A mix-and-match aura kit needs coverage as data (arcs of azimuth x latitude band x radius band per rarity) plus a per-level density multiplier; low tiers own a small arc but must fill it densely (LMUL 0.75 at Common, not 0.45) or they read as "nothing" at 3/4 and 16 studs. Put the Common/Uncommon arc on a flank (centre 90 deg), not behind the pet: a back arc hides behind the body in every front/3-4 shot.
- 2026-10-28 · auras r8 · Beam cores under ~0.06 studs read as hairlines at 10-16 studs: give the kit one LineWeight multiplier (2x) that every beam/trail width goes through. AuraTiers.CullDist 45 culls everything in a 58-stud wide shot: shoot ladders from < 40 studs. Rigs: recipe events must poll rig.choreo.phase in an updater (choreo is built after Build); Rate-0 emitters still :Emit() while disabled.
- 2026-10-28 · auras r8 · Studio capture with no focus: PrintWindow grabs synced to a wall clock (Luau DateTime.now().UnixTimestampMillis vs PowerShell [DateTimeOffset]::UtcNow.ToUnixTimeMilliseconds()) shoot a 20-pose camera sequence in one call; run 3 grabbers in parallel jobs at different offsets and keep the busiest frame (count of bright+saturated pixels) so periodic styles are caught mid-beat. The Studio lock pid is the Lead window's, so sibling subagents share it: check get_studio_state AND that your camera is live (another agent can stop/restart Play under you; check the frames show your lab).
- 2026-10-28 · auras r8 · Pushing module Source from disk to Studio Edit: a tiny local HTTP server (127.0.0.1) + HttpService:GetAsync with HttpEnabled flipped on for the call and restored after; verify by length/hash. Play only sees the new Source after a Play restart (or mutate exposed tables like CoverageLevels on the client copy and re-Apply).
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) Texture overlays on rounded MeshParts break at edges because each face projects separately: anchor U offsets along the perimeter, give Top a FaceBasis that continues Front (R=(-1,0,0), U=(0,0,1)), snap tile size to the body, and add motion as deltas to the anchored phase (never absolute offsets).
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) Play-server DataModel: loadstring is unavailable even with HttpEnabled; ship lab code as ModuleScript Source + require. Retag client-side labs so live controllers ignore them; another agent's RenderStep camera overrides screen_capture camera_position.
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) Shared Studio: batch capture runs (several execute_luau + screen_capture pairs in one message run in order) and drive time by hand in Edit; execute_luau needs datamodel_type.
- 2026-10-28 · ground v3 · Even-zigzag Neon floor cracks read as letters (N/Z/S): use mostly radial cracks, 3-4° wobble, taper, one branch. Floor glow marks that follow the item's moment light it for free (no particles). FX.Pulse during cooldown is a no-op (wait ~3 s). A Workspace client-RunContext Script doesn't run with streaming on: use a LocalScript in PlayerGui and move the camera via ReplicationFocus. A ground with zero pieces errors on build: keep one hidden icon piece.
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) To move a bespoke aura look down the ladder (Secret look → Common): keep its builder as a recipe `custom` + `lite` flag (drop the sky beam and camera grade), and raise the AuraTiers cap for that rarity too, or the tier throttle silently cuts it to ~8 particles.
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) Client-side labs in Play: the client streams out lab models it clones from, so set the source Model's ModelStreamingMode = Persistent on the server for that session only. Aura Recipes exposes palettes as `Recipes.Palettes` (not `.P`).
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) Two agents in one Play session both driving the camera means the other agent's step wins and every grab shows their lab. Check `_G` for another agent's camera keys (e.g. ORL_*) before a timed capture, and agree a handoff flag instead of overriding.
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) Shiny glass panes: a Neon inner core reads as a white blob; use a dark SmoothPlastic core (base lerped 55 % to black) carrying the skin motif so each pane is a window with parallax. Keep texture Color3 HDR glow ≤ ~1.45 or bloom blows it out.
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) Library skin particles (LibraryFX ApplySkinFX) leave the body: clamp after apply — speed ≤ 0.12, life ≤ 0.5, LockedToPart, host box ×0.8 (corners stick out of rounded bodies), Squash ≥ −0.3 and size ≤ 0.22 × host min (a Squash −1 line texture drew 10+ stud scanlines).
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) lyopz dislikes expanding ring / shockwave pulses as an overlay moment: use rising flares, crack flashes, aurora curtains or swirling stars instead.
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) Face clear on auras: don't just skip the front arc (it leaves a hard gap). Warp azimuths out of a ±30° window centred on a front angle that leans toward the camera (`ctx.clearAz` returns warped az + 0..1 fade) and fade beam width by that fade, so the 3/4 view keeps the face open without popping.
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) Beam budget per quality: count beams per handle at Build and Destroy support layers past a cap (keep layer 1 + its glow); guard every later handle call with `if not self.made` so destroyed handles can't error.
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) Lazy world-to-local caching bug: converting saved world points to host-local on the first use (not at attach) bakes in wherever the model moved meanwhile, giving bolts thousands of studs long. Convert right when the points are captured.
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) Falling-code overlays (Matrix): split the glyph rain into a dim trail texture and a bright "head" texture with the SAME Tile, ScrollV, FrameRate and Surge; the controller drives frames from global t x rate, so the heads stay locked on their trails. Three rain depths (tile 1.7 / 2.4 / 3.6, speed 0.38 / 1.05 / 0.7) read as real falling code.
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) Wear and tear on tiled Texture overlays: put grime / chips / scratches at ZIndex 1 under the pattern (Z 2+) with Random offsets, tinted from the base (scratch = lerp to white, grime = lerp to black); exclude Wear layers from any palette recolour pass (CoreFinishes.Coloured turned pale scratches blue).
- (vfx, 2026-10-28) lyopz "keep it so it stays up, same should go for all animations": a looping layer must never fade to Transparency 1 and come back. Surge/Pulse/Flicker layers rest at a floor (SkinOverlay.RestT 0.8) and brighten on top; only travelling glints may sit at 1 between passes.
- 2026-10-28 · showcase rebuild · For a System design in the Showcase, diff files/ against the game folder first (often already identical) and check HANDOFF for an approved twin slug; then the Studio job is only a hash check + its test module, no art waves. A Studio call that times out at 120 s means Studio is hung: stop calling it (each call refreshes the lock and blocks the other Lead windows).
- 2026-10-28 (Lead): running several test suites in ONE execute_luau call (OverlayTests + CosmeticRoll + CosmeticVisual) froze Studio (Not Responding, 1 core busy, MCP timeouts) and it had to be force-closed, losing the unsaved place. Run one suite per call, yield with task.wait() inside long suites, and get lyopz to Ctrl+S before any heavy run.
- 2026-10-28 (Lead): Color3:ToHex() on HDR colours (>1, e.g. glow-tinted Decal/Light colours) logs a warning WITH a stack trace on every call; a test signature loop did that about 3000 times and crashed Studio during Play shutdown (128 MB log). Use a clamp-free string.format of R/G/B*255 instead of ToHex in tests and signatures.
- (vfx, 2026-10-29) Moment strips in Play: launch the hidden grab script with T0 = now+9 s, then have the client busy-wait on DateTime.now().UnixTimestampMillis >= T0 and fire Recipes.Fire(rig,"big"). The frames line up within about 50 ms and you need no second MCP call. To check a Medium build, clone the model, strip its FX, set AuraQ=0.7 and Apply it.
- 2026-10-29 (modeler, wings v3): sky strips for shots: check workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(origin, 60) is empty first (0,400,3000 is taken by _V7_AssetPreview, use 0,400,4400). Shots in day light hide emissive bulbs/beams: give glow parts a light base colour so they read by day too.
- 2026-10-29 (vfx, overlays v2.3): a texture flipbook of a rotating log spiral reads as ZOOM, not spin (a log spiral turned looks like itself scaled). For a spin that reads: give the art rigid landmarks (a bar, knots, dust lanes, arm ends), keep every feature 2-fold symmetric so a 180 deg loop has no pop, use small steps (24 frames x 7.5 deg) and crossfade frame i -> i+1 with a twin Texture (FrameFade, weights min(1, 2(1-f)) / min(1, 2f)). Drop any Orbit wobble on spin layers. 24 frames at 256 px cost half the memory of 12 at 512.
- 2026-10-29 (vfx, overlays v2.3): a wear layer meant to sit ON a moving pattern must be cut from the same cells (same seed) and locked to that layer: same Tile/MinTile/ScrollU/ScrollV/Small, no Random on either, higher ZIndex. Shiny overrides are index-based: appending a Def layer means adding `{}` placeholders to the Shiny list up to its index.
- 2026-10-29 vfx (overlays v2.3): freshly uploaded image ids can render blank for ~1-3 min ("Asset content representation is being generated"). Before review shots, ContentProvider:PreloadAsync the new ids and re-shoot any frame whose status is Failure; a re-upload returns the same id, so just wait.
- 2026-10-29 (modeler, wings v4): Blender `-- --dir <relative>` scripts resolve against Blender's own cwd, so bake_wg_v2.py wrote textures and shots to C:\<dir> at the drive root while the item folder kept stale maps (the v3 uploads used v2 textures on v3 UVs). Always pass an absolute Windows path: `--dir "$(cygpath -w "$PWD/<dir>")"`, and check the map timestamps match the glb before uploading.
- 2026-10-29 (modeler, wings v4): "floppy" flap fix = WAC preset ScrapMech (item attr WingMotionType): Piston ease (rise, hold, slam, hold), TipDelay 0, Stretch 0, no Flutter/Figure8, SegGain ~1 per segment; per-item WingAmp/WingHz attrs instead of new presets. Stiff metal reads from the holds at the top and bottom, not from a smaller angle.
- 2026-10-29 (modeler, wings v4): a thin emissive strand bakes as fully high-AO, so an AO-driven "hot core" turns a lava drip cream/white. Drive the hot core from the up-facing shade instead (mostly orange, small yellow core, deep red in low-AO seams).
- (vfx-artist, 2026-10-29) Live showcase in Edit: a `task.spawn` loop with `RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()` started from execute_luau keeps running after the call returns (~60 fps), so texture animations can play live for lyopz; stop it with a `_G` flag and check the frame count stops changing before you destroy the lab. MCP `screen_capture` leaves `CurrentCamera.CameraType` as Scriptable, so set it back to Custom after any capture before you hand Studio to lyopz.
- 2026-10-29 (scripter): "Live ChestSpot chests" in this place are the tagged masters in ServerStorage.RemodelChests, not Workspace models (no ChestSpot chest is placed in the world yet). Check CollectionService:GetTagged + IsDescendantOf(workspace) before planning a "live" swap, and keep the V4 source folder as the clean copy (swap in clones).
- 2026-10-29 (scripter): Small Studio sync without an HTTP server: check the Studio Source is byte-equal to git HEAD (length + byte-sum, \r stripped), then splice only the changed tail in Studio and compare the same checksum against disk. Rarity-driven stats belong in one table pass at the end of the Config module, so content reshuffles only touch the Rarity field.
- 2026-10-29 (scripter): Queue waiters: "waiting (starts after line 6 is done)" contains the word "done"; match the status after the colon (': (done|skipped)') and include sub-lines like 7b.
- 2026-10-29 (roblox-modeler, wings v5): never call WingAnimationController.Pose in a per-frame loop. It rebuilds the controller from the CURRENT (posed) parts, so poses stack and the wings drift or jitter. For live shows use one WAC.new(model, {Mode="cframe"}) per pet plus c:Step(dt, {state="Hover"}) on Heartbeat (_G stop flag); Edit draws it.
- 2026-10-29 (roblox-modeler): a temp copy of WingAnimationController must sit two levels under RS.Shared (it waits for script.Parent.Parent.Config and script.Parent.AssetMeta). Put it in RS.Shared._tmp with FollowFeel + AssetMeta copies, or require() hangs forever and execute_luau times out.
- 2026-10-29 (roblox-modeler): a bird/dragon beat that reads = upstroke 40% (elbow + wrist fold back via yaw, tips trail), power downstroke 60% (spread, slightly forward), TipDelay ~0.06 per segment, about 1.8-2.0 s per beat, +0.16 stud body lift on the downstroke.
- 2026-10-29 (headgear agent): SetForegroundWindow on Studio can return false (Windows foreground lock). Tap Alt first (keybd_event 0x12 down/up), then call it again.
- 2026-10-29 (headgear v5): Always pass an absolute --out to headless Blender builds; a relative path makes `//` resolve to the drive root and leaves stray folders on C:\.
- 2026-10-29 (headgear v5): Never give two rarities the same ring/halo shape; each tier gets its own signature form, not a recolour.
- 2026-10-29 (headgear v5): When adding a variable to a multi-assign `local a, b, c = ...`, count names vs values; a missing value silently makes the last one nil.
- 2026-10-29 (headgear v5): Put a moving piece's beam attachment on a non-spinning part so the beam stays upright.
- 2026-10-29 (bodies show): BodyFX runs in Edit: require(Shared.BodyFX) then BodyFX.Attach(model, id, {NoLOD=true}); Heartbeat/RenderStepped fire in Edit. Re-attaching after PivotTo is safe.
- 2026-10-29 (bodies show): screen_capture with a camera position leaves CameraType Scriptable, so reset it to Custom. Workspace BillboardGuis don't render in Edit captures; use a SurfaceGui sign for labels.
- 2026-10-29: the Studio id can change between sessions (list_roblox_studios first); check which open place holds the assets.
- 2026-10-29 (egg show): EggHatchMotion runs in Edit: idle via ReadRig/Step/Apply on Heartbeat; hatch via Begin, Update through Anticipation/Energy/Shake/Open, Beat per shake beat, End. Reset by destroying the clone and cloning a fresh egg onto the seat.
- 2026-10-29: screen_capture needs capture_id as well as studio_id.
- 2026-10-29 (aura show): AuraFX runs in Edit: AuraFX.Apply(model, AuraLook) with model attr AuraQ=1 for High, and connect AuraFX.Step to RenderStepped yourself (auto-start only in a running client). ScrapAccessoryFX.Attach self-steps on Heartbeat in Edit; Workspace attr ScrapFXQuality="High" forces High.
- 2026-10-29: aura reviews must be shot within 45 studs (AuraFX.AnimDistance / AuraTiers.CullDist), or the row looks bare.
- 2026-10-29 (chest show): ChestOpenFX makes Workspace.ChestOpenFX_<Name> during an open; to capture mid-open, wait for that folder, aim, wait ~2.5 s. Its particles/beams render in Edit captures. Studio HUD shows in Edit captures, so keep the subject in the middle third.
- 2026-10-29 · roblox-art-director · gpt-image.mjs fails in ~60 s with "ChatGPT message box not found (signed out?)" when the shared Edge window sits on a Cloudflare "Just a moment..." check, not a sign-out. Poll `curl -s http://127.0.0.1:9333/json/list` until the page title is "ChatGPT" (cleared on its own in ~60 s), then rerun.
- 2026-10-29 · roblox-art-director · Giving a sibling egg its own look while matching the Example Egg's depth: a bare "no rivets, no gears, no clocks, no ribs, no metal plates" list made ChatGPT draw a smooth, sparse glass egg. What brought the density back without the banned motifs: a two-layer shell (thick overlapping petals over a frosted inner shell), "each a heavy slab you can see the side thickness of, overlapping like scales with a deep shadow gap", a new small repeat detail (moonstone beads) and stacked segments on the stand.
- 2026-10-29 (auras r12) Team Create Studio can be saved by someone else mid-job: keep install + teardown in one tight run. ScrapFXQuality is unset by default; set it only for a show and clear it after. A thick ribbon needs width along its whole length plus a scrolling strands layer; tapering from ~0 reads as a thin slash. The Read tool adds one leading tab (line-number tab) to copied lines; strip it before scripted find-replace.
- 2026-10-29 (chests v5) A chest personality that sets only S.doorOpen never opened (Kit.lua read only S.open; now reads the larger of both) - check new personalities against Kit. Studio screen capture lags seconds: capture a clone with a long Hold. Serving big installers from a localhost server (HttpEnabled on only during the fetch) beats pasting. Blender Python has no PIL: strip PNG metadata with system Python. Folded wings must be big and turned ~36 deg toward front to read; a symmetric feather logo reads as a hand/fan, make it asymmetric. Studio Kit and repo Kit.lua had drifted ~1.7 KB: reconcile.
- 2026-10-29 (luck spins, scripter) `_G.BAP` is NOT visible from MCP execute_luau (its own VM): run server tests by firing Remotes.DevCommand("<test>", "") from the Client DM, then poll LogService:GetLogHistory() from the Server DM for the "done:" line. A Lib test module edited during Play stays cached: stop Play, resync in Edit, Play again.
- 2026-10-29 (luck spins) Compare Studio Source to git HEAD with a hash that can't overflow doubles: FNV with `* 16777619` loses precision in Luau numbers; use `(x * 31 + byte) % 1000000007` on both sides (LF-normalised). Byte lengths matching is a quick first check.
- 2026-10-29 (luck spins) Testing "same args into ItemService": wrapping RollPetExtras catches InventoryService.AddPet's own later call too; take the first call after a mark and compare tables by JSONEncode, never by identity.
- 2026-10-29 (luck spins) MCP execute_luau can't see _G.BAP: fire Remotes.DevCommand from Client and read LogService:GetLogHistory() on Server; a test module edited during Play stays cached, so stop Play, resync in Edit, Play again. Hash Studio sources with (x*31+byte)%1000000007 (FNV overflows Luau numbers). Wrapping RollPetExtras also catches AddPet's later call: take the first call after a mark, compare with JSONEncode.
- 2026-10-29 (UI v2, ui-designer #2) Edge `--headless=new --window-size=W,H` gives a W-30 x H-95 viewport: open W+30 x H+95 and crop with PIL, or phone mocks lay out at the wrong size. At the 0.69 root UIScale, 64 design px = 44 real px: make every touch button 64. A child named "Name" can't be read as obj.Name (use FindFirstChild).
- 2026-10-29 (UI v2) Shop detail pages need ~1560x880 design px (1220x720 crams them); on phones stack the preview over the info and let the info scroll. Odds tables: nowrap + short numbers (2.5K / 10M / 1B) + a scroll fade. Size HUD counters from TextBounds, not AutomaticSize. A green price pill on a hero reads as a live Buy: use a neutral price tag while buying is off.
- 2026-10-29 (UI v2 sound) One game-sound SoundGroup (SoundService.GameSFX) driven by the saved UISoundVolume makes "mute all" one line: route every UI/opening/reel Sound's SoundGroup to it. Give a volume slider a 64 px invisible hit area around an 18 px bar, keep it non-Selectable (gamepad uses the - / + buttons), and leave half a knob of gap beside the buttons so the knob never covers them at 0% / 100%.
- 2026-10-29 (open counts) When the allowed counts change (1/3/8 -> 1/4/8/16), refuse unknown counts on the server (`Check` -> badcount / needpass + PassKey) instead of rounding; then grep every CLIENT caller that computes a count (auto-hatch "n = tokens / price", saved lastEgg.count) and snap it to an allowed value, or the old in-between counts (2, 3) silently stop auto loops.
- 2026-10-29 (open counts) A busy lock that scales with batch size is easiest as "busy-until" (`last = now + LockSeconds(count)`, check `now < last`) rather than "last time + fixed cooldown"; add a `_ClearLock` dev hook so tests can open back to back.
- 2026-10-29 (open counts) Editing a one-line Lua table call: never append a `-- note` in the middle of the line; everything after `--` (here `KeyboardKeyCode = ...`) silently becomes comment and the code still compiles.
- 2026-10-29 (art-director) gpt-image.ps1 breaks on multi-line prompts: use `node .app/roblox-app/gpt-image.mjs <promptFile> <out>`. Node ESM imports on Windows need `file:///` URLs (pathToFileURL), not bare C:\ paths. ChatGPT likes to add front-face details (faces, logos) to eggs and chests: say "no face, no emblem on the front" when you don't want one.
- 2026-10-29 (auras r9-r11) Higher rarity = more coverage around the body, not just brighter: Common gets a full band + focal piece, Secret owns the space. Keep the cube's front ~50 deg window clear at every level. Ribbons need real width along their whole length (see r12).
- 2026-10-29 (Phantom Egg v2, modeler) Headless Blender glow parts read as white paper cut-outs unless they fade at the edges + Glare bloom + AgX. A soft glow = solid emissive part + a ~1.28x see-through copy. Dark glass goes grey under a sunset key: add a faint self-glow in the glass hue. Compare egg sizes plinth-to-top, never lowest-point-to-top (a hanging spire makes it look taller).
- 2026-10-29 (pause) Scratchpad upload-id lists vanish on restart: copy them into the repo (bodies v4 ids now in models/bodies/v4-upload-ids.json).
- 2026-10-29 (opening reel v2) PrintWindow capture (flag 2) works with Studio covered but needs absolute output paths (else "generic error in GDI+"). Another agent's client script left in Workspace (GroundV2.LabClient) can hijack the Play camera; disable it first. ChestOpenFX's fallback uses Workspace.ScrapChestPreview chests: keep them current on every chest reinstall. Phone check: put the 1280x720 root in an 844x390 frame, UIScale = min(844/1280, 390/720), multiply text sizes by it (rule >= 14 px). Rarity-keyed colour tables need a fallback (Mythic vs Mythical crashed ChestReel:_winCard).
- 2026-10-29 Codex UI: template AutomaticSize and UITextSizeConstraint can override toast sizing. Measure text, bound width, disable those overrides, and verify actual rendered pixels after UIScale. Reel skip must lay out/recycle winner before locating its card; normalize rarity aliases and keep a colour fallback.
- 2026-10-29 Codex: archive inspection evidence establishes inventory, not UI behavior. Read images and sample embedded recordings before claiming a style study; distinguish remaining timer values from schedules, independent drop odds from normalized tables, and observed prices from recommendations. Source: build-a-pet/studies/rea-ui-design-ideas.md.

ChatGPT still prices in the item's own currency: state the price currency per card.

Length/compile-compare every module before Play, and keep API stubs for callers that exist only in Studio. A restyler that recolours light surfaces must skip Glass faces (`ReferenceFinish`), or gradient-tinted buttons go dark.

AbsoluteSize/AbsoluteWindowSize are real px: divide by the ancestor UIScale product before using them as offsets.
