- 2026-11-01 · roblox-ui-designer (shop r10) · Never let a white "sheen" frame rely on its UIGradient for transparency: other code can disable gradients and the frame draws solid white (the r9 "white hero card"). Give the frame its own BackgroundTransparency and let the gradient only fade it.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-ui-designer (shop r10) · To fit more cards per row without rebuilding them, keep the design-size card inside a grid cell frame and scale it with a UIScale; clamp the scale so the buy bar stays >= 44 px real (on phones the touch floor, not the width, sets the column count).
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter · An "Asset is not compliant" id found in no script, file or property is usually a hidden TexturePack: Studio auto-uploads one per SurfaceAppearance when its maps change, and it is rejected if any map was rejected. Find it with `SerializationService:SerializeInstancesAsync({sa})` and a byte search for the id; fix it by replacing the rejected map so Studio builds a new pack.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter · The Studio log (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\Roblox\logs\*_Studio_*_last.log`) shows more than Output: "load failed in fetchTexturePackTextures" means a SurfaceAppearance pack; "Rate limit exceeded for TexturePack uploads" shows when Studio auto-uploaded packs. Match failing ids to changes by timestamp.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter · Studio stops rendering while unfocused, so texture errors only appear once someone views it; an error-free Play with Studio in the background proves nothing. execute_luau shows only the first return value, so return one joined string.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-art-director (storms) · A 10-item lineup sheet in ONE ChatGPT picture works first try when the prompt gives two rows, a numbered line per item (material, shape, glow) and the exact label text for each; it shows a whole rarity ladder for one approval.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-art-director (storms) · ChatGPT puts cartoon faces on anything called "Phantom"/"ghost"/"soul"; write "no faces, no eyes" in the prompt, and in the model brief.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-art-director (storms) · gpt-image.mjs can hand back ANOTHER job's picture when two agents share lyopz's ChatGPT (a 35 s run gave someone else's egg, unique MD5, so the hash check missed it). Open every result before using it; rerun alone if it's wrong.
- (ui, 2026-11-01) Fit grid columns to the list's inner width: subtract the ScrollingFrame's UIPadding (left + right) and the scroll bar before dividing by the UI scale, or the last column overflows and the layout drops back to one fewer column. Let a mouse layout use a smaller minimum target (36 px) than touch (44 px), so desktop fits one more column.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-art-director · `gpt-image.ps1 -Project` takes a path relative to the BuildAPet folder; an absolute path doubles up and the save fails after the image was paid for. A `-Ref` edit saying "keep everything, only change 1) 2) 3)" keeps an approved look exactly. 3-item lineups work in one prompt with LEFT/MIDDLE/RIGHT and one glow colour each.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-art-director · gpt-image can hand back another agent's picture when agents share lyopz's ChatGPT at the same time (the dedup check misses it): open every result before using it. ChatGPT adds cartoon faces to anything called Phantom/ghost/soul, so say "no faces, no eyes".
- (ui, 2026-11-01) TextLabel.TextBounds is already in real screen px (after UIScale), so never multiply it by the UIScale chain again when you check "14 px real". To keep text at 14 px or more under a UIScale, set a fixed TextSize = ceil(14 / realScale) and clamp it. TextScaled + UITextSizeConstraint caps work in design px, so they drop under 14 real on phones.
- (ui, 2026-11-01) Shop wallets: put them as chips in the panel header (next to the title) and hide the HUD counters while the panel is open. The side tab rail then only needs to fit the tab icons (about 60 px real on phones) and no longer has to be as wide as the wallets.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-reviewer · Re-check glints/textures live, not from older card shots taken before a texture swap went live. Bring Studio to the foreground (SetForegroundWindow) before Play or textures and their errors never load. "No X in the shop" includes header wallet chips, not just tabs. Text overflowing badges and countdown chips hides in screenshots; a live TextFits audit of every label catches it.
- (ui, 2026-11-01) Badge text in a star/burst: TextScaled + UITextSizeConstraint (MaxTextSize in real px) overflows once the GUI scale is < 1; use a fixed TextSize, measure with TextService:GetTextSize and size the burst from the text (text width / 0.72), then check TextFits live.
- (ui, 2026-11-01) Client-side shop items can arrive without server-only fields (daily deals had no Grant); derive card art from Id/Name as a fallback so cards never fall back to a generic bag icon.
- (ui, 2026-11-01) rbxasset://textures/ui/Controls/RadialFill.png is a hard-edged ring, not a soft radial glow; used as "glow" it reads as a target/medallion. For a soft glow with no upload, stack ~5 circle Frames (BackgroundTransparency 0.82, sizes 1..0.28) in one CanvasGroup and tween GroupTransparency.
- (ui, 2026-11-01) A card row that "leaves a gap on the right" may simply have fewer items than columns; cap the column count to the item count (keep the full-count row height) before you blame layout timing.
- UI glow (2026-11-01, Shop r12.2): stacked-circle glows on dark wells need many layers (16) with graded alpha (outer ~1 %), or the steps show. Never end a line inside a Luau table constructor with `--` followed by more props: everything after the comment marker is silently dropped (AnchorPoint was lost, so the layers sat off-centre).
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-art-director (paid crates v2) · gpt-image handed back ANOTHER agent's picture twice in one batch (an eyes sheet and a mouths sheet arrived as our aura and egg lineups, both with "COMMON/SECRET" labels on dark grey); fast returns are not the tell, the content is. Open every result, move wrong ones out of the project folder, and rerun only the missing names.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-art-director (paid crates v2) · Paid offers with several top items at the SAME chance should share ONE Secret tier frame/crest; a higher tier crest at equal odds tells players something false. Keep graded tiers only where the odds are graded.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-art-director (paid crates v2) · Secrets from the same product that "look too alike" are fixed in the prompt by giving each its own hue + main material + silhouette tell (e.g. exhaust stacks / roof+handle / torn split) and saying "must look completely different"; squint-test them as three black shapes.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-art-director · Long prompt files with apostrophes broke a Bash heredoc in this environment (exit 2, unmatched quote); write prompt .txt files with the Write tool instead.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-modeler (storm crystals) · Crystal shells with a Neon core inside: use transparent SmoothPlastic (T 0.3-0.55, Reflectance 0.18, no SurfaceAppearance), not Glass material; Roblox Glass hides transparent parts behind it, so the inner core vanished.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-modeler (storm crystals) · Inserted GLB models face +Z in Studio when Blender front was -Y; clone each MeshPart with CFrame = CFrame.Angles(0,pi,0) * s.CFrame and assert a front-only part (the base Gem) has Position.Z < 0 before placing FX attachments.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-modeler (storm crystals) · Shared Studio: another agent can start Play between two screen_capture calls and every capture after that shows their HUD scene; check get_studio_state is Edit right before each batch of shots, and stage shots far away (Y 800, Z -6000) after a GetPartBoundsInBox emptiness check.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (storms) · Odds rows that show a display-rounded "1 in N" (3 significant figures) fail exact checks; test against the raw percentage (e.g. floor(100/Pct+0.5)) and use OneIn only for the label.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (storms) · Boost one rare outcome as weight x mult inside the existing weighted pick, applied only when a boost table is given; with no boost the RNG path and results stay byte-identical, so old tests and saves don't move.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (storms) · New timed server systems can show in an old HUD banner with no UI change by mirroring rows into the legacy replicated folders the banner already reads (leave out fields you don't want shown, like a "bought by" name).
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (storms) · Keep ONE ProcessReceipt: the shop's itemForProduct falls back to a new service's ItemForProduct(productId) that returns a synthetic item, and canGrant/grant get one branch each; never bind a second receipt handler.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (storms) · Pass "paid"/"noStorm" flags down to every roll helper (one OpenCosmeticLuck(player, paid) entry) so server-wide boosts can't leak into Robux opens; grep every caller of the old luck function when adding a boost.
- 2026-11-01 (reviewer, storms) Studio Edit mode can hand back old module copies loaded earlier: clone the Shared folder and require the clones for pure math checks.
- 2026-11-01 (reviewer, storms) If boosts skip paid opens, the odds SHOWN for paid eggs/crates must pass the same paid flag (OddsFor -> ShopOnly), or disclosed odds are false during a boost.
- 2026-11-01 (reviewer, storms) A ProcessReceipt handler that can grant only part (e.g. inventory cap) retries forever: grants must be all-or-nothing and paid grants skip caps.
- 2026-11-01 roblox-modeler: SurfaceAppearance.ColorMap needs the IMAGE id, not the Decal id that roblox-upload returns as assetId (panels stay flat colour). Resolve once with InsertService:LoadAsset(decalId):FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Decal",true).Texture (or read imageId from roblox-assets.json) and bake the image id into the installer. ContentProvider:PreloadAsync reports Failure in Edit even for working maps, so check with a screenshot instead.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (storms review fixes) · Paid timed boosts must survive a shutdown: fire a DataService.Closing signal inside BindToClose before the final saves, refund players still in the server into their profile, and write a side-store record (`<store>_Refunds`, key r_<uid>) for players who already left; pay it once on join and keep the paid ids in the profile, so a failed record delete can't pay twice.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (storms review fixes) · Data cleaning must clamp at a hard sanity cap, not the soft gameplay cap. If Robux grants may pass the soft cap, a reload would otherwise silently delete paid items.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-art-director (eyes + mouths) · ChatGPT (gpt-image.ps1) gives REAL alpha when the prompt says "TRANSPARENT background (PNG with alpha)" and asks for 3 items stacked in bands on a 1024x1536 portrait, with the labelled ladder sheet as -Ref: 16 tier pictures = 48 face PNGs at 0 credits, ~1 min each. Cut with a band splitter that falls back to the faintest row near each third when items touch (frost shards / wings bridge the gap).
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-art-director · Never pipe gpt-image.ps1 through `timeout ... | tail -1`: the stderr log is lost and a slow run gets killed in the Downloads fallback ("Automatic save did not finish"). Send stderr to a log file and let it finish; a re-run saved fine.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-art-director · Upper-tier mouths come back ~2-3:1 (fangs, drips, flames), not 4:1: fitting them by height into a 4:1 FaceZone made them unreadably small on the pet. Fit mouths by width, use the full canvas height and allow up to 1.4x vertical squash; check ladders for repeats across tiers (galaxy maw came back twice) and for blood-red drips (ask for black ink).
- 2026-11-01 (art-director, eyes/mouths): before sending a 24-item ladder, list the families per tier; the first pass had fire-dragon 3 times and two fang-row Secret grins. One family per tier, and make Secret pairs differ by shape (teeth vs runes), not just colour. Readability on busy bodies can be fixed for free in PIL (brightness + a tight 3-4 px dark rim) instead of a new picture.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-modeler (storm crystals v1.1) · Blender layout coords (v7kit `layout()` [x, z, y]) vs the GLB as Roblox imports it (v7kit export + `ROT = CFrame.Angles(0, pi, 0)`): Z keeps its sign but X is MIRRORED. Any FX/Corona attachment placed from layout data must use (-x, y, z). Check it with one asymmetric part (a Part centre vs its attachment) before trusting symmetric models.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-modeler · `roblox-upload.ps1` can fail with "WriteAllText ... user-mapped section" (the json was open in another process); just retry the same upload. Afterwards rebuild `roblox-assets.json` as the HEAD text + appended new keys (python: parse both, append missing keys in the 4/77/73-space format), so the diff only adds lines.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-modeler · editing a procedural texture script later (make_realm_textures.py): keep the rng call order the same and change only the one texture, or every other texture changes and needs a re-upload. Thin neon tori eat the 10k prop budget fast: cut their ring segments (24x6 is plenty) before cutting silhouette.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-ui-designer · MainUI's menu-open signal is its `HudMenuOpen` attribute (listen with GetAttributeChangedSignal); hover-opened popups (dock info drop) must check it too, or they reopen over a menu. The fullscreen ShopV2 keeps CoreUISafeInsets.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-ui-designer · Rotated GuiObjects don't clip children, so radial timer arcs = two unrotated half-width ClipsDescendants frames, each holding a full ring whose UIGradient (hard transparency step) rotates. Selection images ignore AnchorPoint; give badges a custom SelectionImageObject.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-ui-designer · ShopV2 `SH.compact` means portrait only; landscape phones are `SH.small`, so phone-only layouts must check `small`. Half-width buttons at the 14 px floor: hide icons, TextScaled + explicit line breaks ("NONE / OWNED", "NOT ON / SALE YET") and an uneven split (46/54); word wrap silently dropped "YET" and clipped "OWNED". LuckiestGuy "x0" reads as "XO": hide zero pills.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-ui-designer · Timed banners need a debug `hold` option so MCP screen_capture catches them. A debug viewport emulation must sit on MainUI's emulated Root (copy its AbsolutePosition), and the capture image is scaled (1920 wide vs ~1678 real): crop by Root box x scale.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-ui-designer · Big Lua through bash heredocs failed and Python backslash-n inside a <<'PY' heredoc still wrote a real newline into a Lua string: write the chunk with Write and insert with Python; use string.char(10) in Lua. A Play started right after a .Source sync can run old code: wait ~60 s.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-reviewer · `require` of a server service from execute_luau loads a fresh, never-started copy and errors; drive live tests through the DevCommand remote from the Client instead. Dev "give" tools must reject unknown ids loudly: a storm id silently gave 0 crystals and the next Use returned `none`.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-reviewer · A UIStroke on an inset track inside a CanvasGroup is cut at the group's edge: inset the track by at least the stroke thickness. ViewportFrame spinners must check the whole ancestor chain is visible (not just their own Visible), or a closed shop keeps updating every model each frame.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist · Black flame with a coloured rim = a two-tone texture (opaque BLACK body, WHITE rim + soft white halo) tinted by `Color` with LightEmission 0 (black x hue stays black, the rim takes the hue); LightEmission 1 turns the body invisible. Same sheet as an ImageLabel flipbook (ImageRectOffset stepping 256 px frames) in a sibling Frame at ZIndex-1 gives a ViewportFrame its flame ring, since particles don't render in viewports.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist · The game camera script reasserts CameraType every frame: hold a capture shot with BindToRenderStep at RenderPriority.Last+10 and hide HUD ScreenGuis (tag them so they come back). MCP screen_capture works in Play; `_tools/studio_grab.ps1` crops wrong. Bright white crystals (Phantom/Shiny) blow out under the place's Bloom: keep their PointLight <= 0.4 and tint the glow, not white.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist · Syncing a big ModuleScript into Studio without pasting: `python -m http.server <port>` on the scripts folder + HttpService:GetAsync in Edit (turn HttpEnabled on, then restore it). Random-interval effects (lightning bursts, glitch flashes) need a `handle:Trigger()` so captures don't miss them.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-ui-designer · ContextActionService:CallFunction fails from MCP ("lacking capability RobloxScript"): expose gamepad handlers through the screen's debug BindableFunction (e.g. StormDebug "pad","up") to test console flows.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-ui-designer · Decal id -> image id: InsertService:LoadAsset(decalId) in the Server DataModel, read the Decal's Texture. MCP execute_luau does not share _G with game scripts; fire the real DevCommand remote from the Client instead.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-ui-designer · Two notices for one event usually means two systems own it (here the old EventService WorldEvent + the new StormHUD): filter by flag AND by title in the old notice, and drop its toasts too. One thing on screen = one clock: every timer for the same thing reads the same EndsAt at the same tick rate.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-ui-designer · Studio scripts can lag disk (another agent edited the file after the last sync): diff Studio Source length vs disk before a Play test. EventNotice.lua and roblox-assets.json are CRLF; edit byte-aware.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist · Flipbook frames need an empty margin (>= 12 px of 256) on every side: a flame base cut flat at the tile's bottom edge bled into the frame below and drew thin coloured dashes above every particle. Check with numpy (alpha of each tile's first/last rows = 0) before uploading.
- 2026-11-01 (art-director): gpt-image.ps1 with `-Minutes 5` and one picture at a time worked first try after a stuck Downloads-fallback run; stop a run as soon as it says "watch Downloads" and resend rather than waiting.
- 2026-11-01 (art-director, eyes/mouths Studio test): face Decals on a transparent plate render fine in Edit, so you can test faces without Play (fresh ids may be blank on the first capture only). Game Decals need the IMAGE id (InsertService:LoadAsset(decalId) -> Decal.Texture), not the decal id. roblox-upload.ps1's skin-tone HOLD fires on ivory teeth and gold eyes; check them by eye, then use -Reviewed. Never screen_capture during another agent's Play: it moves their client camera.
- 2026-11-01 (art-director): busy gold bodies (Scrap_Overclock gear skin) swallow gold/orange/red face art; on yellow TeslaDynamo, orange faces disappear at 28 studs. Faces need a dark under-shadow or contrast rule per body (cool or pink faces on warm bodies), not just per tier.
- 2026-11-01 · Lead · Privacy: zstd-compressed .blend files hide the Windows username from grep (Blender stores the file-browser Documents path in every save). Scan with Blender's bundled python (zstandard) after decompressing, and fix new files with a same-length byte swap of the name before committing (opens fine afterwards). All 183 older committed .blends still carry it.
- 2026-11-01 (scripter, Build-A-Pet storms r3): keep storm Secret luck a separate `secretLuck` parameter through Odds/CrateOdds, so paid opens exclude it cleanly and Luck+Shiny can take the max instead of multiplying.
- 2026-11-01 (scripter): refund unused timed boosts by recording each use as a slice (SliceStart/SliceEnd) and banking unused seconds per id; retire an event by keeping its definition with Retired=true (refused in Begin, skipped in Pick/Plan) so saves, notices and dev tools don't break.
- 2026-11-01 (scripter): when Studio isn't connected, the Luau CLI (`luau-compile --null`, plus `luau.exe` on a harness that inlines the module's requires) still checks syntax and pure logic offline. In Git Bash, write Lua/Python edit specs with the Write tool; heredocs break on quoting.
- 2026-11-01 (vfx-artist, storm skies): a world BillboardGui hides every Beam behind its whole rectangle (even transparent pixels, even at ImageTransparency 1), and one big opaque glow Beam (T 0, ZOffset -300) hid every other sky beam. Build sky quads from FaceCamera Beams (TextureSpeed 0, or the picture scrolls), keep end keys <= 0.97, and check layers one at a time when a whole sky vanishes. Before ANY Studio call, check list_roblox_studios: the place name, not the old studio id (a studio id can be another game).
- 2026-11-01 (vfx-artist): a non-FaceCamera Beam's width runs along its attachments' Y axis; a seamless sky shell = fixed facets of a cone sharing edges, placed just behind the other layers (1.2x radius) with ZOffset -400 (beams sort by chord midpoint; at 2x with no ZOffset every other layer vanished). The Lighting sun disc draws on top of beams and can't be covered.
- 2026-11-01 (vfx-artist): a normal Roblox camera only sees the sky from el 0 to ~35; put storm signatures (windows, planets, tears) at el 12-30 and make them drift after the camera yaw so they stay in view.
- 2026-11-01 (art-director, Build-A-Pet eyes v2): check the Studio id AND place name match the brief before staging anything. Another place ("pet fighting sim") also holds PetAssets.Bodies, so finding the right assets is not proof it is the right game.
- 2026-11-01 (art-director): gpt-image can stall on ChatGPT's Cloudflare "Just a moment..." check (page title at 127.0.0.1:9333/json). A good concept sheet can be cut into finals with a border-connected background matte, which keeps black ink opaque.
- 2026-11-01 (art-director): the upload tool's skin-tone hold falsely flagged gold eyes and teeth (5 of 48). Check by eye, then rerun with -Reviewed.
- 2026-11-01 (scripter, Build-A-Pet storms r3): Studio copies drift from disk (an old Shared.MultiOpenRules without Check gave "attempt to call a nil value" in Crate/EggService). Diff Studio against disk before blaming code; 63 of 297 scripts differed here.
- 2026-11-01 (scripter): hatch-based test suites leak the hatch cooldown into each other (V7Tests live hatch right after luckspintest returns nil). Run each suite in its own Play or leave a gap.
- 2026-11-01 (scripter): keep secretLuck its own parameter so paid opens can leave it out, and record per-storm time slices so a shutdown banks only the unused seconds (Robux can't be refunded; bank time instead).
- 2026-11-01 (ui-designer, Build-A-Pet notifications): the shared ChatGPT session (gpt-image.ps1) can hand back another agent's picture when two agents use it at once. Open every result before using it; run image jobs one agent at a time.
- 2026-11-01 (ui-designer): to re-tier server notices without touching busy server files, classify the NetService.Notify texts on the client by pattern (Notify.Classify); unknown texts fall back to a toast.
- 2026-11-01 (ui-designer): a UIStroke on a full-screen frame draws half of itself off screen; for an edge flash, inset the frame by half the stroke thickness.
- 2026-11-01 (scripter, Studio drift): to pull many Studio sources, have Studio POST each script to a local scratch server instead of script_read (keeps ~1 MB of source out of chat); restore HttpEnabled after.
- 2026-11-01 (scripter): when diffing Studio vs disk, also scan Studio for scripts with NO disk file; that's where Studio-only features (InventoryWorkbench) and risky dev scripts (owner grant scripts) hide. Classify each Studio-only line against `git log --follow` before syncing either way.
- 2026-11-01 (vfx-artist, shiny invert): a Beam only hides at visibility exactly 0 (the transparency sequence caps at 0.97); when multiplying visibilities, floor anything under 0.006 to 0.
- 2026-11-01 (vfx-artist): to squeeze a whole sky set into an elevation band, register raw attachment positions and remap them, with every per-frame write going through one setPos so nothing escapes the band.
- 2026-11-01 (vfx-artist): inverting glows: 1-c turns bright lights dark; invert then lift value to >=0.75, and near-grey results take the key colour. Surfaces (body, pattern ink) take the plain 1-c negative so patterns stay readable.
- 2026-11-01 (scripter, drift sync): scripts with one-space indents were edited directly in Studio. Python writes CRLF on Windows, so strip `
- 2026-11-01 (scripter): never fire two DevCommand suites that hatch (eventtest + luckspintest) in the same second. They share the hatch cooldown and session, and LuckSpin shows 3 false FAILs ("x8 open = 0 items"). Fire one suite per command and wait for its DONE line. Script-in-ServerStorage checks such as InventoryWorkbenchVerification can be run in Play by cloning them into ServerScriptService from the Server datamodel; loadstring is not available in execute_luau. LogService:GetLogHistory from MCP misses the game's prints, so read get_console_output instead.
- 2026-11-01 (art-director): the ChatGPT window is shared by every agent. Run gpt-image.ps1 only when no other gpt-image.ps1 powershell process is running (check Win32_Process CommandLine), or you get another agent's picture or a failed download. gpt-image.mjs now prefers images inside the assistant message. Start each run with Bash run_in_background, not `&`, because a shell `&` job dies when the call returns.
- 2026-11-01 (art-director): `-Ref` edits that say "keep everything exactly, change ONLY 1-3 named things" fix lineup sheets in one pass (blade counts, a patterned front, glow out of the face zone) and leave the rest pixel-identical. Generate the whole tier lineup first, then fix with an edit instead of regenerating.
- 2026-11-01 (art-director): for long ChatGPT prompts, write the prompt file with the Write tool and pass it with Get-Content -Raw. Bash heredocs broke on the quotes ("unexpected EOF").
- 2026-11-01 (vfx, Shiny inverted skies): BasePart.Color stores 8-bit, so a graded Color3 never equals what you read back; snap graded colours with Color3.fromRGB(math.round(c.R*255), ...) before equality tests.
- 2026-11-01 (vfx, sky captures): execute_luau in the Client DM gets its own require() instance, so module state set there (StormSky.Preview, RippleHold) does nothing; drive the live module through a Studio-only BindableFunction in PlayerGui (StormSkyDebug: preview/hold/crystalinvert/state).
- 2026-11-01 (vfx, Studio Play): a screen_capture can time out and stall for minutes; after a timeout, stop the Play first and re-shoot in the next session so the 3-min Play limit holds.
- 2026-11-01 (ui, Notify Studio pass): a UIGradient MULTIPLIES the frame's BackgroundColor3, so a gradient on a dark fill renders near black; set the fill to WHITE and put every colour in the gradient (the celebration plate read as a black box until this fix).
- 2026-11-01 (ui, Notify Studio pass): UITextSizeConstraint.MinTextSize is in pre-UIScale units; under a Fit UIScale of 0.69 a 14 floor renders 9.7 px. Raise it to ceil(14 / scale) after the scale is known, and widen the panel on phones (up to 1.4x) or the floor clips words ("QUEST" / "HATCH YOUR").
- 2026-11-01 (ui, Studio): ContextActionService:CallFunction needs RobloxScript from execute_luau; test a gamepad action by checking GetAllBoundActionInfo() and calling the same Lua handler the action wraps. multi_edit with the same old/new pairs as the disk edit is the cheapest way to sync a big module into Edit (compare #Source with wc -c).
- 2026-11-01 (vfx, Shiny negative sky): dark UNLIT, untextured veil beams (horizon bands, fog ribbons) invert to pale flat cards with hard straight edges over a light negative sky; fade them under the negative (StormSky NegVeilCut 0.9) instead of inverting them.
- 2026-11-01 (vfx, Shiny negative sky): darkening a pale negative (V x0.7) turns it khaki/grey; lock the darkened dome's hue to the event's Shiny key (DomeH) with a saturation floor, for the WHOLE gradient (a hue switch at a saturation threshold left a hard step).
- 2026-11-01 (vfx, Shiny negative): a white-tinted picture beam inverts to black; give it a curated dark key-colour ink (DarkV) so it reads as a dark portal hole in the light sky, not a black slab and not a flat cream "light" card.
- 2026-11-01 (art, gpt-image queue): a "wait until no gpt-image.ps1 is running" check built on Get-CimInstance CommandLine -like '*gpt-image.ps1*' counts ITSELF (its own command line holds the pattern) and waits forever; exclude $PID. A ChatGPT -Ref run that shows busy=false, new pictures=0 for 10+ min has stalled: stop it and retry with a smaller ref (1600x900).
- 2026-11-01 (art, lineup sheets): to re-lay out items from approved ChatGPT sheets for free, crop a generous box per item, keep the largest non-white connected component plus components that do not touch the box edge (scipy ndimage.label on a dilated mask), and white out known neighbour fragments; no regeneration, designs stay exactly as approved.
- 2026-11-01 (art-director, paid aura crate v6): a 7-item ChatGPT sheet got all 7 auras right but dropped one small signature detail (Ink Enso's red seal). Adding a small flat mark (stamp, badge) with PIL is free and safer than a re-run, which can drift the other 6 items. Draw an abstract mark, never a real letter or kanji (a 3-bar glyph read as 王).
- 2026-11-01 (art-director): to keep 7 auras distinct, give each its own element, silhouette, motion and main colour, plus a 'nearest other / nearest free' column. Ban the free set's elements in the prompt (sun, fire, lightning, crystals, waves, purple, gold). One run gave 7 clearly different auras.
- 2026-11-01 (art-director, aura crate v7): a crate of 7 auras must pass TWO bars at once: distinct (own element/silhouette/motion/colour) AND the aura bar (2-3 tilted rings, base glow, sparkles). Draw each aura's rings in its own motif (stone, paper ribbon, plankton, droplets) so the rings stay distinct too. A -Ref edit that says 'keep everything, only ADD rings/glow/sparkles' upgraded all 7 in one run without drift.
- (vfx, 2026-11-01) A straight Beam renders only a few samples of its ColorSequence/Transparency whatever Segments or CurveSize; narrow colour bands inside one sequence vanish. Paint bands as separate flat beams (a beam's two end colours/opacities always render).
- (vfx, 2026-11-01) In Play the Lighting sun disc draws over opaque Beams and Parts, and Atmosphere haze swallows Parts/Beams beyond ~500 studs; only dome-shell beams with ZOffset ~-400 survive. Hiding the sun needs a Sky setting.
- (studio, 2026-11-01) To hold a shot camera in Play, BindToRenderStep at Enum.RenderPriority.Last.Value + 50 (game cameras override lower priorities). After ~10 Plays in one session Studio's 3D view went blank (HUD only, Edit too): restart Studio before more shots.
- (scripter, 2026-11-01) Policy-gated paid random items: a receipt can arrive for a player whose policy now reads restricted (or unknown). Never return NotProcessedYet forever. Record each prompt made while the player was confirmed unrestricted (7-day window) and grant those normally. With no record, grant a fixed, non-random credit (pick one item up to a value), save it, then return PurchaseGranted. Prune purchase ids after ~30 days.
- (scripter, 2026-11-01) "%.2f" shows 2.165 as 2.16 (binary float). For odds text, round half up with an epsilon: math.floor(x*100 + 0.5 + 1e-7)/100.
- (studio, 2026-11-01) A feature commit can sit on disk and never reach Studio. Before an allow-list sync, serve the parent commit's files on a second port and compare them with Studio. If they match, nobody changed the scripts in Studio, so they are safe to overwrite.
- (app, 2026-11-01) gpt-image.ps1 can drop silently into the "click download" fallback when gpt-image.mjs exits 2 (message box not found on a freshly opened ChatGPT window) and then sit idle for 30 min. Check for a running node gpt-image.mjs; if only the ps1 is alive, kill it and rerun the .mjs directly. gpt-image.ps1 now retries once on exit 2.
- (models, 2026-11-01) Studio raycasts hit a mesh's collision hull, not the visible mesh, so hollow/caged bodies give false face-zone hits. Check face zones with a mounted test face plate + screenshot (remove it after), or probe with CreateMeshPartAsync(..., {CollisionFidelity = PreciseConvexDecomposition}).
- (models, 2026-11-01) Lit-glass bodies: a transparent pane shows whatever is inside (empty box, inner cage rails). Give the inside a warm frosted liner and keep inner cage faces off the view lines, especially for panes that open. Glow strength above ~0.2 with one tint washes a painted gradient flat.
- (vfx, 2026-11-01) Amber/tinted glass hides cool-coloured particles behind it: put the ghost/cool colour on effects outside the glass. Lab test copies need the BaseBodyFX tag (or BodyFX.Attach) to show FX, and stream out in Play unless ModelStreamingMode = Persistent.
- (design, 2026-11-01) Paid random items with a bonus roll (e.g. bonus Secret after a Mythic/Legendary pull) are allowed when disclosed: show the bonus lines on the odds sheet and compute the headline from the true total (4% + 3%x4% + 7%x3% = 4.33%, 1 in 23), and each Secret's total (2.17%), not only the base.
- (models, 2026-11-01) BodyKit.Panels doors: extra parts (frame, rivets, handles) swing with a Panel_ only when parented UNDER that Panel_ part (Util.rest stores BasePart descendants as riders); name them without the `Panel_` prefix or they become panels themselves. Set the CFrame first, then parent. Make opening panels DoubleSided or the door vanishes when seen from inside.
- (models, 2026-11-01) Iron cages around a lit liner: clip every iron triangle that sits deeper inside the shell than the liner (sdf < -0.03, cut on the true sdf) instead of recolouring it; inner skirts, foot tops and corner-cap backs otherwise show as a dark slab through an open pane.
- (tools, 2026-11-01) Blender -b -P with a relative output path writes into the shell's cwd (on Git Bash that was C:\), not next to the script: always pass absolute output paths.
- (scripting, 2026-11-01) A DescendantAdded re-attach hook on an FX'd model must ignore the FX's own pieces (its BFX_* parts, runtime folder, emitters) or it re-attaches forever and every timer inside the FX resets (Soul Lantern doors never opened). Re-attach only for real streamed-in BaseParts, debounced.
- (testing, 2026-11-01) BodyFX freezes past FreezeDistance (LOD): put the Play camera next to the model before judging motion. execute_luau `require` gets its own module copy, not the game's: reach the live runtime through attributes (player attr BodyFXTest = freeze/unfreeze/sig/frame:...) instead.
- (models, 2026-11-01) Hinged side doors need ~70 deg (back ~55) to read from the front camera; 25 deg looks shut.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (v2 eyes/mouths) · Studio sync with no Rojo: serve the scripts folder with `python -m http.server` on 127.0.0.1, then in one Edit execute_luau set HttpService.HttpEnabled true, GetAsync each file, set .Source (CRLF -> LF), set HttpEnabled false again; never save. Repo files mix LF and CRLF, so keep each file's own line endings when editing from scripts.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (v2 eyes/mouths) · Swapping a placeholder item for a new design with the same id: update the existing AssetRegistry record and Parts row in place (no duplicate), so owned copies show the new art. Watch tests that read a rarity off that id: V7Tests' crate-odds check broke because Mouth_OverclockMaw moved from Secret I to Secret II, and the loop `if Rarity == "Secret" then csec = r` keeps the LAST secret row; filter by SecretTier.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (v2 eyes/mouths) · Open any Index tab or odds panel from MCP with the client hook `PlayerGui.MainUI.Debug:Invoke("indextab", "<TabId>")` / `Invoke("odds", "Crate", "<CrateId>")`, then screen_capture; no clicks needed.
- 2026-11-01 (Lead): game apps made by make-game-app.ps1 live outside ~/BuildAPet, so safety-hook.ps1 refused every folder delete there; it now also allows the folder two levels above the hook. Test the hook with commands read from a file: a test command that itself contains a delete path gets checked (and blocked) by the live hook, and Get-Content lines carry PSPath note properties that ConvertTo-Json turns into objects (cast to [string]).
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (retire gloss eyes) · Retiring a STARTER part: give pets their own `Starter = true` copy of the new look (e.g. Starter_GlossDropEyes cloning Eyes_GlossDrop.Face2D, no Chest/Island) instead of handing out the chest item; the Starter flag is what blocks trade, sell, income, bonus rolls, Index count and crate pools, so the real item would mint a free tradable copy per hatch.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (retire gloss eyes) · Rename retired item ids in the every-load repair AND after TradeService.Reconcile (Config.Items.LegacyPartIds + DataService renameLegacyParts, keeps uid/equip/skin/Shiny, stores LegacyAssetID), not only in a versioned migration: half-finished trade records deep-copy old instances back in after migrations ran. No schema bump needed; it is idempotent.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (retire gloss eyes) · Removing an AssetMeta entry turns test lookups like `AssetMeta.Entries.<id>` into nil silently (Compat.Check just skips it, so the test can pass for the wrong reason); grep tests for the id and swap in an inline fixture with the old bounds.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (overlay test r7) · AuraFX only animates from RenderStepped on a running client (AuraFX.lua:376): in Edit the beams are zero-length and emitters Rate 0, so aura shots look blank. Step it from Heartbeat for Edit previews, or shoot in Play after a 1.5 s warm-up; in Play pin the camera with BindToRenderStep (Last+10) since the game camera script moves a Scriptable camera, and remember AuraFX.AnimDistance = 45 swaps far auras to the FarLOD disc.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (overlay test r7) · AuraFX.Apply takes the AuraLook name (Parts[id].AuraLook, e.g. ScrapMotes), not the item id; an item id silently builds nothing.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (overlay test r7) · Shiny via Color multiply can't show on painted SurfaceAppearances (SA.Color white, design in ColorMap): ShinyColor(white) = pearl, almost no change. Painted wings/ground need an explicit Shiny tint or overlay.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (overlay test r7) · Bulk Studio shots without screen_capture: PrintWindow(hwnd, flag 2) on the Studio window grabs the viewport incl. Edit-mode particles; encode the state id as 9 black/white marker squares in a ScreenGui so a background grab loop names frames itself (start the loop BEFORE the run). Logical ViewportSize x1.5 = physical px at 150% DPI. Hide BillboardGuis, ShowDevelopmentGui and note the ViewCube shows in Edit shots.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (overlay test r7) · Luau: a line starting with `(` after a statement ending in `)` or `]` is "Ambiguous syntax" (`(d :: any)[k] = v`); use a typed local (`local inst: any = d`). Plugin-side require caches modules: clone the ModuleScript to require a fresh copy after setting .Source. sed -i on Windows can turn CRLF into LF.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (overlay test r7) · Hue-rotating a colour toward a palette takes the shortest hue path: cyan pulled toward orange passes through green. For far hues snap to the nearest palette colour by lightness instead.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-scripter (retire gloss eyes) · MCP screen_capture in Play ignores its camera arguments and the client camera controller overrides a one-off CFrame; bind a RenderStep at Last+100 that sets a Scriptable camera every frame, shoot, then unbind and restore CameraType Custom. Edit require() kept the old Config even after Source was set, so trust Edit only for suites that clone their modules (PetV2Tests); run the rest in Play.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (Omniverse overlay v2) · Sync a disk script into Studio without Rojo: `python -m http.server` in the scripts folder, then in Edit turn HttpService.HttpEnabled on, GetAsync the file, ScriptEditorService:UpdateSourceAsync, loadstring to check it compiles, turn HttpEnabled back to what it was, and kill the server. Compare Source length with the disk file after stripping CR to prove they match.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (Omniverse overlay v2) · A glow rim behind 2D face art: offset copies of the decal make a dark smudge because the v2 art has a dark keyline. Make one white silhouette per picture instead (alpha dilated + blurred, make_glow.py), upload it free, and tint it. roblox-upload returns a DECAL id; use InsertService:LoadAsset(id) and read Decal.Texture for the image id. Use a Texture, not a Decal, if the controller has to animate it (the hue walk only runs on Texture).
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (Omniverse overlay v2) · Shiny colour inversion turns dark Neon aura cores into glowing white spheres (GearOrbit ClockFace 120a26 became c7db95). For solid aura parts, flip the hue but cap the value at the original +0.15. Aura skin/Shiny is read from the AuraSkin/AuraShiny attributes on the model, not Skin/IsShiny, and aura ids are ScrapXxx.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (aura quality fix) · Recolouring a multi-colour aura for a skin overlay: find its MAIN hue (24 hue bins weighted by saturation x value x emitter rate, skip gold 0.09-0.17 and greys) and pull only colours within 0.08 hue of it. Pulling every colour melts gold rims and second accents into one wash and the aura loses its detail. Shiny = hue +0.5 keeping each colour's own value (never cap value, it dims the centres).
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (aura quality fix) · Before calling an effect "worse", shoot it alone at full quality next to the LATEST approved round's picture: AuraFX.quality() drops to 0.7 on touch or low SavedQualityLevel, and 20 auras 7 studs apart blur into each other. Colour baked into a texture (Cosmos violet "+" sprites) can't be hue-flipped by Color tint; author textures white if they must recolour.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (aura round 11) · A cloned MeshPart that has a SurfaceAppearance keeps the SA's colour: setting part.Color does nothing visible (the Halo_Gear GearRing cogs stayed gold and read as "coins"). Set SurfaceAppearance.Color too, or the tint is silently ignored. Detail that rides a structure needs the same: per-frame colour writes in a style's step overwrite TintAura, so read the tinted colour from ctx.tc; parts faded to Transparency 1 are skipped by the tint pass, so store the base colour in an AuraTint attribute.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (aura round 11) · Lab gotchas: BaseCube_v2 calls its body "Shell", not "Body", so ScrapAccessoryFX overlays fail silently in a lab until you rename it. The cube faces -Z. multi_edit only works in the Edit datamodel and is refused while Play runs, and edits made in Play don't persist: stop Play, multi_edit, then compare the Source length with the disk file (CR stripped). shoot.py marker ids must be < 250.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (aura round 11) · World events end up in VFX shots: the Bolt Rain event drops token coins (black, yellow bolt) that lyopz took for an aura bug. Before you capture, read the workspace WorldEvent* attributes and wait the event out (or hide WorldEvents). COOKBOOK section 4 particle caps (C ≤ 6 … S ≤ 30) and AuraTiers.Caps {24..55} disagree; say which one you measured against.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (aura round 11b) · A rim made with ctx.rim(part, ...) is a one-off clone sized at build time: if the step scales the part (Singularity core collapse), keep a ref to each rim with its grow factor and set rim.Size = part.Size * (1 + grow) every frame, or the rim stays big and the move reads soft.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (aura round 11b) · Check the face your detail sits on: in AuraStyles/Detail the front is -Z, so a neon seam at +Z was on the back of every coal chunk and never showed. "Hot coal" reads when the dark slabs are ~0.86 of the glowing core's size (gaps = cracks) and the seams face the camera.
- 2026-11-01 · roblox-vfx-artist (aura round 11b) · Re-read COOKBOOK section 4 before adding a "flare": an expanding ring on the tell is the banned blast ring even when it is gold and short; make the structure itself open (blades fan out, hold, fold back). Sync check after multi_edit: compare Source length and a byte checksum (CR stripped) with the disk file.
- 2026-11-01 [build-a-pet, Claude art-director] ChatGPT scene sets: append one shared STYLE block (and a SKY block for outdoors) to every prompt so the set matches; "stacked horizontal bands like torn windows, glowing crackling seams, portal rings on the seams" recalls the Storm Crystal sky.
- 2026-11-01 [build-a-pet, Claude art-director] Variant sheets: "same layout so they read as one set" gives recolours; name a different outline and arrangement per item, share only the kit pieces. Pets in scenes need "no ears, wide sketchy toothy grin".
- 2026-11-01 [build-a-pet, Claude Lead] Bash heredocs with apostrophes inside a quoted 'EOF' Python f-string block break the Bash tool; write generator scripts with the Write tool.
- 2026-11-01 [build-a-pet, Claude map-builder] Hub with N plots on a ring: plot centres at r >= 2.2 x plot width / (2 sin(180/N)); rock-path docks halfway between ring buildings (22.5 + n*45 for 8).
- 2026-11-01 [build-a-pet, Claude map-builder] 8-player servers need N/2 arena copies (>=600 studs apart, haze hides them); placed pets scale players x pads (160) so plan distance fading and an animated-pet cap before building plots.
- 2026-11-01 [build-a-pet, Claude scripter] Economy sims must subtract purchases (first egg ~1.5 min sanity check); with pets surviving rebirth use stepped requirements (x6 -> x3 -> x2 -> x1.8) plus a stacking bonus; World Rank hatch/chest counts gate the mid game more than Tokens.
- 2026-11-01 [build-a-pet, Claude map-builder] Play-test jumps from client code with Humanoid:ChangeState(Jumping) plus Move() every frame; setting Humanoid.Jump does nothing there. Put the IslandTop tag on whole models, and raycast a test wedge to confirm its orientation (organic tops from wedge fans cost ~2 parts per edge point).
- 2026-11-01 [build-a-pet, Claude scripter] No luau compiler on the PC: compile-check through Studio loadstring via a local HTTP sync, and keep each file's line endings. Put MemoryStore and Teleport behind adapters with mocks; name arena copies after their pad; lend a fresh test player a temporary pet and restore it after.
- 2026-11-01 [build-a-pet, Claude reviewer+scripter] Cross-server queues: a read must report "read failed" apart from "not found" (else a re-join overwrites a live claim); the claimed server checks the match record whoever claimed; Leave removes the entry and cancels its match record, with a rejoin cooldown; every arrival on a fight server needs a way home (late arrivals too); never match two players from the same server.
- 2026-11-01 [build-a-pet, Claude reviewer+scripter] Only handle teleport failures for teleports you started, and cap retries. Copy arena venues from a ServerStorage template, never the live venue. A purchase grant into a full wallet is logged and counted as granted or it retries forever. Derive Placeholder from a missing ProductId. Purchase tests delete their rows. Search for client copies of income formulas when the server formula changes.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude ui-designer] Studio screen_capture does not show CoreGui: preview a UI in Edit mode as a temp ScreenGui in StarterGui (name it __*_TEMP, delete after). Big modules (>70 KB) go in through a local http.server on a free port (another agent may hold 8765) with HttpEnabled switched on only around GetAsync and restored; require a clone inside a cloned UI folder to compile-check.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude ui-designer] Cancel a running TweenService tween before setting the same property instantly, or the old tween overwrites it next frame. Keep TextScaled notices short (one clause): at the 0.69 phone UIScale a long line drops under 14 px.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude scripter] Server jump checks: cap height a bit below one more air jump's rise instead of counting launches (10 Hz launch samples are unreliable); measured chain peaks ran ~4 studs over the maths. Keep config key names when their meaning changes so code and saves keep working. Rerun the old economy through the same sim before comparing; old doc numbers may have used other settings.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude map-builder] Set an Attachment's Parent before its WorldPosition (unparented it is read as local, so beams/waterfalls land wrong). A Ball part is ~380 tris: use small rotated blocks (12 tris) for flowers, and check a rock's body sphere doesn't poke up through its landing. Serve the build script locally and loadstring one section per call to keep execute_luau small and rebuild sections alone.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude art-director] gpt-image.ps1 shares one ChatGPT window and takes the newest image: two agents at once swap and duplicate pictures. Run picture jobs one at a time across agents and check each result by eye plus md5 against other files before scoring.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude art-director] UI and fight-card pictures: allow only the named words and add "no title banner, no game name"; else ChatGPT invents a game title. Repeat "NO arms" and name the sky in every pet-action prompt (crowd pets grow arms, the sky drifts to daytime).
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude animator] Move sets: build one frame template per archetype and reuse it; turn "expanding shockwave" into real 3D shapes (rings banned); avoid other games' move vocabulary, name moves from our own world.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude art-director] World pictures: a reusable MYTH block (pale ruin stone + cyan rune grooves + violet crystal roots braided into a spike + vines + spout waterfalls into mist + orbiting rune stones) turned plain floating rocks into "mythical" in one try; ChatGPT still draws isle tops as perfect circles with full thick brass rings, so the build brief must say "lobed outline, broken bite, band in 2-3 arcs".
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude art-director] "Pets roam, not pads": ask for a named action per pet (drinking, napping, peeking from a burrow, hopping a log) and a capacity totem with N sockets in exact states (lit / dim / padlocked); ChatGPT got the 5/3/2 split right both times. Give each habitat prop a PetSpot attachment so the roam AI has places to go.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude art-director] To make an add-on area feel "part of the island", prompt "grows out of the island's own rock: same strata, paving and plants flow across, bases sunk into rock plinths"; it came back as terraces stepping DOWN like a hillside, which reads more natural than a raised deck.
- 2026-11-02 [Claude scripter] A new server->client event name must also go in the net module's EVENTS list, or Fire only warns "unknown event" and the client never hears it; grep every Fire("...") name against the list after adding events.
- 2026-11-02 [Claude scripter] Long, yielding match runners (lineup windows, paced replays) get an `Instant` option (no waits, simulate everything at once) so tests can run whole matches in a second and check results.
- 2026-11-02 [Claude scripter] Dev "give pet" commands don't equip; a live team test must fire the real equip remote from the Client DM first, or the live part quietly skips.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude vfx-artist] Effect helper wrappers (NS/CS) must accept a ready NumberSequence/ColorSequence as well as a key table; one bad helper call throws inside a pcall'd step and silently kills the rest of the hit (only the Core showed). Read the console "[FightFX] step:" warnings after every preview run.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude vfx-artist] Flat camera-facing hit sparks/flashes need pulling ~2 studs toward the camera or the pet bodies bury them; in a camera-facing CFrame local -Z points AT the camera. Large camera-facing particles behind a pet clip into the floor, so lift them (~1.6 studs).
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude vfx-artist] Edit-mode screen_capture DOES show StarterGui ScreenGuis (banners), but slow-mo freeze frames land late: wait ~1.5 s before capturing. Neon afterimages at 0.55 transparency look heavy in freeze frames; 0.7 reads as ghosts. Check a preview spot is empty with GetPartBoundsInBox first; reset preview pets after a Knockout run (the loser is left displaced).
- 2026-11-02 · roblox-map-builder (hub v2 r15) · Stacked flat strata fans under a floating island read as a "wedding cake". Hang 6 big inverted kit rocks (height ~1.3x band thickness) off each band's lower edge, end in an inverted kit-rock spike + a 16-stud crystal tip: reads as a mythic cone for ~+12 parts.
- 2026-11-02 · roblox-map-builder · A slot grid on a face that looks down -Z is mirrored for the viewer: local +X is the viewer's LEFT. Put slot 1 at +X so 1..N read left-to-right; check with a front screenshot before reporting.
- 2026-11-02 · roblox-map-builder · Studio screenshots: StarterGui.ShowDevelopmentGui=false (task.delay restore) hides the dev HUD; jump-gap checks need RaycastParams.RespectCanCollide=true or decor (orbit stones, vines) fakes "land" and the gaps come out 0.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude vfx-artist] If a shared mesh template goes missing (Core_Scrap.Gear_R), fallbacks silently turn every gear into a cylinder: keep the asset id in code (SpecialMesh FileMesh) and add a mirrored twin (X scale negative) when the mesh is one-sided. ParticleEmitter LockedToPart = true makes a sunburst/glow ride a moving, spinning hub.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude vfx-artist] Fight helper option types bite: F.Dust{chips = true} crashed "compare number < boolean" (chips is a count). A billboard sunburst behind a ~3-stud pet must sit >= 3.9 studs above body centre or its lower third sinks into the floor.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude vfx-artist] Anticipation recipe that lifted scripted cube moves from 7 to 8.5: 1-frame rise, squash 0.8-0.84 + lean back 8-16 deg, a 3-4 frame tremble hold (math.noise x 0.05), then a 2-frame smear stretch 1.25 and afterimages at every rank. Re-run and wait ~2 s before capturing a crit; the KO harness can leave the loser tumbled and hidden (reset pivot + LocalTransparencyModifier = 0).
- (map-builder, 2026-11-02) CollectionService:GetTagged misses instances not yet parented into the DataModel; while building, find tagged parts with model:GetDescendants() + HasTag.
- (map-builder, 2026-11-02) Studio's camera is shared: another agent's Scriptable camera loop overrides screen_capture camera_position; wait for it to finish (or ask it to pause) before shooting, then leave its CameraType as you found it.
- (map-builder, 2026-11-02) Beam waterfalls: a long opaque white beam reads as a flat slab. Use a blue body cut to ~0.7x the drop with transparency 0.1 -> 1 along its length, a shorter white core, foam puffs at the lip and mist emitters top and bottom.
- (map-builder, 2026-11-02) Single 0.5-stud flower blocks vanish at hub distance; add a flowering bush (2 balls in lilac/rose) next to each clump so it reads from 50+ studs.
- (art-director, 2026-11-02) ChatGPT puts every badge crest of a multi-portal sheet inside the same shield frame unless the prompt says "cut out in the emblem's own outline, never inside a shield or badge frame; every crest a different shape".
- (art-director, 2026-11-02) Sheets of 5 unique structures: give each item a CAPS primary shape plus a closing "Silhouette: ..." line; outlines come out distinct. Two root-based items still converge (R15/R16), so give each organic item a non-root primary shape (angular circuit roots, a squat stump, a crown).
- (art-director, 2026-11-02) A -Ref edit that swaps 15 props into an approved scene keeps camera and world but packs the new props wall to wall and drops the plazas; ask for gaps, plazas and slot width explicitly, and treat per-item sheets, not the hero, as the design source.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude vfx-artist] "Make the hit feel like the liked one" = copy its structure, not its parts: one huge solid 3D object, readable tell, edge-first impact on the target, grind through hit-stop, chunks + crack, B/W frame. For arm-less cube pets a giant copy of the pet's own shell (clone + Highlight outline + neon leading edge) is the object; uniform scale in the pose works for "the pet grows giant".
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude vfx-artist] Arena Kit.Snd only has Clank / Hiss / Whoosh: an unknown key (e.g. "Thud") gives a bad SoundId. Low thud = Clank at pitch ~0.55. Removing a texture entry (F.Tex.Sunburst) crashes every emitter still pointing at it: grep for the key first.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude vfx-artist] Two crossing slash bars read as an X / star burst; a crit double slash must be parallel (offset along the bar's up axis).

Freeze-at-frame is async: capture again once the run reaches the frame. Another agent's temp ScreenGui (ShowDevelopmentGui) can cover Edit captures: set it false just for the capture, then restore.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude map-builder] Patching a big Lua build script from a shell heredoc breaks on quotes and brackets: write the new Lua into scratchpad files and apply them with a small python replace script that checks each old string matches exactly once. Keep your own scratchpad subfolder when several agents share a session.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude map-builder] Recolor a built map without a rebuild by mapping `Color3:ToHex():lower()` old hex -> new colour over descendants. Luau `(a or b and 0 or 0)` silently turns a boolean into 0 in arithmetic: write explicit if-expressions. On a plaque, the SurfaceGui's left edge sits on the part's +X side, so check which way the plaque faces before placing text; the first capture right after a rebuild can miss SurfaceGui text, capture again.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude map-builder] Ambient motion: one client Heartbeat loop reading attributes (SpinDegPerSec, Bob, Sway, Orbit, SlideVec) beats a script per prop; Models via PivotTo, single parts batched in workspace:BulkMoveTo, nested movers composed as parent.cur * parent.base:ToObjectSpace(childPose) and sorted parents-first, distance cull + half rate mid range. Dry-run a client loop in Edit by loadstring + setfenv with a fake RunService that captures the Heartbeat callback, step it, then BulkMoveTo every part back to its snapshot.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude ui-designer] Roblox renders text at most 100 px tall (MaxTextSize caps at 100, TextBounds stays 100 even under a UIScale 2), so a big title "slam" by UIScale barely grows on large screens: animate position (drop-in) + squash instead. A UIStroke on fading text must fade with it (TextTransparency alone leaves a dark ghost outline).
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude ui-designer] Glass helpers that add a Sheen child frame break UIListLayout containers (the sheen becomes a list item, pushing text down and stretching AutomaticSize width): pass sheen=false inside list layouts. Per-mode layout tables (full/short/narrow) need every key in every mode; a missing TextSize key silently left a "--" label.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude ui-designer] Pet ViewportFrames: PetBuilder.Build without a body id and parts gives a blank white shell, so the HUD row must carry Build = {Species, Parts, Body}; camera distance ~2.7x model radius frames a part-laden pet. In Studio Edit the require cache keeps a stale Config (new child modules like Movesets missing): test against a fresh clone of the module, and freeze tweens at fixed poses for screenshots since timed Edit captures are unreliable.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude vfx-artist] F.HitStop / a hit-stop only freezes particles and timers, not scripted poses: write the held squash or pose into the move timeline for the hit-stop frames, or the stamp reads light.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude vfx-artist] A giant object erupting UNDER the target swallows it; put it just behind the target (front face kissing the target's back) so the target stays silhouetted in front. Navy shadows vanish on dark arena floors: use black plus a thin hue Neon rim.

Shelf-3 view of R2-R11 went from 10-62% to 48-90% seen.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude map-builder] Smooth curved blades from parts: Catmull-Rom through 4-5 control points, then box segments plus a disc (cylinder) at every joint = a capsule chain with no gaps or kinks. Neon rings/arcs need 22-36 segments; 6 reads as a hexagon from any distance.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude map-builder] Locked portals: dim the veil to ~60% toward navy, transparency 0.6, particles LightEmission 0.1-0.3 and store the open values as Unlocked<Prop> attributes (UnlockedLightEmission needs a line in the client's PROP_FOR map). `sed s/x/y/` without g on lines with non-ASCII (the middle dot) changed the wrong comment lines: use python with count checks. Several Studio screen_captures sent in parallel can return the same frame: send them one by one.

- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude ui-designer] Particles never render in a ViewportFrame; show auras in 3D card windows as a few Neon part stand-ins (orbit shards, sparkles, ring) around the bounding box
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude ui-designer] A white glint band on a cream title face is invisible on a still frame; give the glint TextLabel a white UIStroke (Contextual) so the UIGradient band lights the dark outline too
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude ui-designer] Phone portrait pickers: stack each round as a ROW (wide own card + mini rival card) instead of shrinking columns; one-line TextScaled labels with TextWrapped=false shrink instead of wrapping "1.2K" or truncating names
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude ui-designer] Judge phone shots at real scale (root UIScale = device scale, anchor top then bottom, stitch); shrinking the root to fit the Studio window pushes TextScaled text under its floor and fakes truncation

Another agent may hold port 8765: serve on another port and kill only your own

Grouping per parent model + 32-stud cell, <=60 parts per union, cut HubV2 12.8k -> 7.4k parts with no visible change; overlapping ball clumps even got cheaper (~160 tris/ball vs 380+).

Workspace StreamingTargetRadius/MinRadius/IntegrityMode are NOT readable from MCP execute_luau or inspect_instance: check them by hand in Properties.

Bash heredocs break on apostrophes inside $(...) prompts: write prompt files with Python and pass them via P=$(cat file).

Module: build-a-pet/scripts/ReplicatedStorage/FX/PortalVortex.lua.

Force the fan normals toward -Y after recalc. A flat n-gon plate on a curved capsule buries its middle: stand the plate proud (or subdivide) instead of curving only the outline verts.

- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude scripter] Path checks sampled every 2 studs still let a leg clip a box corner between samples (2 of 2352 audit samples in a river box): add a box-only test at each half step (no ray). Play-test cameras can be left Scriptable by other UI (camera at the lobby): set CameraType Custom from the Client DM before measuring distance-LOD code.
- 2026-11-02 (build-a-pet r18) A union's CFrame is its first part's; use ExtentsCFrame/ExtentsSize for box tests.
- 2026-11-02 Missing light spill: drop a bright test PointLight first. If it doesn't show either, it's rendering (Lighting.Technology / graphics quality), not your geometry or merge.
- 2026-11-02 Save camera CFrame, FOV and view size next to every before/after shot so the after shot can match.
- 2026-11-02 glTF object and mesh named differently → Studio loads Model "<object>" > MeshPart "<mesh>"; key parts by the parent Model's name in loaders.
- 2026-11-02 Two agents sharing Studio: the newest capture file may be the other agent's. Set the camera and capture in the same call and copy shots by exact name.
- 2026-11-02 Frame shots from the bounding box (dist = halfHeight / tan(FOV/2)), not a fixed distance; tall models get cut off.
- 2026-11-02 Built piece misses its concept on colour only → change the colour data and reinstall; no Blender rerun or re-upload.
- 2026-11-02 Lead check vs concept: measure the hero feature's share of the frame (portal opening ~65% of width in concept vs 35% built). Agents self-score high when silhouettes match but proportions don't.
- (map-builder r18, 2026-11-02) screen_capture with camera_position in Play moves the camera only for the shot: distance-culled client FX (PortalVortex GuiCullDistance 250) stay off, so the vortex looks black. Set workspace.CurrentCamera (Scriptable) on the Client, task.wait(1.5), then capture with no camera args.
- (map-builder r18) HubAmbient spins a BasePart about its CFrame centre and ignores PivotOffset: when cloning lab spinners to the hub, wrap pivot-offset spinners in a Model (WorldPivot = part:GetPivot()) and move SpinDegPerSec/SpinAxis/tags onto it; turn SpinAxis by placedRot * labRot:Inverse().
- (map-builder r18) Requiring a server Service module from execute_luau gives a fresh copy with no Init (nil services); drive dev actions through the real remote instead (Client: ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.DevCommand:FireServer("setrank","4")).
- (modeler r17 v4, 2026-11-02) Blender build scripts: never share one dict (e.g. a SPIN/pivot entry) between several parts when a later pass warps per part. The pass moves the pivot once per part, so the shared value drifts (R12 Moons pivot 8.79 vs 8.0). Give each part its own dict.
- (modeler r17 v4) Reinstall in place: build the new Model fully, then Destroy the old one just before `Parent = lab`. A failed LoadAsset then leaves the old frame standing instead of a hole that the map builder's clones point at.
- (modeler r17 v4) "Brighter vortex" without touching the module: boost the anchor colours in HSV (main s*1.25, v=1; dark v 0.32 with s+0.1; core = white lerped 10% toward main) and add VortexCore/VortexDepth attributes that the client reads. The Studio viewport can stall (only the view cube renders) after long sessions, so check every capture for a blank frame before saving it.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude modeler r18 portals v5] Faceting on a warped (squashed-oval) ring sweep comes from equal-angle sampling: the tight top/bottom curves get too few rings. Sample the sweep at equal arc length along the warped centre line (2000-sample table, cached), and 96 around then looks smooth at player-eye distance for the same tris.

Carry it as a `gloss` key from the Blender layout.json to the installer (`r=`). Drops hung on the inside of an opening's rim read as teeth: keep drips on the band front.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude modeler] execute_luau shares one plugin VM: define a big installer once as `_G.Fn` in the first call, then send only the data in later calls (saves ~half the paste), and nil it at the end.

Use a small box (~70x14x50) that follows the camera 30 studs ahead (flattened LookVector, clamped to the zone); particles are world space so moving it never drags them. At that range Size needs 1.5-2.4 studs and Brightness ~2.2 to show on a bright sky.

Evenly spaced rim streak emitters draw a star web; give each slot a random angle that re-rolls after its streaks fade.

Fake ground light = a thin part with a Top-face SurfaceGui (LightInfluence 0, Brightness ~1.5) holding a white-RGB/alpha-shape ImageLabel tinted by ImageColor3; shows in any Technology and costs nothing measurable at 200+ pools.
- 2026-11-02 [build-a-pet, Claude vfx-artist r18 light] Floor raycasts for decals: RespectCanCollide=false plus a loop that skips hits with Transparency >= 0.9 (and your own pool parts), or the ray lands on a hidden slab under the real floor. On a Top-face SurfaceGui the image's top edge points to the part's local -X and its width runs along Z: CFrame.fromMatrix(pos, -dirTop, up), Size (depth, thick, width).

MeshPart.Color then only shows through transparent texels, so the texture is the colour.

Parts that surround a glow-spill emitter (tunnel, back cap) bake near-white: leave them untextured.

Bake rim as its own pass, keep key min near 0.34 and AO about 2x, and re-check at player distance (40 studs) before calling it done.
- 2026-11-02 (modeler): if Studio screen_capture comes back as a blank grey viewport with only the ViewCube, check whether the Studio window is minimised (user32 IsIconic). Restore it with ShowWindow(h, 4) (no focus steal), shoot, then ShowWindow(h, 7) to minimise it again.
- 2026-11-02 (modeler): cached bake passes (npz) must be re-baked when a lip or swirl colour changes. Blender GLB export can throw a transient OSError Errno 22, so re-run only the failed ranks from the cache.
- 2026-11-02 (scripter r21): To shelve a feature reversibly, move its models to one ServerStorage backup folder (Note attr + OriginalParent attr per child) and gate every server/client script on one Config flag checked as `== true`, keeping the remote registered so it answers Code "disabled" instead of hanging. To check Studio source == disk, compare an LF-normalised byte length + a matching Python/Luau rolling checksum, because CRLF files make raw lengths differ.

Check `find build-a-pet -name "*.png" -size <bytes>c` right after each save; a second run in the same session came back fresh, and the next three runs back to back were all fresh. For a mash-up/collided brief, naming each building as "Scrap base + ONE guest rank chunk with hex" plus a fixed clock-position layout gave a hero picture that matched the zone map on the first fresh try.

- 2026-11-02 (modeler r21): raycast-check layout points against the Path before placing lamps (two lamps landed on the cobble path); a string lamp needs a ~14-stud span to cross the path, with one collider per post, never one in the middle.
