- 2026-11-02 (modeler r21): puffy canopy = 3-5 big lobes, each a flattened core plus rows of thick shingle leaves pointing down-slope (Y = down-slope, Z = normal, lifted ~9 deg, u10 v5); round pillows with Z = normal read as a cracked turtle shell.
- 2026-11-02 (modeler r21): baked key light + a low sunset sun bleaches sun-facing textured meshes to cream (trunk roots, bush sides); bake a softer key on the side that faces the place's sun, or lower the baked top light, before calling a kit piece done.
- 2026-11-02 (reviewer r21): kit Colliders are CanQuery=false, so a walk test's stuck-ray reports "no hit"; log the nearest kit collider by distance from a pre-collected list instead (found MossRocks_2's 2.4-stud box blocking the outer lap; Humanoid steps ~2 studs).
- 2026-11-02 (reviewer r21): sum placed tris per plot, not per asset: r21 Plot 1 = ~455k (23 bushes x 4.5k = 105k, 7 big trees x 14.9k = 104k); crowd foliage belongs on the low/medium tier even when the game default is high.
- 2026-11-02 (reviewer r21): a footprint raycast that reports "sunk 10 studs" under a prop means it overlaps another structure (LampBrass_2 inside the Arrival arch pillar); run it after every kit placement.
- 2026-11-02 (art-director r22): gpt-image.ps1 called through `powershell -File ... -Prompt $p` breaks on double quotes and newlines in the prompt (the rest spills into -Minutes: "Cannot convert value ... to System.Int32"); replace `"` with `'` and newlines with spaces before passing a prompt file.
- 2026-11-02 (art-director r22): ChatGPT room/scene pictures blow the hero prop up (egg drawn ~3x player height) and plans swap specific props for placeholders (crates, cream egg, exit as a floor pad); always ship an exact numbers plan (PIL script, b2-plan-exact.png) next to the painted one and tell the builder to place the real existing assets at their real size.
- 2026-11-02 (modeler r21c): baked light vs a low Brightness-2.4 sun: a baked key above 1.0 (kmax 1.04, gain 1.04) plus an orange rim add (rim 0.85, rimlift 0.45) bleached the sun side of orange leaves and brass to cream, while plain parts in the same colours stayed fine. Sun-safe mix for this place: kmin 0.52, kmax 0.8, rim 0.22, rimlift 0.08, gain 0.98, brass C8902E/E8B650/8E5C20.
- 2026-11-02 (modeler r21c): "cracked plates" canopy came from leaf-gap AO (near black) plus painted tuck/underside darks along jagged leaf intersections. Fix: teardrop leaves (taper the down-slope tip, no curl, rz 0.34r, lift ~22 deg), a light rim on each leaf instead of dark edges, and cap the AO occlusion (aocap 0.5 in kitlib bake_atlas) for foliage. A tip curl makes the hidden tips poke through the row above as nubs.
- 2026-11-02 (modeler r21c): a reviewer's "sunk 2.4 studs" came from a raycast hitting a bench seat under the tree, not the ground: check what the ray hit before raising a prop. Moving it 2.8 studs off the bench fixed it.
- 2026-11-02 (scripter r22): teleports with StreamingEnabled must move the player's anchored pet models on the SERVER (PetService.SnapToOwner right after PivotTo, also added to TravelService): pets that only the client moves stay at the old place on the server, stream out for the owner, and arrive late or not at all. Real Play walk city -> room -> city: pets 3.5-4.3 studs from the player on server and client both ways.
- 2026-11-02 (scripter r22): walk-through exit in a wall: put the anchor plane INSIDE the doorway (r 36.5 for a wall face at r 36) with a 5-stud recess behind it; with the plane flush on the wall the HumanoidRootPart (half depth 1) can never reach local Z >= 0 and the crossing never fires. Add an ExitGrace (2 s, code "grace", silent on the client) so a fresh arrival can't bounce straight back out.
- 2026-11-02 (scripter r22): pushing disk scripts into Studio without a plugin: `python -m http.server 8765 --bind 127.0.0.1` in the sync folder, then execute_luau toggles HttpService.HttpEnabled on, GetAsync each file into .Source (gsub CRLF to LF), restores the flag, and compares Source == file; loadstring works in execute_luau, so builder scripts kept on disk (world/rank-rooms/build_scrap_blockout.lua) run straight from the same server. Workspace.StreamingTargetRadius/MinRadius can't be read by script (error), don't query them.
- 2026-11-02 (scripter wild pets): execute_luau in the Server datamodel runs in its own VM: require(service) gives a FRESH module copy, not the live one (live tables are empty). Drive live Play tests through the client's real remotes (plus the DevCommand remote for setup: "pet" grants, "equipgoal" equips a team) and read results from replicated attributes.
- 2026-11-02 (scripter wild pets): in this place something holds the Play client camera at a fixed Scriptable CFrame and screen_capture's camera args are ignored in Play; for shots bind RunService:BindToRenderStep("Shot", Camera.Value + 50, set CFrame) and unbind after. Config.Parts has no "Headgear" slot: the Scrap Headgear chest rolls Halo / Horns / Ears, so read the slot from Config.Parts[id].Slot.
- 2026-11-02 (scripter wild pets): RemoteGuard has no "table" arg type (by design); a settings-like remote that edits a structured record is cleaner as one call per change (group, key, on, confirm?) with the pure rules in a shared module the client panel can also require (Shared/AutoDeleteFilter) than as a whole-table upload.
- 2026-11-02 (art-director) gpt-image.ps1 takes one -Ref file: put several references (city hero + building kit + hero building) into one montage PNG with PIL and send that. ChatGPT copied both the city look and the kit detail from it.
- 2026-11-02 (art-director) Painted bird's-eye cities drift on counts and repeats: 5 Matrix orbs instead of 4, and 8 identical plots. Always ship a PIL exact plan (plan_exact.py, 2 px per stud, angles clockwise from north) next to the painting, and list the drift in NOTES.md as builder fixes.
- 2026-11-02 (vfx, r22 wind grass): an Open Cloud Decal upload returns a DECAL id; MeshPart.TextureID needs the IMAGE id -> InsertService:LoadAsset(decalId):FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Decal",true).Texture (PreloadAsync may say Failure and it still renders).
- 2026-11-02 (vfx): require() from execute_luau / the command bar gets its own module instance, so a controller's Stats() reads zeros there; expose live stats as attributes or measure the parts. Cloning a live wild pet for a test needs every BasePart anchored, then place it by GetBoundingBox (unanchored clones fall apart).
- 2026-11-02 (vfx, r22): 1.4-2.8 stud grass tufts hide ~2.2 stud wild pets from a mid camera; mob zones need short tuft kinds only (or tufts <= 1 stud).
- 2026-11-02 (animator, r22 pet motion): pet follow jerk came from the goal, not the spring: the formation slot turned with the owner's raw facing (sharp turn = goal jumps studs), the velocity lead and wobble weights used raw Humanoid speed (instant start/stop = pop), and |sin| hops kink at every step. Smooth the formation (owner yaw, lead, speed) before the spring; use the closed-form critically damped spring (frame-rate independent); waddle bounce = B*(0.5+0.5cos2t), no |sin|.
- 2026-11-02 (animator): detect owner teleports as |(pos-lastPos) - vel*dt| > 8 studs; that catches room/plot teleports without a remote, and walking or jumping never trips it. Snap pets cleanly once per teleport id.
- 2026-11-02 (animator): another agent's Play session blocks the Edit datamodel ("Edit datamodel is not available in Play mode"); write the module files first and push them when Studio is back in Edit. Don't stop someone else's test.
- 2026-11-02 (scripter, r22b door portal): one door used both ways = build the frame once with a doorUnit(F, anchorFlip, vestibuleSide) function; the room copy goes at InsideDoor * Angles(0, pi, 0) (back face to the room) with its anchor flipped back so LookVector points at the walker, and its vestibule goes into a wall recess. Derive both landings from the door CFrames in config (arrival = inside * (0,0,-ArriveIn) looking at the egg; exit = door * (0,0,-ArriveOut) facing along the door's LookVector) so nothing is hand-placed twice.
- 2026-11-02 (scripter): keep art from moving a trigger: the server only accepts an anchor within 2 studs of its config CFrame (else no_portal), and a test moves the anchor 6 studs and expects nil. Front/back face tests must compare flat (XZ) distances; a sign above a kick plate fails a 3D distance check.
- 2026-11-02 (scripter): a stray `python -m http.server` from an earlier context can still hold the port, and a new server on that port fails silently. Read the 404s as "wrong folder served", pick another port, and stop every server you started before you report.
- 2026-11-03 (animator): a Play test can get a fixed Scriptable camera from a lab script's BindToRenderStep (MapAssetsLab.GroundV2.LabClient "GroundV2Cam", Last+10). Camera-distance LODs then think every pet is far (600 studs) and motion looks dead. Check cam position before judging motion; UnbindFromRenderStep("GroundV2Cam") for the session. A camera hold has to live in a LocalScript (PlayerGui), because execute_luau threads die with the call.
- 2026-11-02 (roblox-scripter, wild pets r22): PetMotion's gait turns off past LOD.Off (150 studs) from the camera, and the Studio play camera sits ~600 studs away (held by a script), so a live 'roll = 0' read is the LOD, not a bug. Check the wobble with an offline m:Pose loop (camDist 25 gave roll 6.9 deg, lag 0.9 studs at 5 studs/s).
- 2026-11-03 (modeler, r22 water): Blender's bundled Python has no scipy. A blur made with np.roll leaves a checker pattern in baked AO; use a cumsum box blur, 3 passes, for soft contact shadows. Piece.add() frees the bmesh, so copy the vertex coords first if a second side reuses them, or you get "BMVert has been removed".
- 2026-11-03 (modeler, r22 water): a water surface mesh imported from a GLB comes in without alpha. Add a SurfaceAppearance (uploaded ColorMap with alpha, AlphaMode Transparency) for the clear shore fade, and scroll ripples with Textures on the same MeshPart (OffsetStudsU/V from one client script, stop when far). Clip the grid to the island with a Studio raycast first, or it hangs off the rim.
- 2026-11-03 (modeler, bushes): a bush made of leaf cards stays faceted at any card count. One ray-marched smooth dome (a soft union of 3 lobes minus fibonacci leaf cushions, colour from the crease depth) reads as a stylized bush at about 2k tris.
- 2026-11-03 (ui-designer, r22 notify): require() inside execute_luau returns a fresh module copy, so a UI module that finds-or-makes its ScreenGui by name ends up fighting the game's own copy (both destroy and rebuild "Notify"). Rename the test copy's gui ("NotifyTest") and keep it in _G. Test notices also need duration 12+ s, because screen_capture shows the screen ~2 s late.
- 2026-11-03 (ui-designer, r22 notify): to parse-check a module before setting .Source, wrap it as "return function(...)\n"..src.."\nend" in a temp ModuleScript and require it. That compiles it without running top-level yields, which time out. UIStroke, rays and glows inside a CanvasGroup are clipped to its rect, so anything that breaks out goes on the parent host.
- 2026-11-03 (ui-designer, r22 notify): a TextScaled + TextWrapped sub-line with a raised phone MinTextSize (14 px real floor) wraps instead of shrinking, and the second line gets clipped in a one-line label. Set TextWrapped false + TextTruncate AtEnd on one-line labels, and keep the copy short.
- 2026-11-03 (scripter, auto-delete on eggs + crates): when a full inventory converts plain rolls to a payout but keeps rares, charge the convert. If a full inventory refused plain rolls for free, it would become a free reroll (players would only ever pay for rares). If nothing can be deleted, refuse BEFORE the roll; offline hatching stops instead of converting.
- 2026-11-03 (scripter): when one action fires two events and the old tests check `fired[#fired]`, fire the new shared event (ItemAutoDeleted) BEFORE the old one (WildPetAutoDeleted). Then the old suite (96 checks) passes untouched.
- 2026-11-03 (scripter): to feed once per batch, the inner Open returns early on `opts.Batch` before its own feed, and the batch caller (OpenChest) feeds the aggregate after caching the nonce. A repeated nonce returns from the cache above that point, so it can never pay or feed twice.
- 2026-11-03 (ui-designer, r22 auto-delete panel): `require` from execute_luau (plugin VM) gives a SEPARATE copy of a ModuleScript, so its state (IsOpen, chips) reads empty while the real client copy is open. For QA, expose a Studio-only BindableFunction (e.g. LocalPlayer.AutoDeleteFilterQA) from the live module and Invoke it.
- 2026-11-03 (ui-designer): ProximityPrompt:InputHoldBegin()/InputHoldEnd() from the client fires Triggered even when the prompt is not on screen, so an in-world prompt hook can be tested on a client-only stand-in Part (named like the real station) when the real eggs/crates are not in Workspace.
- 2026-11-03 (ui-designer): a grid's inner width from a "margins" sum under-counted padding and the scrollbar, so it computed 4 columns but UIGridLayout fit 3 (empty right side). Use w - 72 for a Glass sleek panel with a scrolling list, or read list.AbsoluteCanvasSize/AbsoluteWindowSize after layout.
- (2026-11-03, scripter) Table player stubs need `Parent = Players` or any `if not player.Parent then "left"` loop (EggService.RunOffline) stops on the first step and hides the real result.
- 2026-11-03 modeler: Roblox's GLB import turns each mesh 180 deg about Y (around its own bbox centre) compared with the Blender (X, -Y, Z) -> Roblox (x, y, -z) mapping; set each MeshPart CFrame = CFrame.new(centre) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.pi, 0), or the placed pieces come out mirrored. A flat mesh (bbox height 0, MeshSize.Y = 0) is never drawn: add a hidden keel sliver ~0.3 below an edge vertex.
- 2026-11-03 modeler: Terrain water at 4-stud voxels reads as dark facets next to a baked-mesh pond (side-by-side test on Plot 1); for a small stylized pond/river, a mesh + SurfaceAppearance + two scrolling Textures wins. EditableMesh (AssetService:CreateEditableMeshAsync(Content.fromUri(MeshId)), #GetFaces()) counts a plot's tris in Edit mode.
- 2026-11-03 modeler: cutting a ray-marched dome bush from 42x16 to 32x12 rings (2028 -> 1462 tris) brought back a faceted, notched silhouette; keep >= 40x16 rings on smooth foliage and save tris elsewhere.
- 2026-11-03 (reviewer): before accepting "these test failures are stale", read what the failing check asserts. EggOpenTests' "EggStation ScrapEgg anchor exists in Workspace" was dismissed as stale, but Workspace really had no EggStation/CrateStation/EggStand/ChestSpot tag at all, so Stations.Near refuses every hatch and crate open on a live server (Studio-only AllowMissingInStudio hid it). One check: `#CollectionService:GetTagged("EggStation")` filtered to `:IsDescendantOf(workspace)`.
- 2026-11-03 (reviewer): the DevCommand "pet" grants a pet without equipping it, so nothing follows and the lunge/follow can't be tested; use "followdummies" (oversized legacy models, fine for numbers, not for looks) or the equip remote.
- 2026-11-03 (reviewer): phone shots taken with a GUI's DebugViewport attribute show a dark centred rectangle (the emulated viewport) and leave the real HUD unscaled, so they can't judge HUD overlap; treat them as layout checks only and confirm on a real phone or Studio device emulation.
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, arena spec): before writing an Auto loop, check every fight path goes through the same reward limiter (bot fights skipped RewardLimiter).
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, arena/market spec): put service-building doors in the same row list as rank-room doors so they get the same door checks; only 'exit while queued' needs an exception.
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, market spec): for cross-server listings the DataStore record is the truth and MemoryStore only an index; buys use a pending debit plus a single-use id so a crash recovers either way.
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, animator): every V5 pet is Float/Hover (PetBuilder sets Hover on all), so a gait "Flyer" profile meant for winged pets hit every Scrap core and made the waddle too subtle; key gait profiles on real wings (`Winged`), not on Float, and give hover cores their own Hover profile (±5° roll, 3° yaw, 0.1 bob).
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, animator): two tilt systems on one pet fight on a brake (FloatMotion's underdamped accel tilt pitched against PetMotion's lean = 10° overshoot + second bounce); let one module own lean/brake/bank (FloatParams TiltPerAccel = 0) and stop a waddle by blending out fast (Out 9) while easing the phase to the nearest upright, which settles in about 0.25 s with no second sway.
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, animator): frame strips during Play: bind a camera at RenderPriority.Camera+50 (it survives across execute_luau calls), start the move with Humanoid:MoveTo, then grab the Studio window with PrintWindow (flag 3) from PowerShell; screen_capture lags about 2 s and ignores its camera args in Play.
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, scripter r22 B1): Stations.Near skips its range check in Studio when nothing is tagged, so a rank-room egg copy and chest stands "worked" in Play solo but would refuse on a live server; wire copied stations with a server-side idempotent setup (Lib/RankRoomStations, run at Start) and assert the tags exist in a test, never trust Studio Play for range checks.
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, scripter tests): stale tests came from hard-coded content (rarest overlay name, a Secret body no longer in the pool, 4 outcomes, 8 opens without perRoll); read expected values from Config (rarest = largest OneIn, live Secret = Odds.SecretTiers with Odds.Species > 0, #outcomes) and clear the EggService hatch lock (_ClearLock) between fixture phases.
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, scripter): a service Start() that connects only Players.PlayerAdded misses the Studio Play player who joined before Start; also loop Players:GetPlayers(). Studio shots without stealing focus: PrintWindow(h, dc, 2) on the RobloxStudioBeta main window from PowerShell.
- 2026-11-03 (ui-designer, r22 room labels): world name labels sized in studs (UDim2 scale on a BillboardGui) blow up at close range in small rooms and cover ScreenGui prompts; size them in offset (height from ViewportSize.Y, width from TextService:GetTextSize), but then far labels in a ring all land on one horizon line and stack: add a per-frame nearest-first screen declutter (WorldToViewportPoint + rect test, ~6 labels = trivial) and reserve the egg pill ScreenGui rects first (viewport Y = AbsolutePosition.Y - screenGui.AbsolutePosition.Y, the top-bar inset).
- 2026-11-03 (ui-designer): "is the egg prompt showing?" from another controller = ProximityPromptService.PromptShown/PromptHidden filtered by prompt.Name ("VolcanicHatchPrompt"; it lives on Workspace.Terrain.VolcanicHatchPromptAnchor, not in the egg). Studio Play camera viewport was 1678x611, so plateH's 24 px floor already showed the phone size on PC.
- 2026-11-03 (ui-designer): Edit-mode script sync from disk: the local server on 127.0.0.1:8765 serves scripts/, but HttpService.HttpEnabled is off in this place; flip it on just for the GetAsync and restore it (as _tools/studio_sync.lua does), loadstring-check, then set .Source.
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, art-director): for many ChatGPT pictures, a queue script that runs gpt-image.ps1 one prompt file at a time, with a Monitor on its output grepping for ".png|SKIPPED|NO_IMAGE", gives 8 pictures in about 12 min with no stale repeats (md5 check each result).
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, art-director): a numbered 4x2 white-background sheet "shared base + ONE crashed theme + its own door shape" gives 8 distinct buildings on the first try, but the second sheet drifted the shared base (arched windows, bigger); for sheet 2 say "the exact same base as sheet 1 (porthole windows, same size)" and attach sheet 1 as the reference.
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, art-director): ChatGPT paints portal doors for service buildings about 3 players wide and leans gold-heavy; state the door size in players ("door 2 players wide") and "brass only on trims" in the prompt, and set exact studs in the notes (ranks 8x12, services 12x16).
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, modeler r22b): to swap a world mesh without moving it, keep the loose parts that touch the bbox min/max when thinning in Blender (scatter meshes: tufts, reeds); the GLB comes back with the same Size, so the old CFrame/TextureID/attributes copy straight over (Tufts_PC 16.4k -> 7.3k, no re-placing).
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, modeler r22b): Cycles-baked atlases make thin water (fall sheets, white streak ribbons) read grey; in Studio drop the TextureID on water sheets and use a plain SmoothPlastic colour (58C6F2, T 0.08) and let the scrolling Beams carry the streaks.
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, modeler r22b): after swapping a mountain mesh, ray-dropping the old pines onto it can leave a pine sticking out of a steep slope in silhouette; check the hero angle and move bad ones to the backup instead of trusting the raycast.
- 2026-11-03 (Build-A-Pet, modeler r22b): Plot tri audit = AssetService:CreateEditableMeshAsync(Content.fromUri(MeshId)) #GetFaces per unique MeshId over every MeshPart (Parts/Unions not counted); cheap budget cuts with no visible loss: near-duplicate scatter props (two bushes 1 stud apart) and props hidden behind mountains/houses at the hero angle.
- 2026-11-03 (reviewer, Plot 1 r22b): a big, high-contrast caustic texture makes stylized water read as a swimming pool. Use a deep-to-shallow colour fade plus a soft foam edge, and keep ripples low-contrast and large.
- 2026-11-03 (reviewer): a waterfall must visibly feed the water below it (a stream or a splash pool in view), or it reads as a decal on a wall.
- 2026-11-03 (lead): when the builder's own scores (8s) and a cold reviewer pass (5) disagree, believe the reviewer and send a focused next round on the owner's named ask (here, the water) before polishing anything else.
